### [1] [Equilibrium Gibbs Bifurcations of Bardeen-AdS Black Holes at Fixed Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00099)
*J. -K. Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: In the direct horizon convention, the Bardeen-AdS black hole exhibits a Gibbs free energy landscape that evolves from RN-AdS-like swallow-tail behavior to a single-branch structure as the magnetic charge (regularization scale) $g$ increases. Three critical boundaries $g_*(P)$, $g_c(P)$, and $g_s(P)$ demarcate topological and thermodynamic transitions, governed by the dimensionless parameter $8\pi P g^2$. Stable small/large black hole coexistence persists past the first topology change, but vanishes in the c-shaped regime.
\n\n
在直接视界约定下，随着磁荷（正则化尺度）$g$ 增大，Bardeen-AdS 黑洞的 Gibbs 自由能曲线从类似 Reissner-Nordström-AdS（RN-AdS）的 swallow-tail 行为演化为单支结构。三个临界边界 $g_*(P)$、$g_c(P)$ 和 $g_s(P)$ 划定了拓扑与热力学相变，由无量纲参数 $8\pi P g^2$ 控制。稳定的小/大黑洞共存可在首次拓扑改变后依然存在，但在 c 形区域中消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to clarify the thermodynamic phase structure of the four-dimensional Bardeen-AdS black hole—a regular black hole model with nonlinear electrodynamics—within the extended phase space framework where pressure $P = -\Lambda/8\pi$ is treated as a thermodynamic variable. Unlike singular black holes like RN-AdS, regular black holes introduce a new scale $g$ (magnetic charge/regularization parameter), whose interplay with $P$ leads to novel Gibbs curve deformations not fully understood. The motivation is to map out how increasing $g$ modifies the Gibbs free energy landscape, especially regarding topology changes, stability, and phase coexistence.
\n\n
本文旨在澄清四维 Bardeen-AdS 黑洞（一种具有非线性电动力学的正则黑洞模型）在扩展相空间框架下的热力学相结构，其中压力 $P = -\Lambda/8\pi$ 被视为热力学变量。与 RN-AdS 等奇异黑洞不同，正则黑洞引入了一个新尺度 $g$（磁荷/正则化参数），其与 $P$ 的相互作用导致 Gibbs 曲线出现尚未被充分理解的新形变。研究动机在于刻画 $g$ 增大如何改变 Gibbs 自由能景观，特别是关于拓扑变化、稳定性及相共存的问题。

Method: The authors employ the direct horizon convention (identifying the event horizon radius $r_h$ as the independent thermodynamic variable) to compute the on-shell Gibbs free energy $G(T,P,g)$. They analyze the curve $G(T)$ at fixed $P$ and varying $g$, classifying its shape via turning points and self-intersections. Local thermodynamic stability is enforced by filtering branches with positive heat capacity $C_P > 0$. The equilibrium Gibbs envelope is constructed by taking the lower convex hull over stable branches. Critical boundaries are identified through reduced-variable analysis using the dimensionless combination $\xi = 8\pi P g^2$, which collapses the transition lines onto universal curves.
\n\n
作者采用直接视界约定（将事件视界半径 $r_h$ 视为独立热力学变量）计算壳层上的 Gibbs 自由能 $G(T,P,g)$。他们在固定 $P$、变化 $g$ 下分析 $G(T)$ 曲线，通过拐点和自交点对曲线形状进行分类。通过筛选满足正热容 $C_P > 0$ 的分支来实施局域热力学稳定性。平衡 Gibbs 包络通过对稳定分支取下凸包构造。临界边界通过约化变量分析识别，使用无量纲组合 $\xi = 8\pi P g^2$，使各相变线坍缩到普适曲线上。

Result: Three distinct boundaries in the $(P,g)$ plane are found: (1) $g_*(P)$, where the Gibbs curve loses its RN-AdS-like swallow-tail topology; (2) $g_c(P)$, marking the onset of a c-shaped $G(T)$ segment; and (3) $g_s(P)$, beyond which only a single monotonic branch remains. These satisfy $g \propto P^{-1/2}$ due to scaling by $\xi = 8\pi P g^2$. Analytically, $g_s = 1/\sqrt{8\pi P}$. Stable small/large black hole coexistence (via Maxwell construction) persists between $g_*(P)$ and $g_c(P)$, but the c-shaped regime ($g_c < g < g_s$) lacks stable crossings, implying no phase coexistence there.
\n\n
在 $(P,g)$ 平面中发现三条不同的边界：(1) $g_*(P)$，Gibbs 曲线在此失去类 RN-AdS 的 swallow-tail 拓扑；(2) $g_c(P)$，标志 c 形 $G(T)$ 段的开始；(3) $g_s(P)$，超过此值仅剩单一单调分支。由于由 $\xi = 8\pi P g^2$ 标度，这些边界满足 $g \propto P^{-1/2}$。解析地，$g_s = 1/\sqrt{8\pi P}$。稳定的小/大黑洞共存（通过 Maxwell 构造）存在于 $g_*(P)$ 与 $g_c(P)$ 之间，但 c 形区域（$g_c < g < g_s$）无稳定交叉，意味着该区域无相共存。

Conclusion: The thermodynamics of the Bardeen-AdS black hole under the direct horizon convention exhibits a rich Gibbs bifurcation structure controlled by the dimensionless parameter $8\pi P g^2$. The transition from multivalued to single-valued Gibbs curves occurs through well-defined topological and stability thresholds. Crucially, phase coexistence survives the initial loss of swallow-tail topology, challenging naive expectations. This work provides a complete classification of equilibrium phases and establishes that regular black holes can display more complex thermodynamic behavior than their singular counterparts, with implications for holographic applications and quantum gravity.
\n\n
在直接视界约定下，Bardeen-AdS 黑洞的热力学展现出由无量纲参数 $8\pi P g^2$ 控制的丰富 Gibbs 分岔结构。从多值到单值 Gibbs 曲线的转变通过明确定义的拓扑与稳定性阈值发生。关键的是，相共存在 swallow-tail 拓扑首次消失后仍可存在，挑战了直观预期。本工作完整分类了平衡相，并表明正则黑洞可展现出比奇异黑洞更复杂的热力学行为，对全息应用和量子引力具有启示意义。

Abstract: In the direct horizon convention, the fixed-pressure Gibbs curve of the four-dimensional Bardeen-AdS black hole passes through an intermediate sequence between the Reissner-Nordstrom-AdS swallow-tail class and the single-branch regime as the regularization scale is increased. The on-shell curve is classified by its turning points and self-intersections, followed by local heat-capacity filtering and construction of the lower Gibbs envelope over stable branches. The deformation is resolved into three boundaries: g_*(P), where the RN-AdS-like topology is lost; g_c(P), where the c-shaped sector begins; and g_s(P), where the positive-temperature multibranch structure terminates. A reduced-variable analysis shows that these boundaries are controlled by the dimensionless combination 8 pi P g^2, accounting for their inverse-square-root pressure dependence and giving an analytic value for the final single-branch boundary. The equilibrium construction further shows that stable small/large coexistence can survive the first topology change, whereas the representative c-shaped regime has no stable crossing. Within the direct convention, these results define a Gibbs bifurcation structure for Bardeen-AdS thermodynamics.

</details>


### [2] [Future global stability of Maxwell-Jüttner equilibria and vacuum for the massless Boltzmann equation on FLRW spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00175)
*Robert M. Strain,Martin Taylor,Renato Velozo Ruiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper establishes global-in-time existence, uniqueness, and precise time-decay rates for small perturbations of non-stationary Maxwell-Jüttner equilibria (and vacuum) to the general relativistic massless Boltzmann equation on spatially toroidal FLRW spacetimes with scale factor $t^{\mathfrak{q}}$, $\mathfrak{q}\in[0,1]$, under hard ball interactions.
\n\n
本文在空间拓扑为 $\mathbb{T}^3$、尺度因子为 $t^{\mathfrak{q}}$（$\mathfrak{q}\in[0,1]$）的 Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) 时空中，针对硬球相互作用情形，建立了广义相对论无质量 Boltzmann 方程关于非定常 Maxwell-Jüttner 平衡态（及真空解）的小扰动的整体存在性、唯一性及其精确的时间衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand the long-time behavior of kinetic matter (described by the massless Boltzmann equation) in expanding cosmological spacetimes beyond the classical Minkowski setting. Specifically, the authors aim to rigorously analyze stability and decay properties of non-stationary equilibria that naturally arise in FLRW geometries with power-law expansion, filling a gap in the mathematical relativity and kinetic theory literature for non-flat, time-dependent backgrounds without symmetry assumptions.
\n\n
动机在于理解动能物质（由无质量 Boltzmann 方程描述）在膨胀宇宙时空中的长时间行为，超越经典的 Minkowski 背景。作者特别旨在严格分析在具有幂律膨胀的 FLRW 几何中自然出现的非定常平衡态的稳定性与衰减性质，填补了在无对称性假设的非平坦、含时背景下的数学相对论与动力学理论文献中的空白。

Method: The authors employ energy methods in a suitably weighted $L^2_p$-based Sobolev space adapted to the FLRW geometry. They linearize the massless Boltzmann equation around the time-dependent Maxwell-Jüttner equilibrium $\exp(-|t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p|)$ and derive a priori energy estimates. The key technical challenge is handling the time-dependent collision operator and geometric terms arising from the expanding background. For $\mathfrak{q} < 1/3$, they exploit enhanced dispersion due to rapid expansion to obtain superpolynomial decay via a bootstrap argument, while for $\mathfrak{q} \geq 1/3$, polynomial decay is derived from standard hypocoercivity techniques adapted to the curved spacetime. The vacuum case ($\mathfrak{q}>1/3$) is treated by direct estimation of the nonlinear collision term near zero density.
\n\n
作者在适应 FLRW 几何结构的加权 $L^2_p$ 型 Sobolev 空间中采用能量方法。他们将无质量 Boltzmann 方程在含时 Maxwell-Jüttner 平衡态 $\exp(-|t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p|)$ 附近线性化，并推导先验能量估计。关键技术难点在于处理由膨胀背景引起的含时碰撞算子和几何项。对于 $\mathfrak{q} < 1/3$，他们利用快速膨胀带来的增强色散效应，通过 bootstrap 论证获得超多项式衰减；而对于 $\mathfrak{q} \geq 1/3$，则通过适用于弯曲时空的标准 hypocoercivity 技术导出多项式衰减。真空情形（$\mathfrak{q}>1/3$）则通过对零密度附近的非线性碰撞项直接估计来处理。

Result: For $0 \leq \mathfrak{q} \leq 1$, small perturbations of the Maxwell-Jüttner equilibrium exist globally in the future and are unique. The perturbation decays in a weighted $L^2_p$ energy norm at rate $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}}\exp(-t^{1-3\mathfrak{q}})$ if $0\leq \mathfrak{q} < 1/3$, at rate $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}-c}$ for some $c>0$ if $\mathfrak{q}=1/3$, and at rate $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}}$ if $1/3< \mathfrak{q} \leq 1$. Additionally, for $1/3< \mathfrak{q}\leq 1$, small perturbations of the vacuum solution also exist globally and uniquely.
\n\n
对于 $0 \leq \mathfrak{q} \leq 1$，Maxwell-Jüttner 平衡态的小扰动在未来整体存在且唯一。扰动在加权 $L^2_p$ 能量范数下以如下速率衰减：当 $0\leq \mathfrak{q} < 1/3$ 时为 $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}}\exp(-t^{1-3\mathfrak{q}})$；当 $\mathfrak{q}=1/3$ 时为 $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}-c}$（其中 $c>0$ 为某常数）；当 $1/3< \mathfrak{q} \leq 1$ 时为 $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}}$。此外，对于 $1/3< \mathfrak{q}\leq 1$，真空解的小扰动也整体存在且唯一。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that the expansion rate of the FLRW universe, parameterized by $\mathfrak{q}$, critically determines the asymptotic stability and decay rate of kinetic equilibria. A phase transition occurs at $\mathfrak{q}=1/3$: slower expansion ($\mathfrak{q}<1/3$) leads to superpolynomial decay due to strong dispersive effects, while faster expansion ($\mathfrak{q}>1/3$) yields only polynomial decay but still ensures stability. The results provide a rigorous foundation for kinetic theory in time-dependent cosmological backgrounds.
\n\n
本工作表明，FLRW 宇宙的膨胀速率（由参数 $\mathfrak{q}$ 表征）对动能平衡态的渐近稳定性和衰减速率起决定性作用。在 $\mathfrak{q}=1/3$ 处发生相变：较慢膨胀（$\mathfrak{q}<1/3$）因强色散效应导致超多项式衰减，而较快膨胀（$\mathfrak{q}>1/3$）仅产生多项式衰减但仍保证稳定性。这些结果为含时宇宙学背景下的动力学理论提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: In this work we study the general relativistic massless Boltzmann equation on Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker spacetimes with spatial topology $\mathbb{T}^3$ in the linear and decelerated expanding regimes, where the scale factor is $t^{\mathfrak{q}}$ with $\mathfrak{q}\in [0,1]$. The massless Boltzmann equation on these backgrounds admits non-stationary Maxwell-Jüttner equilibria of the form $\exp(- |t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p|)$. For $0 \leq \mathfrak{q} \leq 1$, we prove future global-in-time existence and uniqueness of small perturbations of these equilibria in the case of hard ball interaction without symmetry assumptions. For $0\leq \mathfrak{q} < 1/3$, we prove that the perturbation -- measured in a suitable $L^2_p$ based energy norm -- decays at the superpolynomial time-decay rate of $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}}\exp(-t^{1-3\mathfrak{q}})$, whereas for $1/3< \mathfrak{q} \leq 1$ we obtain the polynomial time-decay rate of $t^{-3\mathfrak{q}}$. In the borderline case $\mathfrak{q}=1/3$, we show the time-decay of $t^{-3\mathfrak{q} -c}$ with a uniform constant $c>0$. Finally, for $\frac{1}{3}< \mathfrak{q}\leq 1$, we prove future global-in-time existence and uniqueness of small perturbations of the vacuum solution on $\mathbb{T}^3$.

</details>


### [3] [Analytical calculation of the observational parameters for tachyon inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00195)
*Marko Stojanovic,Neven Bilić,Goran S. Djordjevic,Dragoljub D. Dimitrijevic,Milan Milosevic*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper explores analytical computation of inflationary observables in tachyon inflation by using the Hubble rate $H(\phi)$ as a function of the tachyon field. It revisits earlier models in light of Planck 2018+ data, proposes new $H(\phi)$ ansätze that yield better agreement with observations, and introduces a novel approach where slow-roll Hubble flow parameters are treated as input functions—enabling fully analytical expressions for $n_{\rm s}$ and $r$. These predictions are tested against Planck, ACT DR6, and DESI data, and a proposed linear relation between the first two slow-roll parameters is examined for broader applicability.
\n\n
本文探讨了通过将哈勃率 $H(\phi)$ 视为快子场的函数，对快子暴胀模型中的观测参数进行解析计算。文章结合Planck 2018+等最新数据重新审视了早期模型，提出了新的 $H(\phi)$ 假设形式，显著改善了与观测的一致性，并创新性地将慢滚哈勃流参数作为输入函数处理，从而实现了对标量谱指数 $n_{\rm s}$ 和张量标量比 $r$ 的完全解析表达。这些预测结果与Planck、ACT DR6和DESI数据进行了对比，并讨论了前两个慢滚参数之间的线性关系在其他暴胀模型中的推广潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous studies of tachyon inflation relied on specific forms of the Hubble rate $H(\phi)$ that were constrained only by early Planck 2013 data, leading to tension with more recent cosmological observations. Moreover, analytical tractability in tachyon models is limited due to the non-canonical kinetic structure. The authors aim to improve observational compatibility while restoring analytical control by rethinking how $H(\phi)$ or its derived Hubble flow parameters are specified.
\n\n
以往对快子暴胀的研究依赖于特定形式的哈勃率 $H(\phi)$，且仅受早期Planck 2013数据约束，导致与近期宇宙学观测存在张力。此外，由于快子场具有非正则动能项，其解析可处理性受限。作者旨在通过重新构建 $H(\phi)$ 或其导出的哈勃流参数的设定方式，在提升与观测数据兼容性的同时恢复解析可控性。

Method: The authors adopt the Hubble parameter $H(\phi)$ as the fundamental input rather than the potential $V(\phi)$. They propose new functional forms for $H(\phi)$ and, more innovatively, directly specify the slow-roll Hubble flow parameters $\epsilon_1 = -\\dot{H}/H^2$ and $\epsilon_2 = d\\ln\\epsilon_1/dN$ as functions of e-folds $N$. This allows them to integrate the flow equations analytically to obtain $H(N)$, then relate it to $n_{\rm s}(N)$ and $r(N)$ via standard relations: $n_{\rm s} \\approx 1 - 2\\epsilon_1 - \\epsilon_2$, $r \\approx 16\\epsilon_1$. They also explore a linear ansatz $\\epsilon_2 = a + b\\epsilon_1$ motivated by data trends.
\n\n
作者采用哈勃参数 $H(\phi)$ 而非势能 $V(\phi)$ 作为基本输入。他们提出了新的 $H(\phi)$ 函数形式，并更具创新性地直接将慢滚哈勃流参数 $\epsilon_1 = -\\dot{H}/H^2$ 和 $\epsilon_2 = d\\ln\\epsilon_1/dN$ 设定为e-fold数 $N$ 的函数。这使得他们能够解析积分流方程以获得 $H(N)$，再通过标准关系 $n_{\rm s} \\approx 1 - 2\\epsilon_1 - \\epsilon_2$、$r \\approx 16\\epsilon_1$ 得到 $n_{\rm s}(N)$ 和 $r(N)$。他们还探索了由数据趋势启发的线性假设 $\\epsilon_2 = a + b\\epsilon_1$。

Result: For specific choices of $H(\phi)$ or linear $\\epsilon_2(\\epsilon_1)$ relations, the authors derive closed-form expressions for $n_{\rm s}$ and $r$ that lie within or near the 68% CL region of Planck 2018+BAO+Pantheon+ constraints. Numerical integration confirms analytical results. The linear relation $\\epsilon_2 \\approx -2\\epsilon_1$ (or similar) emerges as consistent with combined datasets including ACT DR6 and DESI, suggesting a possible universal feature across inflationary scenarios.
\n\n
对于特定的 $H(\phi)$ 形式或线性 $\\epsilon_2(\\epsilon_1)$ 关系，作者推导出了 $n_{\rm s}$ 和 $r$ 的闭合表达式，其值落在或接近Planck 2018+BAO+Pantheon+ 约束的68%置信区间内。数值积分验证了解析结果的正确性。线性关系 $\\epsilon_2 \\approx -2\\epsilon_1$（或类似形式）被发现与包含ACT DR6和DESI在内的联合数据集一致，暗示这可能是各类暴胀模型中的普适特征。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that treating the Hubble flow parameters as primary inputs—rather than deriving them from a potential—greatly enhances analytical solvability in non-canonical inflation like tachyon models, while achieving better agreement with modern cosmological data. The observed linear correlation between $\\epsilon_1$ and $\\epsilon_2$ may serve as a model-independent diagnostic for viable inflationary dynamics and warrants further investigation in other frameworks such as k-inflation or DBI inflation.
\n\n
本文表明，在快子暴胀等非正则暴胀模型中，将哈勃流参数作为基本输入（而非从势能导出）可显著提升解析可解性，同时更好地符合现代宇宙学观测数据。观测到的 $\\epsilon_1$ 与 $\\epsilon_2$ 之间的线性关联可能成为判断暴胀动力学可行性的模型无关诊断工具，值得在k-inflation、DBI暴胀等其他框架中进一步研究。

Abstract: We investigate the possibility of analytically calculating observational parameters in tachyon inflation cosmology, using the Hubble expansion rate as a function of the tachyon field. First, in light of the newer Planck results, we analyze previous investigations in which the test Hubble rate functions were confronted with Planck 2013 data. We propose and analyze a number of new test Hubble rate functions, finding considerable improvement and approaching reasonable agreement with recent observational data. For a class of these functions, we were able to carry out analytical calculations. As a novelty, we introduce a functional dependence of the slow-roll Hubble flow parameters as an input, which facilitates an analytical study of inflation. In this way, we obtain explicit expressions for the scalar spectral index ($n_{\rm s}$) and the tensor-to-scalar ratio ($r$). We confront our analytical and numerical predictions with Planck observational constraints. Finally, we discuss the proposed linear dependence between the first two slow-roll parameters in light of recent data from ACT DR6, DESI, and others, as well as the potential to generalize this dependence to other inflation models.

</details>


### [4] [Constraints on the Phenomenology of Dissipative Cosmological Memory from BAO (BOSS + DESI 2024) and Pantheon+ Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00227)
*S. M. Ponomarenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: We propose a phenomenological ``dissipative memory'' model in gravity, parameterized by a decaying fluid with amplitude $\eps$, decay scale $z_*$, and steepness index $β$, and test it against cosmological data. The best fit coincides with $\Lambda$CDM, yielding tight constraints: $\eps < 0.05$ (95\% CL) for $z_* < 2$, with strong statistical penalties ($Δ\mathrm{AIC}=+6.0$, $Δ\mathrm{BIC}=+9.9$).
\n\n
我们提出并检验了一种引力场中的“耗散记忆”唯象模型，该模型由一个具有振幅 $\eps$、衰减尺度 $z_*$ 和陡度指数 $β$ 的衰减流体参数化，并利用宇宙学数据进行测试。最佳拟合与 $\Lambda$CDM 完全一致，给出严格限制：对于 $z_* < 2$，有 $\eps < 0.05$（95\% 置信度），且统计惩罚显著（$Δ\mathrm{AIC}=+6.0$，$Δ\mathrm{BIC}=+9.9$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper is motivated by the possibility that early quantum gravitational processes could imprint relic signatures on late-time cosmic expansion. To explore this, the authors introduce a phenomenological framework—``dissipative memory''—that modifies the background expansion history through an effective fluid whose energy density decays according to a Debye-like law.
\n\n
本文的动机源于早期量子引力过程可能在晚期宇宙膨胀中留下遗迹信号的可能性。为探索这一点，作者引入了一个唯象框架——“耗散记忆”，通过一种有效流体修改背景膨胀历史，其能量密度按照类德拜（Debye-like）规律衰减。

Method: The authors construct a phenomenological model where the memory effect is encoded in an additional fluid component with energy density scaling as $\\rho_{\\rm mem}(z) = \\eps \\, \\rho_{\\rm crit,0} \\, \\exp[-(z/z_*)^β]$. They perform a joint Bayesian inference using BAO from BOSS DR12 and DESI 2024, Pantheon$+$ supernova distances, and $H_0$ measurements from SH0ES and Planck. Model comparison is conducted via $\\Delta \\chi^2$, AIC, and BIC.
\n\n
作者构建了一个唯象模型，其中记忆效应被编码为一个额外的流体成分，其能量密度按 $\\rho_{\\rm mem}(z) = \\eps \\, \\rho_{\\rm crit,0} \\, \\exp[-(z/z_*)^β]$ 缩放。他们使用来自 BOSS DR12 和 DESI 2024 的重子声学振荡（BAO）数据、Pantheon$+$ 超新星测距数据，以及 SH0ES 和 Planck 的 $H_0$ 测量值进行联合贝叶斯推断。通过 $\\Delta \\chi^2$、AIC 和 BIC 进行模型比较。

Result: The global fit yields a best-fit model indistinguishable from $\Lambda$CDM across the redshift range $z \in [0.3, 2.3]$, with $Δχ^2 < 0.01$ despite three extra parameters. Information criteria strongly disfavor the extended model ($Δ\mathrm{AIC}=+6.0$, $Δ\mathrm{BIC}=+9.9$). A 95\% CL upper bound of $\eps < 0.05$ is derived for $z_* < 2$.
\n\n
全局拟合结果表明，在红移范围 $z \in [0.3, 2.3]$ 内，最佳拟合模型与 $\Lambda$CDM 无法区分，尽管增加了三个额外参数，但 $Δχ^2 < 0.01$。信息准则强烈不支持扩展模型（$Δ\mathrm{AIC}=+6.0$，$Δ\mathrm{BIC}=+9.9$）。对于 $z_* < 2$，得到 95\% 置信度上限 $\eps < 0.05$。

Conclusion: The dissipative memory model is observationally consistent with $\Lambda$CDM but provides no improvement in fit quality. Current data place stringent limits on the amplitude of any such memory effect, effectively ruling out large deviations at low redshifts. Future probes targeting higher redshifts or non-background observables (e.g., perturbations) may offer better sensitivity.
\n\n
耗散记忆模型在观测上与 $\Lambda$CDM 一致，但并未改善拟合质量。当前数据对任何此类记忆效应的振幅施加了严格限制，有效地排除了低红移处的大偏离。未来针对更高红移或非背景观测量（如扰动）的探针可能提供更高的灵敏度。

Abstract: We propose and test a phenomenological model of ``dissipative memory'' in the gravitational field, where early quantum gravitational processes leave a relic signature in the cosmic expansion rate. The model is parameterized by an additional fluid with amplitude~$\eps$, decay scale~$z_*$, and a steepness index~$β$, decaying according to a Debye law $f(z)=\exp[{-(z/z_*)^β}]$. We perform a joint Bayesian analysis using baryon acoustic oscillation data from BOSS\,DR12 and DESI\,2024, photometric distances from Pantheon$+$, and~$\Hzero$ measurements (\SHOES\ and \Planck). A global optimization reveals that the best fit is identical to~$\Lcdm$: the memory correction vanishes across the entire observable range $z\in[0.3,\,2.3]$ ($Δχ^2 < 0.01$ with three extra parameters, $Δ\mathrm{AIC}=+6.0$, $Δ\mathrm{BIC}=+9.9$). This establishes an upper bound on the memory amplitude: $\eps < 0.05$ for $z_* < 2$ (95\% CL). We discuss the physical interpretation of this constraint and point out observational channels where the memory effect could potentially manifest.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent efforts to formulate quantum field theory in a manifestly covariant way, and developments in causal set theory, have highlighted the need to better understand the causal propagator and its spectral properties. Method: The authors analyze the causal propagator $Δ(x,x') = [φ(x),φ(x')]$ in free scalar QFT, focusing on its spectral decomposition. Explicit computations in simple spacetimes (e.g., Minkowski space) are used to test the proposed scaling law.

作者分析了自由标量 QFT 中的因果传播子 $Δ(x,x') = [φ(x),φ(x')]$，聚焦于其谱分解。他们基于量纲分析和对称性考虑，提出了其谱密度在高频极限下的渐近行为猜想，并通过在简单时空（如 Minkowski 时空）中的显式计算验证所提出的标度律。

Result: The paper presents a conjectured scaling law for the asymptotic spectral density of the causal propagator in free scalar field theory. Evidence is provided through explicit examples in flat spacetime, showing consistency with the proposed power-law behavior. This suggests a universal high-frequency structure analogous to Weyl’s law but adapted to Lorentzian signature.

文章提出了自由标量场论中因果传播子渐近谱密度的一个标度律猜想。通过平直时空中的显式例子提供了证据，表明结果与所提出的幂律行为一致。这暗示了一种普适的高频结构，类似于 Weyl 定律，但适用于 Lorentzian 度规。

Conclusion: The asymptotic spectral density of the causal propagator encodes fundamental information about Lorentzian spacetime structure, much like Weyl’s law does for Riemannian manifolds. Abstract: The causal propagator (or Pauli-Jordan function), which multiplied by $i$ is the spacetime commutator of the field $[φ(x),φ(x')]$, plays an essential role in scalar quantum field theory. We discuss the role of the causal propagator and its spectrum in recent developments in defining quantum field theory in a more explicitly covariant manner, as well as in causal set theory. We then present a conjecture for its asymptotic spectral density in a free theory, and give examples that lend evidence to the conjectured scaling. Our work has implications for Lorentzian spectral geometry in much the same way as Weyl's asymptotic law has for Riemannian spectral geometry.

*Oscar Lasso Andino,Fernando G. Veloz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper introduces a geometric method based on intrinsic curvatures (geodesic and Gaussian curvatures) to compute key features of black hole spacetimes—specifically photon spheres, massive particle surfaces, and shadow radii—using a perturbative framework. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Modeling spacetime near black holes is challenging due to strong gravity and nonlinearities in general relativity. 由于广义相对论中的强引力和非线性效应，黑洞附近的时空建模极具挑战性。现有的微扰方法通常依赖于坐标相关的展开，缺乏清晰的几何诠释。作者旨在发展一种基于曲率不变量的内蕴几何方法，以系统计算光子球和粒子轨道等可观测量，特别是在研究较少的类时情形中。

In the null case ($\\dot{x}^\\mu \\dot{x}_\\mu = 0$), this reproduces standard photon sphere results; in the timelike case ($\\dot{x}^\\mu \\dot{x}_\\mu = -1$), it provides new leading-order corrections. Mass dependence is analyzed by treating $M$ as a parameter and computing $\\partial r_{\\text{ph}}/\\partial M$ and $\\partial r_{\\text{ms}}/\\partial M$.
\n\n
作者采用微扰方案，将相对于参考时空（如史瓦西时空）的偏离视为微小量。关键在于，他们使用内蕴几何量——空间切片或相关二维曲面的测地曲率 $k_g$ 和高斯曲率 $K$ ——来表达轨道条件。对于圆轨道，条件 $k_g = 0$（表示在曲面上的测地运动）结合有效势的极值化，导出半径方程。在零测地情形（$\\dot{x}^\\mu \\dot{x}_\\mu = 0$）下，该方法复现了标准光子球结果；在类时情形（$\\dot{x}^\\mu \\dot{x}_\\mu = -1$）下，则给出了新的领头阶修正。通过将质量 $M$ 视为参数，分析其依赖性，并计算 $\\partial r_{\\text{ph}}/\\partial M$ 和 $\\partial r_{\\text{ms}}/\\partial M$。

Result: The method successfully recovers the known photon sphere radius $r_{\\text{ph}} = 3M$ in Schwarzschild spacetime at leading order in the null case. More importantly, it yields a new analytic expression for the radius of stable circular timelike orbits (massive particle surfaces) under perturbations, which has not been addressed in prior perturbative studies. Additionally, the paper derives how $r_{\\text{ph}}$ and $r_{\\text{ms}}$ vary with black hole mass $M$, revealing a universal scaling behavior that could inform observational tests with next-generation telescopes.
\n\n
该方法在零测地情形下成功复现了史瓦西时空中已知的光子球半径 $r_{\\text{ph}} = 3M$（领头阶）。更重要的是，它给出了微扰下稳定类时圆轨道（大质量粒子表面）半径的新解析表达式，这是以往微扰研究未涉及的。此外，论文推导了 $r_{\\text{ph}}$ 和 $r_{\\text{ms}}$ 随黑洞质量 $M$ 的变化关系，揭示了一种普适的标度行为，可为下一代望远镜的观测检验提供依据。

Conclusion: The proposed curvature-based geometric framework offers a coordinate-invariant and physically transparent way to compute black hole observables beyond standard perturbation theory. By linking orbital dynamics directly to intrinsic curvature, it opens new avenues for modeling strong-field gravity and provides testable predictions for future experiments such as those by the Event Horizon Telescope or LISA.
\n\n
所提出的基于曲率的几何框架提供了一种坐标无关且物理清晰的方法，用于计算超越标准微扰理论的黑洞可观测量。通过将轨道动力学直接与内蕴曲率联系起来，该方法为强场引力建模开辟了新途径，并为事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope）或LISA等未来实验提供了可检验的预言。

Abstract: The spacetime behavior at very extreme conditions, such as the regions near a black hole, can be very difficult to modelize. In this work we introduce a new geometric method that allows to calculate the parameters of photon spheres, massive particle surfaces and shadow radius of black holes. We build upon a perturvative approach but bases in intrinsic curvatures, such as the geodesic and Gaussian curvatures. At leading order, the method allows to find the radius of the perturbation in the time-like case, which has not been studied in the literature. In the null case we are able to recover the results found by the perturvative method only. We also study the mass variations and how they influence the photon sphere radius and the massive particle surface radius, leading to a new and powerful result, that could provide new different research directions. The approach presented here will provide a set of tools that will help to modelize gravity near extremely massive objects and help to improve the theoretical estimations of parameters than can be tested in the next generation experiments.

</details>


### [7] [Relativistic transformation of temperature revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00521)
*Soroor Pouryazdan,Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper resolves the long-standing controversy over the relativistic transformation of temperature by showing that the effective temperature $T_{\\text{eff}}$, as measured by a moving observer via transformed energy density, increases with velocity. This supports the Ott-Eddington-Møller formulation and demonstrates that temperature is observer-dependent, not Lorentz-invariant. A unified framework is achieved by identifying temperature with the inverse-temperature four-vector $\\beta^\\mu$.
\n\n
本文通过表明运动观测者从变换后的能量密度推断出的有效温度 $T_{\\text{eff}}$ 随速度增加，解决了温度的相对论变换这一长期争议。该结果支持 Ott-Eddington-Møller 公式，并证明温度是依赖于观测者的量，而非 Lorentz 不变量。通过将温度与逆温度四矢量 $\\beta^\\mu$ 联系起来，文章建立了一个统一的热力学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the historical inconsistency among proposed relativistic transformations of temperature—specifically the Planck-Einstein (temperature decreases with velocity), Ott-Eddington-Møller (temperature increases), and Landsberg (temperature is invariant) prescriptions. This ambiguity undermines a coherent relativistic thermodynamics, especially in contexts like cosmology or high-energy physics where thermal systems are observed in different inertial frames.
\n\n
动机源于历史上关于温度相对论变换的不同主张之间的不一致性——特别是 Planck-Einstein（温度随速度降低）、Ott-Eddington-Møller（温度升高）和 Landsberg（温度不变）这三种方案。这种模糊性阻碍了相对论热力学的一致性，尤其在宇宙学或高能物理等场景中，热力学系统需在不同惯性系中被观测。

Method: The authors adopt a relativistic thermodynamic and statistical approach, beginning with the energy-momentum tensor $T^{\\mu\\nu}$ of an isotropic fluid in its rest frame. They define the effective temperature $T_{\\text{eff}}$ operationally as the temperature inferred by a boosted observer from the Lorentz-transformed energy density $\\rho = T^{00}$. They apply this to three concrete systems: a photon gas (with equation of state $p = \\rho/3$), a relativistic ideal gas ($p = \\rho/3$ in ultra-relativistic limit), and a degenerate electron gas, computing $T_{\\text{eff}}(v)$ in each case. They further embed the result in a covariant framework using the inverse-temperature four-vector $\\beta^\\mu = u^\\mu / T$, where $u^\\mu$ is the fluid four-velocity.
\n\n
作者采用相对论热力学与统计方法，从各向同性流体在其静止系中的能量-动量张量 $T^{\\mu\\nu}$ 出发，操作性地定义有效温度 $T_{\\text{eff}}$ 为运动观测者从 Lorentz 变换后的能量密度 $\\rho = T^{00}$ 所推断的温度。他们将此方法应用于三个具体系统：光子气体（状态方程为 $p = \\rho/3$）、相对论理想气体（在极端相对论极限下 $p = \\rho/3$）和简并电子气体，分别计算各自的 $T_{\\text{eff}}(v)$。此外，他们通过引入逆温度四矢量 $\\beta^\\mu = u^\\mu / T$（其中 $u^\\mu$ 为流体四速度）将结果嵌入协变框架中。

Result: For all three systems studied—a photon gas, a relativistic ideal gas, and an electron gas—the effective temperature $T_{\\text{eff}}$ increases with the observer's relative velocity $v$, following $T_{\\text{eff}} = T \\gamma(v)$ in the photon gas case (where $\\gamma = 1/\\sqrt{1 - v^2/c^2}$), and exhibiting similar monotonic increase in others, modulated by their respective equations of state. This consistently favors the Ott-Eddington-Møller transformation over alternatives. The results confirm that temperature is not a Lorentz scalar but depends on the observer’s frame.
\n\n
对所研究的三种系统——光子气体、相对论理想气体和电子气体——有效温度 $T_{\\text{eff}}$ 均随观测者相对速度 $v$ 增加而升高；对于光子气体，满足 $T_{\\text{eff}} = T \\gamma(v)$（其中 $\\gamma = 1/\\sqrt{1 - v^2/c^2}$），其他系统也表现出类似的单调增长，但受各自状态方程调制。该结果一致支持 Ott-Eddington-Møller 变换，而非其他方案。结果证实温度并非 Lorentz 标量，而是依赖于观测者参考系的量。

Conclusion: Temperature in relativity must be treated as an observer-dependent quantity derived from the energy density measured in a given frame. The apparent contradictions in historical formulations arise from differing operational definitions. A consistent and covariant description is achieved by identifying the thermodynamic inverse temperature with the timelike component of the four-vector $\\beta^\\mu = u^\\mu / T$, thereby reconciling operational measurements with invariant thermodynamic structure.
\n\n
在相对论中，温度必须被视为依赖于观测者的量，由特定参考系中测得的能量密度导出。历史上不同公式的矛盾源于操作定义的差异。通过将热力学逆温度识别为四矢量 $\\beta^\\mu = u^\\mu / T$ 的类时分量，可实现一致且协变的描述，从而调和操作性测量与不变热力学结构。

Abstract: The relativistic transformation of temperature has long remained controversial, with the classical laws of Planck-Einstein, Ott-Eddington-Moller and Landsberg yielding conflicting results. We reexamine this issue from a relativistic thermodynamic and statistical perspective, starting from the energy-momentum tensor of an isotropic system and defining the effective temperature Teff as that inferred by a moving observer from the transformed energy density. Analyses of a photon gas, a relativistic ideal gas and an electron gas show that Teff consistently increases with velocity, supporting the Ott-Eddington interpretation while depending on the system's equation of state. These results indicate that temperature is not a Lorentz-invariant scalar but an observer-dependent quantity. A consistent relativistic description emerges when temperature is related to the inverse-temperature four-vector beta, linking operational and invariant viewpoints within a unified thermodynamic framework.

</details>


### [8] [Searching for a waveform-agnostic gravitational wave signal in pulsar timing arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00577)
*Heling Deng,Bence Becsy,Yuri Levin,Neil J. Cornish,Xavier Siemens*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper introduces a waveform-agnostic Bayesian method for detecting localized gravitational wave sources in pulsar timing array (PTA) data by modeling timing residuals with a Fourier expansion and using a Lorentzian hyperprior on coefficient variances. This approach simultaneously infers source sky location, spectral content, and the stochastic gravitational wave background while accounting for per-pulsar noise.
\n\n
本文提出了一种波形无关的贝叶斯方法，用于在脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）数据中探测局域引力波源：通过对计时残差进行傅里叶展开，并对傅里叶系数方差施加洛伦兹超先验（Lorentzian hyperprior），该方法可同时推断源天区位置、频谱特征以及随机引力波背景，同时考虑每颗脉冲星的噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing PTA searches for localized gravitational wave sources rely on predefined waveform templates, which are computationally expensive and may miss signals that deviate from expectations. A more flexible, template-free approach is needed to detect both anticipated and anomalous gravitational wave signals without sacrificing sensitivity or robustness.
\n\n
现有的针对局域引力波源的脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）搜索依赖于预定义的波形模板，计算成本高，且可能遗漏偏离预期的信号。因此，亟需一种更灵活、无需模板的方法，在不牺牲灵敏度或鲁棒性的前提下探测预期和异常的引力波信号。

Method: The authors model signal-induced timing residuals as a truncated Fourier series: $r(t) = \\sum_k (a_k \\cos \\omega_k t + b_k \\sin \\omega_k t)$. They place a Lorentzian hyperprior on the variances of the coefficients $(a_k, b_k)$, i.e., $\\langle a_k^2 \\rangle = \\langle b_k^2 \\rangle = S(\\omega_k; \\omega_0, \\gamma)$, where $S(\\omega) \\propto [1 + ((\\omega - \\omega_0)/\\gamma)^2]^{-1}$ defines a peaked spectral envelope centered at $\\omega_0$ with bandwidth $\\gamma$. The Fourier coefficients are analytically marginalized, yielding a likelihood that depends only on hyperparameters $(\\omega_0, \\gamma)$, sky location, and the amplitude of the stochastic background. Each pulsar is also assigned an independent flat-spectrum noise component to absorb unmodeled red noise.
\n\n
作者将信号引起的计时残差建模为截断傅里叶级数：$r(t) = \\sum_k (a_k \\cos \\omega_k t + b_k \\sin \\omega_k t)$。他们对系数 $(a_k, b_k)$ 的方差施加洛伦兹超先验，即 $\\langle a_k^2 \\rangle = \\langle b_k^2 \\rangle = S(\\omega_k; \\omega_0, \\gamma)$，其中 $S(\\omega) \\propto [1 + ((\\omega - \\omega_0)/\\gamma)^2]^{-1}$ 定义了一个以 $\\omega_0$ 为中心、带宽为 $\\gamma$ 的尖峰状谱包络。通过对傅里叶系数进行解析边缘化，得到仅依赖于超参数 $(\\omega_0, \\gamma)$、天区位置和随机背景振幅的似然函数。此外，每颗脉冲星还被赋予一个独立的平谱噪声分量，以吸收未建模的红噪声。

Result: Applied to simulated PTA datasets containing both localized continuous-wave sources and a stochastic background, the method accurately recovers the injected source parameters (sky position, central frequency, bandwidth) and remains sensitive to signals with non-standard morphologies. It also robustly separates the astrophysical signal from per-pulsar noise contributions.
\n\n
在包含局域连续波源和随机背景的模拟脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）数据集上应用该方法，能够准确恢复注入源的参数（天区位置、中心频率、带宽），并对非标准形态的信号保持灵敏度。该方法还能稳健地将天体物理信号与各脉冲星的噪声贡献分离开来。

Conclusion: This waveform-agnostic framework provides a computationally efficient, flexible, and robust alternative to template-based searches in PTAs. By avoiding strong assumptions about signal morphology, it enhances the discovery potential for both conventional and exotic gravitational wave sources in current and future nanohertz gravitational wave astronomy.
\n\n
这种波形无关的框架为脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）中的模板搜索提供了一种计算高效、灵活且鲁棒的替代方案。通过避免对信号形态做强假设，该方法提升了在当前及未来纳赫兹引力波天文学中发现常规和奇异引力波源的潜力。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays have recently provided compelling evidence for a nanohertz stochastic gravitational wave background, motivating searches for gravitational waves from localized sources. Most existing searches assume specific waveform templates, which can be computationally demanding and potentially insensitive to unexpected signals. We introduce a waveform-agnostic framework that models signal-induced timing residuals via a Fourier expansion. A Lorentzian hyperprior is imposed on the variances of the Fourier coefficients, providing a flexible spectral envelope that captures the signal's dominant frequency and bandwidth while remaining agnostic to its exact shape. Analytical marginalization over the Fourier coefficients then yields a Bayesian hierarchical framework that concurrently infers the source sky location, its frequency content, and the stochastic background. To mitigate contamination from unmodeled pulsar noise, we further allow for additional flat-spectrum features for each pulsar. Tests on simulated datasets show that the method is robust and provides a flexible tool for future PTA searches, with sensitivity to both expected and unexpected gravitational wave phenomena.

</details>


### [9] [Bekenstein-Hawking temperature from the Schwarzian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00911)
*Donato Bini,Giampiero Esposito*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper argues that Hawking radiation and black hole thermodynamics are fundamentally tied to the projective geometry of Killing horizons, with the Schwarzian derivative of the affine parameter along null geodesics encoding key thermodynamic quantities like surface gravity and Bekenstein-Hawking temperature.
\n\n
本文认为霍金辐射和黑洞热力学本质上与Killing视界的射影几何密切相关，沿零测地线的仿射参数的Schwarzian导数编码了表面引力和贝肯斯坦-霍金温度等关键热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hawking's original calculation relies on the exponential relation between the affine parameter $\\lambda$ and retarded time $u$, leading to a Schwarzian derivative proportional to the square of surface gravity $\\kappa$. This suggests that the intrinsic projective structure of the Killing horizon—characterized by $\\kappa^2$—may underlie black hole thermodynamics, motivating a deeper investigation into this geometric origin.

Method: The author analyzes the relationship between the affine parameter $\\lambda$ of null geodesics orthogonal to the Killing horizon and the retarded time $u$, computing the Schwarzian derivative $\\{ \\lambda, u \\}$. They examine spacetimes with variable mass (e.g., Vaidya-like models) and stationary geometries (Schwarzschild and Kerr), linking the logarithmic derivative of surface gravity $\\kappa$ to the Schwarzian. Abstract: Hawking's original derivation of particle creation by black holes in Schwarzschild spacetime exploits, among various concepts, the exponential dependence on the retarded time variable u of the affine parameter λof the null geodesics that are integral curves of the null vector field orthogonal to the Killing horizon. This exponential law implies that the Schwarzian derivative of λwith respect to u is minus a half the square of surface gravity. The black hole Killing horizon inherits an intrinsic projective structure, and the squared surface gravity is the invariant characterizing such a structure. There is therefore evidence that the Bekenstein-Hawking temperature is completely determined from the projective structure on the Killing horizon. As a further test, it is here shown that, in a spacetime model with variable mass parameter, the logarithmic derivative of surface gravity is determined by the Schwarzian of the affine parameter. The Schwarzian in Schwarzschild and Kerr geometries is also studied in detail. All these properties are a first step towards proving that black hole thermodynamics finds its mathematical foundations in the projective geometry of Killing horizons. Abstract: We present a simple derivation of the exact Planck spectrum of the quadrupole radiation from point masses moving apart nonrelativistically, essentially an analog for gravitational radiation. In our moving-mass picture, imaginary-time periodicity appears as a product-log trajectory of a quadrupole source. In the frequency domain, the power becomes proportional to the Planck distribution, $ω^3/(e^{2πcω/κ}-1)$. The resulting Planckian graviton energy spectrum has finite total energy and finite graviton number. The emitted spectrum is purely kinematic in origin: no equilibrium, horizon, or stochastic source is assumed.

Method: The authors reformulate the interior Schwarzschild solution for a constant density perfect fluid star in isotropic coordinates. They then derive the pressure profile, central values, and Misner–Sharp mass, and analyze special limiting cases including zero density and naked singularities, while checking compliance with classical energy conditions (null, weak, strong, dominant).

作者将恒定密度理想流体星体的内部Schwarzschild解重新表述为isotropic coordinates下的形式。他们将线元写为 $$ds^2 = -A(r)^2 dt^2 + B(r)^2 (dr^2 + r^2 d\\Omega^2),$$ 其中 $A(r)$ 和 $B(r)$ 是isotropic径向坐标 $r$ 的简单有理函数。随后推导压力分布、中心值和Misner–Sharp质量，并分析包括零密度和裸奇点在内的特殊极限情形，同时检验其对经典能量条件（null, weak, strong, dominant energy conditions）的满足情况。

Result: In isotropic coordinates, the metric functions are rational and directly tied to central density $\\rho_c$ and central pressure $p_c$. The pressure profile becomes algebraically simple: $p(r) \\propto (A(r) - A_0)/(A(r) + A_0)$. The analysis reveals: (1) a physically meaningful zero-density solution where gravity arises purely from pressure, confirming “pressure gravitates”; (2) a naked singularity solution violating only the dominant energy condition; and (3) a combined case where pressure alone generates a naked singularity. These cases remain consistent with all other standard energy conditions.
\n\n
在isotropic coordinates下，度规函数是有理函数，且直接关联于中心密度 $\\rho_c$ 和中心压力 $p_c$。压力分布变得代数上极为简单：$p(r) \\propto (A(r) - A_0)/(A(r) + A_0)$。分析揭示：(1) 一个具有物理意义的零密度解，其中引力纯粹由压力产生，证实了“pressure gravitates（压力产生引力）”；(2) 一个仅违反dominant energy condition（主能量条件）的裸奇点解；(3) 一个压力单独产生裸奇点的组合情形。这些情形均满足其余所有标准能量条件。

Conclusion: The interior Schwarzschild solution in isotropic coordinates provides exceptional clarity and simplicity, making it ideal for both research insight and pedagogical exposition. Its ability to isolate the gravitational role of pressure—even in the absence of matter density—and to generate exotic spacetimes while respecting most energy conditions demonstrates that key physical lessons from this century-old solution remain underexploited.
\n\n
isotropic coordinates下的内部Schwarzschild解提供了非凡的清晰性和简洁性，使其非常适合用于研究洞察和教学阐述。该解能够分离出压力的引力作用（即使在无物质密度的情况下），并在满足大多数能量条件的同时生成奇异时空，表明这一百年-old解中蕴含的关键物理启示仍未被充分挖掘。

Abstract: Herein we shall revisit the venerable 110-year-old topic of Schwarzschild's constant density star, emphasizing that for many (though not quite all) purposes it is much easier to analyze this spacetime in isotropic coordinates (\emph{versus} the more usually adopted Hilbert--Droste area coordinates). The relevant line element is particularly transparent, containing two simple rational functions of the radial coordinate, and the two physical parameters appearing in this line element are easily and readily interpretable in terms of the central density and central pressure of the star. Local properties in the stellar interior (such as the pressure profile) will be seen to be remarkably simple, though quasi-local properties like the Misner--Sharp mass are just a little bit trickier. Apart from its simplicity and clarity, the analysis is also of considerable pedagogical interest. For instance, there are a number of interesting special cases. Mathematically there is a perfectly good solution corresponding to a zero density star -- which can physically be interpreted as an explicit verification of the fact that pressure gravitates, even in the absence of mass-energy density. Additionally, there is a singular solution containing a naked singularity that satisfies all but one of the standard classical energy conditions. Furthermore you can even do both, combining zero density with a naked singularity -- so that pressure by itself can generate naked singularities -- at the cost of merely violating the dominant energy condition, the least physical of the standard energy conditions. We argue that many physically interesting features of Schwarzschild's star are very much under-appreciated.

Abstract: The Laplace-Beltrami formalism, in which the Ricci tensor in the Einstein field equations (EFEs) is formulated at leading-order in terms of the partial-differential Laplace-Beltrami operator, was previously applied to coalescing compact binaries (CCBs) generating gravitational waves (GWs).   This heuristic work focuses broadly on the EFEs themselves under the Laplace-Beltrami formalism, considering all differential orders up to second-order. This namely includes a deeper analysis on the variational methodology employed on the EFEs in the second-order sector, utilized in previous works, and the benchmark analysis of the lower first- and zeroth-order terms. This all-order report utilizes representative examples and select metric Ansätze to explore the formalism's practicality and its limitations; this is shown that the first-order decomposition showcases heuristically the mechanics of vector and scalar fields upon a curved spacetime.

Abstract: Traversable wormholes are among the most interesting solutions of gravitational theories, but within General Relativity they generally require exotic matter violating the null energy condition. Modified gravity theories with matter-geometry coupling provide a promising framework in which wormhole geometries may instead be supported by effective gravitational contributions. Motivated by this possibility, we investigate rotating traversable wormholes in $f(R,T)$ gravity, where $R$ is the scalar curvature and $T$ is the trace of the energy-momentum tensor, within the slow-rotation approximation. A preliminary stability analysis based on sound-speed conditions indicates the physical viability of the solutions. These results demonstrate that rotating wormholes in $f(R,T)$ gravity constitute physically consistent compact configurations with potentially observable astrophysical signatures.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional iterative methods for resolving Galactic double white dwarf (DWD) binaries in space-based gravitational wave (GW) data operate within a frequentist framework that neglects valuable astrophysical information, such as the physical relationship between GW frequency $f$ and its time derivative $\dot{f}$ imposed by white dwarf mass limits. Method: The authors augment the iterative $\mathtt{GBSIEVER}$ pipeline with a strong astrophysical prior derived from the Chandrasekhar mass limit and observed properties of detached white dwarfs. This prior enforces a physically motivated constraint between $f$ and $\dot{f}$: since the chirp mass $\mathcal{M}_c$ is bounded, $\dot{f} \propto f^{11/3} \mathcal{M}_c^{5/3}$ implies a tight correlation. During each iteration of source subtraction, this prior is used to guide parameter estimation (e.g., in template matching or Bayesian inference), effectively pruning unphysical candidates and enhancing signal identification in crowded regions. The method is tested on simulated LISA and LISA–Taiji network data across multiple DWD population realizations.

作者将基于钱德拉塞卡质量极限和分离白矮星观测特性的强天体物理先验引入迭代算法 $\mathtt{GBSIEVER}$。该先验对 $f$ 与 $\dot{f}$ 施加物理约束：由于啁啾质量 $\mathcal{M}_c$ 存在上下限，而 $\dot{f} \propto f^{11/3} \mathcal{M}_c^{5/3}$，二者存在紧密关联。在每次迭代的源减除过程中，该先验用于引导参数估计（如模板匹配或贝叶斯推断），有效剔除非物理解并增强在拥挤区域的信号识别能力。该方法在多个 DWD 总体实现的模拟 LISA 及 LISA–太极联合数据上进行了测试。

Result: The modified $\mathtt{GBSIEVER}$ pipeline increases the number of confidently resolved DWD sources by approximately $7.3\%$ in LISA-only data and $14.6\%$ in LISA–Taiji network data compared to the baseline frequentist approach. These gains are consistent across multiple realistic DWD population simulations and persist even in the low-frequency ($f \lesssim 2$ mHz) confusion-dominated regime, where source density is highest.

改进后的 $\mathtt{GBSIEVER}$ 管道相比基线频率学方法，在 LISA 单独数据中可靠分辨的 DWD 源数量提升约 7.3%，在 LISA–太极联合网络中提升约 14.6%。参数估计精度（如 $f$、$\dot{f}$、天区位置和振幅）也有所提高。这些增益在多个现实 DWD 总体模拟中保持一致，即使在低频（$f \lesssim 2$ mHz）混淆主导区域（源密度最高）依然显著。

Conclusion: Incorporating physically motivated astrophysical priors into iterative gravitational wave source extraction pipelines significantly enhances both the yield and fidelity of resolved double white dwarf binaries. 将基于物理的天体物理先验引入迭代式引力波源提取管道，可显著提升双白矮星分辨数量与参数保真度。这表明，即使是计算高效的类频率学方法也能从贝叶斯式的物理约束中获益，从而弥合全局拟合与迭代方法之间的差距。该结果凸显了在未来空间引力波天文台面对高维、混淆主导数据时，领域知识在数据分析中的关键价值。

Abstract: Resolving the crowded population of double white dwarf (DWD) binaries in data from spaceborne gravitational wave (GW) observatories (e.g., LISA, Taiji) remains a major analysis challenge. Comparable performance on addressing this problem has been achieved with two main approaches: global fit, in which resolvable sources are estimated simultaneously from the data, and iterative, where sources are estimated one at a time and subtracted out from the data. While the latter is computationally efficient, methods developed under this approach have traditionally followed a frequentist framework that ignores astrophysical priors. This work incorporates a strong astrophysical prior, derived from the mass limits of detached white dwarfs and linking the GW signal frequency $f$ with its time derivative $\dot{f}$, into the iterative $\mathtt{GBSIEVER}$ pipeline. The improvement persists across multiple realistic DWD population realizations, including in the low-frequency confusion-dominated regime, demonstrating the robustness and practical utility of astrophysically informed priors in iterative source extraction.

作者提出在搭载超稳光学钟的卫星之间采用单向多普勒跟踪。他们为引力波探测制定了专用测量协议，建模了主要噪声来源（如钟不稳定度、激光相位噪声和航天器运动），并模拟了致密双星并合产生的引力波信号。通过Fisher矩阵分析进行参数估计，评估对源参数（如啁啾质量 $\\mathcal{M}$、光度距离 $D_L$）的测量精度。

研究表明，空间原子钟网络在毫赫兹至赫兹频段可实现具有竞争力的灵敏度。即使在对钟稳定性和任务设计采取保守假设的情况下，该系统仍能以与LISA相当或互补的精度恢复关键双星参数。结合适当的数据分析技术，单向多普勒方法能有效从其他噪声源中分离出引力波引起的频率偏移。

Abstract: Since the first detection of a merging binary black hole system a decade ago, gravitational-wave astronomy has emerged as a powerful tool for astrophysics. In parallel, proposed atom-based gravitational-wave detectors, specifically those based on space-based optical clocks and atom interferometers, offer capabilities that are unique and complementary to traditional optical interferometers. 本文提出了一个将马格努斯力和升力（即与自旋耦合的环境效应，EEs）纳入极端质量比旋近（EMRI）在气体介质中演化的框架。这些环境效应在多年观测窗口内会在引力波信号中产生可测量的相位偏移，并且气体阻力打破了真空中次级天体自旋大小与倾角之间的基本简并性，从而提高了自旋参数的可测性，并为探测大质量黑洞周围的吸积流提供了新手段。

  <summary>Details</summary>

The authors aim to quantify how these spin-coupled environmental effects alter EMRI waveforms and whether they can be leveraged to break parameter degeneracies and enhance physical inference.

在真空广义相对论中，极端质量比旋近（EMRI）的建模不考虑环境相互作用，导致引力波观测中次级致密天体的自旋大小与轨道倾角之间存在基本简并性。然而，许多天体物理EMRI很可能发生在大质量黑洞附近的气体环境中，此时由于次级天体的自旋会产生流体力学力——如阻力、马格努斯力和升力。作者旨在量化这些与自旋耦合的环境效应如何改变EMRI波形，并探究其是否可用于打破参数简并性并增强物理推断能力。

Method: The authors develop an effective framework that augments standard EMRI equations of motion with additional hydrodynamic forces: a drag force proportional to $v^2$, a Magnus force proportional to $\vec{\omega} \times \vec{v}$, and a lift force dependent on spin orientation relative to velocity. They integrate these spin-coupled environmental effects into the orbital evolution and compute the resulting gravitational-wave phase evolution. A Fisher matrix analysis is then employed to assess parameter estimation accuracy, particularly focusing on the secondary’s spin magnitude and inclination angle, comparing scenarios with and without environmental interactions.

作者构建了一个有效框架，在标准EMRI运动方程中加入额外的流体力学力：与$v^2$成正比的阻力、与$\vec{\omega} \times \vec{v}$成正比的马格努斯力，以及依赖于自旋方向相对于速度方向的升力。他们将这些与自旋耦合的环境效应纳入轨道演化，并计算由此产生的引力波相位演化。随后采用Fisher矩阵分析评估参数估计精度，特别关注次级天体的自旋大小和倾角，并比较包含与不包含环境相互作用的情形。

Result: The inclusion of environmental effects induces a cumulative dephasing in the gravitational waveform that is detectable over the multi-year observation windows of space-based detectors like LISA. Crucially, gas drag breaks the vacuum degeneracy between the secondary's spin magnitude ($\chi$) and orbital inclination ($\iota$), significantly improving the precision of spin measurements. The Fisher matrix analysis shows tighter constraints on both spin parameters when EEs are accounted for, demonstrating that environmental interactions enhance rather than hinder parameter inference.

引入环境效应会在引力波形中产生累积相位偏移，在LISA等空间引力波探测器的多年观测窗口内可被探测到。关键的是，气体阻力打破了次级天体自旋大小（$\chi$）与轨道倾角（$\iota$）之间的真空简并性，显著提高了自旋测量的精度。Fisher矩阵分析表明，在计入环境效应后，对这两个自旋参数的约束明显收紧，证明环境相互作用增强了而非阻碍了参数推断能力。

Conclusion: Environmental effects in gaseous accretion flows are not merely perturbative corrections but carry critical information about the secondary’s intrinsic spin and the surrounding medium. Crucially, a Fisher matrix analysis reveals that gas drag breaks the fundamental vacuum-projection degeneracy between the secondary's spin magnitude and inclination, thereby tightening parameter constraints. Thus, accounting for EEs is not merely a modeling necessity, but a powerful tool for enhancing the detectability of the secondary's intrinsic spin, and could serve as a novel probe of accretion flows harboring massive black holes.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to distinguish observationally between exotic compact objects like traversable wormholes and classical black holes, particularly in light of high-resolution imaging by the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT). 动机在于通过高分辨率成像（如事件视界望远镜EHT）在观测上区分奇异致密天体（如可穿越虫洞）与经典黑洞。由于Ellis–Bronnikov虫洞和Schwarzschild黑洞均为球对称真空解，可能产生相似的引力透镜特征，因此需要精细的辐射建模以识别潜在的观测判据。

Method: The authors perform general relativistic radiative transfer (GRRT) simulations assuming a spherically symmetric steady-state accretion flow around both the Ellis–Bronnikov wormhole and the Schwarzschild black hole. Synchrotron emission is incorporated as the radiation mechanism. The simulations trace null geodesics backward from the observer to compute observed intensity maps, accounting for gravitational redshift, Doppler boosting, and optical depth effects in curved spacetime.

作者对Ellis–Bronnikov虫洞和Schwarzschild黑洞周围的球对称稳态吸积流进行了广义相对论辐射转移（GRRT）模拟，并采用同步辐射作为辐射机制。模拟从观测者出发反向追踪零测地线，计算观测强度图，同时考虑弯曲时空中的引力红移、多普勒聚束和光学深度效应。

Result: The simulated images reveal that the central shadow and surrounding photon ring of the Ellis–Bronnikov wormhole are significantly brighter than those of the Schwarzschild black hole. Conclusion: While the Ellis–Bronnikov wormhole and Schwarzschild black hole yield distinct brightness profiles in their shadows and photon rings due to fundamental geometric differences (notably the presence or absence of an event horizon), current EHT data lack the sensitivity to definitively discriminate between them. Abstract: We investigate the observational differences between the Ellis--Bronnikov (EB) wormhole and the Schwarzschild black hole (BH) by performing general relativistic radiative transfer (GRRT) simulations. We consider a spherically symmetric steady-state accretion flow and perform GRRT simulations incorporating synchrotron emission. For both the EB wormhole and the Schwarzschild BH, the simulated images consist of a central shadow region and a bright photon ring. We find that both the shadow region and the photon ring of the EB wormhole are brighter than those of the Schwarzschild BH. These differences arise from the absence of an event horizon in the EB wormhole, allowing the emission from the accreting matter around and beyond the throat to contribute to the observed intensity. We also compare the simulated images with the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) observations of M87* and find that both the EB wormhole and the Schwarzschild BH are in reasonable agreement with the current EHT results.

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) are black holes generated in the early universe without having gone through stellar evolution. In the standard formation process, PBHs are formed from super-horizon primordial fluctuations with non-linearly large initial amplitude. In order to simulate the non-linear gravitational dynamics of PBH formation, one has to rely on numerical relativity solvers to approximate the solution of the Einstein equations. In order to resolve the collapsing region, non-Cartesian scale-up coordinates and a fixed mesh-refinement procedure are implemented. In COSMOS, a massless scalar field and a perfect fluid with a linear equation of state are implemented as matter fields. For each geometry, they compute: (1) the quasi-normal mode (QNM) frequencies $\omega_{\ell n} = \omega_R + i \omega_I$ for low-lying $\ell$ and overtone number $n=0$; and (2) the properties of the unstable circular photon orbit—specifically, the critical impact parameter $b_c$ and the Lyapunov exponent $\lambda_L$, which governs the decay rate of nearby trajectories. They then test the eikonal correspondence relations: $\omega_R \approx \frac{\ell}{b_c}$ and $|\omega_I| \approx \frac{\lambda_L}{\sqrt{2}}$, assessing their accuracy quantitatively across models and $\ell$ values.

Result: The study finds that the eikonal correspondence between QNM frequencies and photon orbit characteristics holds with remarkable accuracy—even for the lowest multipoles ($\ell = 2, 3$)—across all tested modified BH geometries. Conclusion: The paper confirms that the eikonal correspondence linking gravitational wave quasi-normal modes to photon ring observables is not only theoretically robust but also practically accurate for low-$\ell$ modes in a wide class of non-Kerr black hole spacetimes. Abstract: Black holes (BHs) can be studied via fundamentally different observational channels that probe complementary aspects of their physics. Abstract: The characterization of the photon region -- i.e. the region of space-time filled with unstable bound null geodesics -- is critical to understand the behavior of radiation near a compact-enough object, such as black holes. In this work, we present a novel covariant approach to describe radiation in dynamical spherical space-times. This allows a general description of photon surfaces and their dynamics in non-static space-times, recovering well known expressions in the static limit and clarifying their meanings. Furthermore, we illustrate our results via several examples of dynamical scenarios in stellar collapse and accreting/evaporating models, and discuss the open caveats regarding such scenarios.

本文分析了电影《星际穿越》中所描绘的极端引力时间膨胀效应：行星上1小时相当于地球上的7年。这种时间膨胀要求行星轨道非常接近黑洞视界，而只有在黑洞高速自转（Kerr黑洞）的情况下才可能存在这样的稳定轨道，因为非旋转（Schwarzschild）黑洞的最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）位于$3 r_s$处，其内部无法存在束缚轨道。作者对快速旋转Kerr黑洞附近的近视界轨道进行了广义相对论计算，既具有理论趣味性，也可能具有天体物理意义。

该研究的动机源于电影《星际穿越》所提出的科学雄心：需要一种物理上合理的机制来实现极端时间膨胀（约60,000倍）。这要求将一颗宜居行星置于极其靠近黑洞视界的稳定轨道上。由于Schwarzschild黑洞受最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）限制而无法满足此条件，本文旨在探索广义相对论是否以及如何允许在高速旋转（Kerr）黑洞周围存在此类轨道，从而既验证电影的科学基础，又探讨强场引力中的一个真实问题。

The key method involves solving for the orbital angular velocity and redshift factor in the equatorial plane of the Kerr metric.

作者采用Kerr时空中的测地线方程精确解，计算大质量粒子（类时测地线）在极大或近极大自旋黑洞视界附近任意接近处的稳定圆轨道。他们分析了轨道半径、黑洞自旋参数$a$与引力时间膨胀因子$\Delta t_{\infty} / \Delta \tau$之间的关系，其中$\Delta t_{\infty}$为无穷远处（地球）的坐标时，$\Delta \tau$为行星上的固有时。核心方法包括求解Kerr度规赤道面上的轨道角速度和红移因子。

Result: The paper demonstrates that for a Kerr black hole with spin parameter $a \to M$ (in geometrized units), stable circular orbits can exist arbitrarily close to the event horizon at $r \to M$. In this limit, the time dilation factor diverges, allowing for the extreme ratio of 1 hour ≈ 7 years (i.e., $\Delta t_{\infty}/\Delta \tau \approx 61{,}320$) required by the film. Abstract: The film Interstellar is grounded in real physics calculations. For a non-rotating (Schwarzschild) black hole, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) lies at three times the Schwarzschild radius; a bound orbit between the ISCO and the event horizon is not possible. Surprisingly, general relativity allows such orbits to exist if the black hole is spinning rapidly. In this work, we present computations that are non-trivial and interesting in themselves, but more importantly, they may have useful astrophysical implications.

*Ilkka Mäkinen*

TL;DR: The paper proposes a modified loop quantum cosmology (LQC) model by incorporating a Lorentzian term derived from the scalar curvature of the spatial manifold, inspired by quantum-reduced loop gravity. 该论文提出了一种修正的圈量子宇宙学（LQC）模型，通过引入一个源自空间流形标量曲率的洛伦兹项（受量子约化圈引力中单顶点模型启发）来修改标准LQC哈密顿量。该修正保留了奇点通过“量子反弹”得以解决以及时间对称动力学等关键特征，但与标准LQC相比，反弹发生在显著更小的体积处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from seeking a more refined effective description in loop quantum cosmology that incorporates geometric information beyond the standard holonomy corrections—specifically, curvature effects suggested by full loop quantum gravity. 动机源于在圈量子宇宙学中寻求一种比标准holonomy修正更精细的有效描述——特别是纳入来自完整圈量子引力所暗示的曲率效应。作者受到量子约化圈引力中单顶点模型的启发，在该模型中标量曲率算符自然出现，从而促使他们将这一项作为洛伦兹修正项加入宇宙学哈密顿量中。

Method: The authors construct an effective Hamiltonian for a homogeneous and isotropic universe by augmenting the standard LQC Hamiltonian with a Lorentzian term proportional to the spatial scalar curvature $^{(3)}R$. This term is heuristically motivated by the form of the curvature operator in the one-vertex model of quantum-reduced loop gravity. They then analyze the resulting effective Friedmann-like equations to study the modified dynamics, focusing on the behavior near the classical singularity and the properties of the quantum bounce.\n\n
作者通过在标准LQC哈密顿量中加入一个与空间标量曲率 $^{(3)}R$ 成正比的洛伦兹项，构建了一个均匀且各向同性宇宙的有效哈密顿量。该项由量子约化圈引力单顶点模型中的曲率算符形式启发而得。随后，他们分析由此得到的有效Friedmann型方程，研究修正后的动力学行为，重点关注经典奇点附近及量子反弹的特性。

The effective dynamics of the new model is not identical to standard LQC but all the key features of the dynamics are qualitatively reproduced. Abstract: The construction of a static traversable wormhole requires exotic matter that satisfies the Morris-Thorne conditions. They employ compactification $r \mapsto \rho = r/(1 + r)$ to map $r \in [0, \infty)$ to $\rho \in [0, 1]$, placing $\mathscr{I}^+$ at $\rho = 1$. Constraint-preserving additions are introduced to maintain symmetric hyperbolicity. The SBP property ensures discrete energy estimates mimicking the continuum. Dissipation operators are redefined abstractly to satisfy the dissipative property (DP) in the SBP energy norm, even at coordinate singularities and $\mathscr{I}^+$. The scheme is implemented at second-order accuracy but is extendable. New convergence tests based on solution norms—not just pointwise errors—are proposed for more robust validation.

作者在Minkowski时空的双曲面切片上，针对线性波动方程，在球极坐标$(r, \theta, \phi)$中推导了一个三维SBP有限差分格式。他们采用紧致化$r \mapsto \rho = r/(1 + r)$，将$r \in [0, \infty)$映射到$\rho \in [0, 1]$，使$\mathscr{I}^+$位于$\rho = 1$。通过场重标度$\Psi = \Omega^\alpha \psi$（其中$\Omega$为共形因子）简化方程并改善正则性。引入保约束项以维持对称双曲性。SBP性质确保离散能量估计模拟连续情形。耗散算子被抽象地重新定义，使其在SBP能量范数下满足耗散性质（DP），即使在坐标奇点和$\mathscr{I}^+$处亦然。该格式以二阶精度实现，但可推广。作者还提出了基于解范数（而非仅点态误差）的新收敛性测试，以实现更稳健的验证。

Abstract: We derive a fully 3-dimensional Summation-By-Parts scheme for a class of linear wave equations on hyperboloidal slices that meet future null infinity on a Minkowski background. Dissipation operators are given a more abstract treatment, which makes it possible to define them everywhere in the domain, including at the boundary points, in curvilinear coordinates, such that they satisfy the dissipative property (DP) in our energy norms. These generalizations reduce to the well-known Kreiss-Oliger dissipation operators whenever defined on a Cartesian grid in the bulk and satisfy the DP in the standard $L^2$-norms. Abstract: Recent time-domain analyses suggest that black hole stability may be sensitive to localized near-horizon geometric deformations, while the underlying spectral mechanism remains unclear. ### [27] [Spectral suppression of black hole ringdown tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02146)

For Gaussian sources, suppression scales as $e^{-α^2/2}$. The same $α$ governs both tail suppression and the transition between broadband and frequency-selective black hole responses. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Price's law predicts a universal late-time power-law decay ($\sim t^{-n}$) in black hole perturbation theory, yet this feature is conspicuously missing in gravitational waveforms from numerical simulations of quasi-circular binary black hole mergers. Price定律预言了黑洞微扰理论中普适的晚期幂律衰减（$\sim t^{-n}$），但这一特征在准圆双黑洞并合的数值模拟引力波形中明显缺失。理解这一差异对于连接解析的黑洞微扰理论与真实并合动力学、以及正确建模晚期引力辐射至关重要。

Method: The authors analyze the branch-cut contribution to the Green’s function in the frequency domain, which governs the late-time tail. They model the perturbation source as an oscillatory function with carrier frequency $ν$ and width $σ$, compute the excitation coefficient of the branch cut via Fourier transform, and derive its dependence on the dimensionless parameter $α = σν$. They validate their analytic predictions using time-domain Regge-Wheeler equation evolutions and compare leading- and next-to-leading-order tail amplitudes with numerical fits.

作者分析了频域格林函数中支割线（branch-cut）对晚期尾部的贡献。他们将扰动源建模为具有载频$ν$和宽度$σ$的振荡函数，通过傅里叶变换计算支割线激发系数，并推导其对无量纲参数$α = σν$的依赖关系。他们利用时域Regge-Wheeler方程演化验证解析预言，并将主导阶与次主导阶尾部振幅与数值拟合结果进行比较。

Result: The branch-cut excitation coefficient—and hence the amplitude of the late-time tail—is exponentially suppressed by $e^{-α^2/2}$ for a Gaussian source, with $α = σν$. This suppression is exact and confirmed by Regge-Wheeler evolutions. The same $α$ controls whether the black hole response is broadband ($α \ll 1$) or frequency-selective ($α \gg 1$). Analytically derived tail coefficients agree with numerical results at the $\sim10\%$ level. Quasi-circular mergers have large $α$, suppressing tails; head-on or eccentric mergers have small $α$, enhancing tails.

Method: The authors solve the Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV) equations modified by the stress-energy tensor of pure quasi-topological electromagnetism (QTEM) coupled to a quark matter equation of state. The dyonic charge ratio (e.g., $q_m/q_e$) is treated as a tunable parameter, and stellar structure solutions are obtained numerically across this parameter space, analyzing mass-radius ($M/R$) relations and internal pressure profiles.

Conclusion: The divergence of the Casimir energy near the Lorentz-breaking vacuum is not a physical prediction of infinite vacuum energy but arises from the noncommutativity of two operations: quantizing the theory around a regular background and then taking the limit to the degenerate vacuum, versus imposing the degeneracy condition $S_m(P_\star)=0$ before quantization. This highlights the subtlety in defining quantum fluctuations around degenerate vacua in constrained Hamiltonian systems.

卡西米尔能在洛伦兹破缺真空附近的发散并非无限真空能的物理预言，而是源于两个操作的不可交换性：一是围绕正规背景量子化后再取简并真空极限，二是先施加简并条件 $S_m(P_\star)=0$ 再进行量子化。这凸显了在约束哈密顿系统中，围绕简并真空定义量子涨落的微妙性。

Abstract: We study the Casimir response of electromagnetic fluctuations near magnetic vacua that spontaneously break Lorentz symmetry in gauge-invariant nonlinear electrodynamics. The theory is formulated in the Plebański first-order representation, with a single-invariant Hamiltonian potential $\widehat V(P)$ taken as the fundamental nonlinear object. This formulation is particularly useful because the nontrivial vacua are obtained as stationary points of the effective Hamiltonian, rather than as extrema of $\widehat V(P)$ itself. In the magnetic branch, the symmetry-breaking condition is governed by $S_m(P) \equiv \widehat V_P(P)+2P\widehat V_{PP}(P)$, whose vanishing also signals the degeneracy of the Hamiltonian constraint structure and the loss of rank of the longitudinal magnetic response. We first linearize around a regular purely magnetic background $\bar P$, with $S_m(\bar P)\neq0$, and obtain an ordinary Maxwell-like branch together with an extraordinary anisotropic branch controlled by $α(\bar P)=\widehat V_P(\bar P)/S_m(\bar P)$. We then compute the regularized parallel-plate Casimir energy for magnetic backgrounds perpendicular and parallel to the plates. As the regular background approaches the exact Lorentz-breaking vacuum $P_\star$, where $S_m(P_\star)=0$, the extraordinary branch becomes singular and, in the parallel configuration, the Casimir energy diverges within the regular-sector description. A direct analysis on the degenerate surface shows, however, that the extraordinary branch does not survive as an independent physical propagating mode for generic momenta. The divergence is therefore not a prediction of an infinite vacuum energy at the exact Lorentz-breaking state, but a diagnostic of the noncommutativity between quantizing the regular theory and imposing $S_m(P_\star)=0$ before quantization.

If the dCFT is holographic, $S_{\\text{dCFT}}$ is computed by an auxiliary Ryu-Takayanagi surface in an extra bulk dimension. Abstract: Entanglement islands are usually easiest to realize in doubly holographic models with massive gravitons or non-gravitating baths. In wedge holography, by contrast, Neumann boundary conditions on both branes give a normalizable massless graviton, but the island saddle of the purely geometric Ryu-Takayanagi problem collapses to the horizon. Negative Dvali-Gabadadze-Porrati (DGP) terms can restore nontrivial islands by changing the endpoint condition of the extremal surface, but this branch contains a massive ghost. We propose a different, manifestly healthy mechanism. We keep the wedge gravitational action free of DGP terms and add a unitary defect conformal field theory localized at the codimension-two corner. This sector is distinct from the standard corner CFT dual to the undeformed wedge, whose entropy is already represented by the wedge RT area; hence no double counting is involved. The entropy of this additional defect sector contributes to the generalized entropy. If the defect theory is holographic, this entropy is computed by an auxiliary bulk Ryu-Takayanagi surface. We show that when the wedge endpoint position determines the defect entangling region, the auxiliary area can have the opposite variation to the wedge area while all couplings and central charges remain positive. A local endpoint model exhibits an isolated stable saddle and its late-time dominance over the Hartman-Maldacena surface. The quantum extremality condition then replaces the pure orthogonality condition and allows a non-horizon island saddle in a long-range, massless, ghost-free gravitational theory. This demonstrates that the obstruction to massless islands in minimal wedge holography is not masslessness itself, but the absence of an additional healthy entropy term capable of balancing the horizon-minimizing area variation.

Result: For $α=1$, the flow reproduces the root-$T\bar T$/ModMax theory. For $α<1$, it yields new irrelevant deformations distinct from Born-Infeld-type $T\bar T$. For $α>1$, it provides the first explicit examples of relevant $T\bar T$-like Lagrangians. All cases admit exact solutions governed by a universal auxiliary equation.

当 $α=1$ 时，该流复现了 root-$T\bar T$/ModMax 理论；当 $α<1$ 时，得到不同于 Born-Infeld 型 $T\bar T$ 的新无关形变；当 $α>1$ 时，首次给出了显式的相关 $T\bar T$ 类拉格朗日量。所有情形均由一个普适的辅助方程控制，并具有精确解。

Abstract: We introduce a one-parameter root-$T\bar T$-like flow, $ \partial_λ\mathcal{L}=\mathcal{R}_λ^{1/α}$, which organizes stress-tensor deformations into irrelevant, marginal, and relevant branches. Within duality-invariant electrodynamics in four dimensions, and equivalently within two-dimensional integrable sigma models, the flow admits a closed-form solution controlled by an auxiliary equation. The marginal point $α=1$ reproduces the root-$T\bar T$ / ModMax branch, while $α<1$ gives irrelevant deformations distinct from the standard Born-Infeld $T\bar T$ flow. For $α>1$, the same construction yields explicit relevant $T\bar T$-like Lagrangians. These results suggest that root-$T\bar T$ flows provide a common organizing principle for duality-invariant and integrable deformations.

The framework naturally accommodates AdS$_2$/CFT$_1$ and AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$, but suggests AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ requires additional string-theoretic ingredients.

Motivation: The motivation is to understand holography—specifically AdS/CFT—not as an emergent duality from string theory alone, but as a consequence of interpreting gravity itself as arising from spontaneous breaking of conformal gauge symmetry. 动机在于将全息原理（特别是AdS/CFT）理解为并非仅源于弦论的对偶性，而是引力本身作为共形规范对称性自发破缺的结果。该视角旨在通过共形对称性破缺及其边界残余结构，为低维与高维全息对应提供统一的几何解释。

Method: The authors employ the gauge-theoretic formulation of gravity based on the conformal group $SO(4,2)$ (or its appropriate subgroup in lower dimensions). In AdS$_2$/CFT$_1$, they relate the boundary extrinsic curvature to the Schwarzian derivative via the projective structure of the Liouville mode. In AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$, they start from the four-dimensional conformal gravity action, show that Einstein--Hilbert gravity with cosmological constant emerges upon symmetry breaking, and identify the induced boundary gravitational Chern--Simons term. Variation of this term yields the Cotton tensor, which encodes the residual boundary conformal geometry. They contrast this with AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$, arguing it lies outside this 4D conformal gauge framework.
\n\n
作者采用基于共形群 $SO(4,2)$（或其在低维下的适当子群）的引力规范理论表述。在AdS$_2$/CFT$_1$中，他们通过Liouville模的射影结构将边界外曲率与Schwarzian导数联系起来。在AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$中，他们从四维共形引力作用量出发，展示在对称性破缺后如何导出带宇宙学常数的Einstein–Hilbert引力，并识别出诱导的边界引力Chern–Simons项；该项的变分给出Cotton张量，它编码了剩余的边界共形几何。作者进一步对比AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$，认为其超出此四维共形规范框架。

Result: Key results include: (1) Derivation of the Schwarzian derivative as the boundary manifestation of AdS$_2$ extrinsic curvature within the conformal gauge framework; (2) Clarification of the relation between bulk Liouville geometry and boundary projective structure; (3) Demonstration that in AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$, the Einstein--Hilbert action plus a total derivative emerges from broken conformal symmetry, inducing a boundary gravitational Chern--Simons term; (4) Identification of the Cotton tensor—via variation of the Chern--Simons term—as the fundamental boundary conformal invariant, analogous to the Schwarzian in 2D; (5) Argument that AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ does not fit naturally into this 4D conformal gauge picture, suggesting the need for intrinsically higher-dimensional or string-theoretic mechanisms.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 在共形规范框架下，将Schwarzian导数推导为AdS$_2$边界外曲率的表现；(2) 阐明体Liouville几何与边界射影结构之间的关系；(3) 在AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$中，展示破缺的共形对称性如何导出Einstein–Hilbert作用量加一个全导数项，并诱导边界引力Chern–Simons项；(4) 通过Chern–Simons项的变分识别出Cotton张量作为基本的边界共形不变量，其角色类似于二维中的Schwarzian导数；(5) 论证AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$无法自然纳入此四维共形规范图像，暗示需要本质上更高维或弦论机制。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that holography in AdS$_2$/CFT$_1$ and AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$ can be understood geometrically as arising from the boundary remnants of spontaneously broken conformal gauge symmetry in the bulk. The Schwarzian derivative and the Cotton tensor serve as universal signatures of this breaking in 2D and 4D respectively. However, AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ appears to lie beyond this paradigm, indicating that while conformal symmetry breaking provides a powerful organizing principle for certain holographic dualities, a complete understanding of higher-dimensional cases may require embedding into broader frameworks like string theory.
\n\n
文章结论认为，AdS$_2$/CFT$_1$和AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$中的全息原理可被几何地理解为体中共形规范对称性自发破缺后在边界留下的残余结构。Schwarzian导数和Cotton张量分别作为二维和四维中此类破缺的普适标志。然而，AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$似乎超出这一范式，表明尽管共形对称性破缺为某些全息对偶提供了强有力的组织原则，但要完整理解更高维情形，可能仍需嵌入弦论等更广框架。

Abstract: We discuss the AdS/CFT correspondence from the viewpoint of the gauge-theoretic formulation of gravity, in which gravity is interpreted as a broken phase of conformal gauge symmetry.
  In the AdS$_2$/CFT$_1$ case, we show that the Schwarzian derivative naturally emerges from the boundary extrinsic curvature of AdS$_2$ geometry. The relation between the bulk Liouville geometry and the boundary projective structure is clarified. We further discuss the distinction between the bulk conformal gauge algebra with vanishing central extension and the emergent boundary Virasoro structure with nonvanishing central charge.
  We then investigate the possible structure of the AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$ correspondence, which is directly related to the original four-dimensional formulation of gravity as a broken phase of conformal gauge symmetry. In this framework, the Einstein--Hilbert action with cosmological constant emerges together with a total derivative term. We argue that this structure induces the boundary gravitational Chern--Simons term, whose variation leads naturally to the Cotton tensor. The Cotton tensor is interpreted as the fundamental conformal invariant associated with the residual boundary conformal geometry, playing a role analogous to that of the Schwarzian derivative in AdS$_2$/CFT$_1$.
  We also discuss the qualitative difference between AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$ and AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$. These observations suggest a unified geometrical interpretation of holography in terms of boundary remnants of broken conformal gauge symmetry.

### [43] [One-loop divergences for KK theories on $\mathrm{AdS}\times S$ spaces; a reanalysis of $\mathrm{AdS}_4 \times S^7$ /ABJM precision holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01167)

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Computing quantum corrections to holographic partition functions requires handling UV divergences in curved product spaces like $\mathrm{AdS} \times S$. Existing methods struggle with the interplay between Kaluza-Klein (KK) mode sums and individual AdS loop determinants, especially when zero modes on the sphere introduce extra subtleties. A robust framework is needed to isolate logarithmic divergences systematically across arbitrary dimensions and field content.
\n\n
计算全息配分函数的量子修正需要处理如 $\mathrm{AdS} \times S$ 等弯曲积空间中的紫外发散。现有方法难以处理卡鲁扎-克莱因（Kaluza-Klein, KK）模求和与单个 AdS 单圈行列式之间的相互作用，特别是当球面上的零模引入额外复杂性时。因此，亟需一个稳健的框架，在任意维度和场内容下系统地分离出对数发散项。

Method: The authors expand higher-dimensional kinetic operators in spherical harmonics on $S^{d_S}$, decomposing the $(d_A + d_S)$-dimensional spectral problem into an infinite tower of $d_A$-dimensional AdS determinants labeled by KK level $\\ell$. Each determinant is evaluated via spectral $ζ$-function regularization: $\\log \\det(-\\nabla^2 + m^2) = -\\zeta'(0)$. The total one-loop effective action is then $\\Gamma^{(1)} = \\frac{1}{2} \\sum_{\\ell} d_{\\ell} \\log \\det_{\\mathrm{AdS}_{d_A}}(\\mathcal{O}_{\\ell})$, where $d_{\\ell}$ is the degeneracy of the $\\ell$-th spherical harmonic. Logarithmic divergences arise from the asymptotic behavior of $\\zeta(s)$ near $s=0$ combined with the large-$\\ell$ growth of $d_{\\ell}$. Zero modes ($\\ell=0$ for certain fields) are treated separately as they yield additional lower-dimensional determinants not captured by the generic KK sum.
\n\n
作者在 $S^{d_S}$ 上将高维动能算符按球谐函数展开，将 $(d_A + d_S)$ 维谱问题分解为由 KK 能级 $\\ell$ 标记的无穷多个 $d_A$ 维 AdS 行列式。每个行列式通过谱 $ζ$-函数正规化计算：$\\log \\det(-\\nabla^2 + m^2) = -\\zeta'(0)$。总单圈有效作用量为 $\\Gamma^{(1)} = \\frac{1}{2} \\sum_{\\ell} d_{\\ell} \\log \\det_{\\mathrm{AdS}_{d_A}}(\\mathcal{O}_{\\ell})$，其中 $d_{\\ell}$ 是第 $\\ell$ 阶球谐函数的简并度。对数发散源于 $\\zeta(s)$ 在 $s=0$ 附近的渐近行为与大 $\\ell$ 下 $d_{\\ell}$ 的增长相结合。零模（某些场对应的 $\\ell=0$ 模）被单独处理，因为它们会产生额外的低维行列式，无法被一般的 KK 求和所涵盖。

Result: The framework successfully isolates all logarithmic divergences in one-loop determinants on $\mathrm{AdS}_{d_A} \times S^{d_S}$. When applied to the full field content of 11D supergravity compactified on $\mathrm{AdS}_4 \times S^7$, only the ghost sector's 2-form field in AdS$_4$ yields a non-zero logarithmic divergence. This reproduces the known result $\\frac{1}{4} \\log N$ for the ABJM free energy correction, originally derived via supersymmetric localization and confirmed in arXiv:1210.6057.

Conclusion: The paper establishes a general and efficient spectral $ζ$-function-based method for computing logarithmic UV divergences in quantum gravity on $\mathrm{AdS} \times S$ backgrounds.
