Abstract: We develop the Boltzmann--Wasserstein (BW) distance, a temperature-dependent metric on the space of quantum theories defined as the optimal $W_2$ distance between Boltzmann-weighted energy spectra. For semiclassical theories differing by a small entropy shift, the normalised BW distance collapses to a squared horizon-area comparator, $\tilde{\mathcal{W}}^2 \approx (δA/4G)^2/8$, with the two areas evaluated at equal energy. When the Hamiltonians of the theories differ by an operator $V$, then the BW distance equals a time-averaged thermal two-point function of $V$; for primary perturbations where this vanishes by conformal invariance, a four-point representation appears at the next order. The Schwinger--Keldysh wormhole that determines $C_{\max}$ -- two Euclidean caps sharing a single horizon, joined by a Lorentzian segment that adiabatically interpolates between the two theories -- sees only the rearrangement of the spectrum. The perturbative two-point formula computes the genuinely quantum correction sensitive to the eigenvectors of the perturbation, invisible to the classical gravity saddle. The Lorentzian part of the wormhole is essential for the construction. We work out two examples -- two BTZ black holes with different cosmological constants and a $T\bar{T}$ deformation of BTZ.

Abstract: We study the pseudo-gauge transformation (PGT) freedom at freeze-out in relativistic spin hydrodynamics. Abstract: In connection with the question of confinement of massive ghost in quadratic gravity (QG), color confinement in quantum chromodynamics (QCD) has been reconsidered in the superfield formalism. It is also shown that the asymptotic field of the gluon field obeys the field equation for not the conventional massive Klein-Gordon field but the massive dipole field. In case of quark confinement, it is shown that the quark field satisfies the massive spinor dipole equation in the confinement phase, which might suggest a physical picture such that a pair of quark and anti-quark constitutes a bound state and is confined into a meson. These facts encourage us to conjecture that the similar phenomenon could take place in confinement of massive ghost, which violates the unitarity of the physical S-matrix, and might provide us with a resolution to the unitarity problem in QG.

作者采用在dyonic Kerr-Sen黑洞事件视界上正则的内向Eddington-Finkelstein坐标，从而可清晰施加纯内向边界条件。他们对标量场的Klein-Gordon方程进行变量分离，将其约化为一个径向常微分方程，并以合流Heun函数形式给出精确解。为确保物理解的合理性，他们在空间无穷远处施加平方可积性（正则性）条件，这导致Heun函数级数的多项式截断条件，并由此导出复准定态频率 $\\omega = \\omega_R + i \\omega_I$ 的精确代数量子化条件。

Electric and magnetic charges systematically shift the entire spectrum. Most significantly, all physical branches obey a universal rule: if $\\omega_R > 0$, then $\\omega_I > 0$ (exponential growth); if $\\omega_R < 0$, then $\\omega_I < 0$ (exponential decay). Purely imaginary modes ($\\omega_R = 0$) are non-propagating.

分析揭示了一个多分支的准定态谱：一类模式独立于黑洞自旋 $a$ 和dyonic电荷 $(Q_e, Q_m)$，另一类则显式依赖于这些参数。由于场的方位量子数 $m$ 与黑洞自旋 $a$ 的耦合，共转（$m>0$）与逆转（$m<0$）模式之间存在显著不对称性。电荷和磁荷对整个谱产生系统性频移。最关键的是，所有物理分支遵循普适规律：若 $\\omega_R > 0$，则 $\\omega_I > 0$（指数增长）；若 $\\omega_R < 0$，则 $\\omega_I < 0$（指数衰减）。纯虚模式（$\\omega_R = 0$）无传播性。

Conclusion: The exponential growth of positive-frequency modes in the region beyond the outer horizon—including the causally pathological interior containing closed timelike curves—provides strong evidence for a dynamical instability that enforces Hawking’s chronology protection conjecture. The absence of propagating modes along CTCs (due to either exponential blow-up or non-oscillatory pure imaginary behavior) suggests that semiclassical physics prevents the formation of macroscopic time machines. Thus, the dyonic Kerr-Sen geometry, despite permitting CTCs in its maximal analytic extension, is destabilized by massive scalar perturbations in a manner consistent with cosmic censorship and chronology protection.

外视界之外区域（包括含闭合类时曲线的因果病态内区）中正频率模式的指数增长，为支持Hawking时序保护猜想的动力学不稳定性提供了有力证据。沿CTC不存在传播模式（或因指数发散，或因纯虚模式无振荡），表明半经典物理阻止了宏观时间机器的形成。因此，尽管dyonic Kerr-Sen几何在其极大解析延拓中允许CTC存在，但大质量标量扰动会以符合宇宙监督和时序保护的方式使其失稳。

Abstract: We found sets of exact analytic quasi-stationary states of a massive scalar field in a dyonic Kerr-Sen black hole~(DKSBH) background in the maximally extended spacetime region. A central novelty is the use of horizon-regular ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates, which enables a direct and unambiguous imposition of the ingoing boundary condition at the horizon. The exact radial solutions are in the form of confluent Heun functions. Imposing regularity at spatial infinity enforces a series truncation condition, yielding an exact quantization of the quasi-stationary frequencies. The spectrum exhibits a rich multi-branch structure, which we show splits into two distinct classes: modes that are insensitive to the black hole spin and charges and modes that explicitly depend on them. We uncover a clear asymmetry between co-rotating and counter-rotating configurations, driven by the spin-angular momentum coupling, as well as a systematic shift of the spectrum induced by electric and magnetic charges. The physical branches exhibit a universal behavior: modes with positive real frequency possess positive imaginary parts and therefore grow exponentially in time, whereas modes with negative real frequency are damped and decay. This suggests that positive-energy excitations in the region behind the outer horizon including the inner region of the inner horizon which contains the closed-timelike-curve, exponentially destabilize the background spacetime, supporting Hawking's chronology protection conjecture. In addition, the purely imaginary modes contain no oscillatory component and hence do not propagate through the spacetime, preventing traveling excitations along closed timelike curves and remaining consistent with the conjecture.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The authors aim to clarify the inner workings of semidefinite programming when applied to lattice Hamiltonians: why it succeeds in certain regimes (e.g., at criticality or for free theories), how to extend it beyond ground state energy to excited states and correlators, and whether incorporating physical constraints—such as global charge conservation or structures from integrable models—can systematically improve its accuracy and efficiency.
\n\n
作者旨在阐明半定规划应用于格点哈密顿量时的内在机制：为何它在某些情形（如临界点或自由理论）下有效，如何将其从仅计算基态能量拓展至激发态和关联函数，并探究引入物理约束（如全局电荷守恒或可积模型结构）是否能系统性提升其精度与效率。

Method: The paper employs semidefinite programming with moment matrices built from local operators. Key innovations include: (i) imposing charge selection rules to isolate excited states with definite quantum numbers; (ii) incorporating approximate Virasoro symmetry constraints near criticality to extract conformal data like central charge $c$ and scaling dimensions $\Delta$; (iii) using known free-fermion solutions of the Ising model to benchmark SDP exactness; and (iv) testing additional constraints motivated by integrability in the Potts model. The SDP bounds are analyzed as functions of system size and constraint input.
\n\n
本文采用基于局域算符构造矩矩阵的半定规划方法。关键创新包括：(i) 引入电荷选择规则以分离具有确定量子数的激发态；(ii) 在临界点附近施加近似的Virasoro对称性约束，以提取中心荷 $c$ 和标度维数 $\Delta$ 等共形数据；(iii) 利用Ising模型的已知自由费米子解作为基准，验证SDP的精确性；(iv) 在Potts模型中测试受可积性启发的额外约束。作者将SDP给出的界作为系统尺寸和约束输入的函数进行分析。

Result: At criticality, SDP combined with approximate Virasoro constraints successfully estimates the central charge and critical exponents of charged operators, and helps locate phase transitions. For the free-fermion solvable Ising model, SDP exactly reproduces the ground state energy and fermionic two-point functions. In interacting (non-free) cases like the 3-state Potts model, SDP yields accurate ground state energies but only qualitative agreement for correlators. The method’s performance degrades with increasing lattice volume due to poor scaling of required input data.
\n\n
在临界点，结合近似Virasoro约束的SDP成功估算了中心荷和带电算符的临界指数，并有助于定位相变点。对于可解的自由费米子Ising模型，SDP精确复现了基态能量和费米子两点函数。在相互作用（非自由）情形如三态Potts模型中，SDP给出准确的基态能量，但关联函数仅达到定性吻合。由于所需输入数据随格点体积增长而急剧增加，该方法的性能随系统增大而显著下降。

Conclusion: Semidefinite programming is a powerful numerical bootstrap tool for 1D quantum systems, capable of exact results in free theories and useful approximations in interacting ones. However, its efficacy hinges on physically motivated constraints (e.g., charge conservation, Virasoro symmetry), and it faces intrinsic scalability challenges that limit its application to large lattices unless new structural insights or algorithmic improvements are introduced.
\n\n
半定规划是研究一维量子系统的有力数值Bootstrap工具，在自由理论中可得精确结果，在相互作用体系中也能提供有用近似。然而，其有效性依赖于物理动机明确的约束（如电荷守恒、Virasoro对称性），且面临固有的可扩展性挑战，若无新的结构洞见或算法改进，其在大尺度格点上的应用将受限。

Abstract: We study semidefinite programming (SDP) methods to analyze spin chain Hamiltonians. We examine the ground state energy, the first excited charged states and ground state correlators in two simple models: the Ising model in a transverse magnetic field and the closely related 3-state Potts model. Our goal is to understand precisely what the SDP program is doing and when it works well, why it does so. We focus on the following novel ingredients: using charge constraints to obtain excited states and to see if additional constraints from integrable models are effective at improving the method.
  At criticality we also explore to what extent we can use approximate Virasoro correlators to extract conformal data: the central charge and some critical exponents of charged states. We also use these to identify the location of the phase transition. In the special case where the system is made of free fermions we prove that the SDP finds the exact energy of the ground state and produces the correct two point functions of the fermions. Away from free fermion theories, the SDP gets progressively worse at estimating data beyond the value of the ground state energy (like correlation functions), although it qualitatively matches these. In order to be effective, the SDP seems to run into scaling issues where the amount of input needed scales poorly with the lattice volume.

</details>

*Luca V. Delacretaz*

Main category: hep-th

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fluctuating hydrodynamics universally governs late-time dynamics in thermalizing local many-body systems, yet a systematic, controlled theoretical framework to describe its emergence from microscopic physics has been historically challenging. Method: The paper employs modern effective field theory techniques rooted in the concept of "strong-to-weak" spontaneous symmetry breaking, where hydrodynamic modes arise as Goldstone-like degrees of freedom associated with broken time-translation and other symmetries in thermal states. The method systematically constructs Schwinger-Keldysh path integrals for open quantum systems, incorporating stochastic noise and dissipation consistent with microscopic unitarity and thermodynamic consistency. Examples include spin chains, relativistic QFTs, and theories with generalized global symmetries or 't Hooft anomalies. Transport coefficients are treated as Wilson coefficients in the EFT, subject to UV/IR matching conditions.
\n\n
本文采用基于“强到弱”自发对称性破缺概念的现代有效场论（EFT）技术，其中流体力学模作为与热态中时间平移及其他对称性破缺相关的Goldstone型自由度出现。该方法系统地构建开放量子系统的Schwinger-Keldysh路径积分，纳入与微观幺正性和热力学一致性兼容的随机噪声与耗散。示例包括自旋链、相对论性量子场论（QFT），以及具有广义全局对称性或't Hooft anomalies的理论。输运系数被视为EFT中的Wilson系数，并受紫外/红外匹配条件约束。

Result: The lectures demonstrate that fluctuating hydrodynamics can be rigorously derived as a universal low-energy EFT governed by symmetry and thermodynamic principles. The “strong-to-weak” symmetry breaking paradigm successfully accommodates diverse systems—from non-relativistic lattice models to relativistic QFTs—even in the presence of generalized symmetries or 't Hooft anomalies. Furthermore, the framework yields concrete UV/IR constraints on transport parameters (e.g., diffusion constants, viscosities), interpreted as renormalization-group flow of Wilson coefficients between microscopic and hydrodynamic scales.

讲座表明，涨落流体力学可被严格推导为由对称性和热力学原理支配的普适低能有效场论（EFT）。“强到弱”对称性破缺范式成功适用于从非相对论格点模型到相对论性量子场论（QFT）等多种系统，即使存在广义对称性或't Hooft anomalies亦成立。此外，该框架给出了对输运参数（如扩散常数、粘滞系数）的具体紫外/红外约束，这些参数被解释为Wilson系数在微观尺度与流体力学尺度之间的重整化群（renormalization group）流。

Conclusion: The modern EFT approach to fluctuating hydrodynamics, grounded in symmetry breaking and Schwinger-Keldysh formalism, provides a powerful and unified language for late-time dynamics in thermalizing quantum systems. 基于对称性破缺和Schwinger-Keldysh形式体系的现代涨落流体力学有效场论（EFT）方法，为热化量子系统中的晚期动力学提供了强大而统一的语言。它连接了凝聚态物理与高能物理，为计算输运性质提供了系统方法，并揭示了反常、对称性与非平衡物理中普适性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Fluctuating hydrodynamics emerges in essentially any local many-body system at nonzero temperature. Effective field theory (EFT) approaches enable the quantitative study of this emergence, providing a controlled framework to capture late-time observables. These lectures introduce the organizing principles behind equilibrium and out-of-equilibrium dynamics in these thermalizing systems. A central focus is the modern construction of these EFTs, which frames fluctuating hydrodynamics through the lens of "strong-to-weak" spontaneous symmetry breaking. Drawing examples from both high-energy and condensed matter physics, we show how this paradigm adapts to systems ranging from spin chains to relativistic quantum field theories, including models with generalized symmetries or symmetries with 't Hooft anomalies. Finally, we discuss UV/IR constraints on transport parameters -- viewed as the Wilson coefficients of hydrodynamic EFTs -- both in continuum and on the lattice.

</details>


### [51] [Cumulant dynamics in finite-memory diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02446)
*Navid Abbasi,Xin An,Shanjin Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper investigates how memory effects in charge diffusion—modeled via Maxwell–Cattaneo theory rather than standard Fickian diffusion—influence the evolution of conserved-charge fluctuation correlators in the quark–gluon plasma (QGP). These memory effects modify freezeout cumulants, particularly higher-order ones, altering their magnitude, position, and shape compared to both equilibrium expectations and Fickian predictions, which is crucial for interpreting QCD critical point signatures.
\n\n
本文研究了电荷扩散中的记忆效应——通过Maxwell–Cattaneo理论而非标准Fickian扩散建模——如何影响夸克–胶子等离子体（QGP）中守恒荷涨落关联函数的演化。这些记忆效应会改变冻结（freezeout）时刻的累积量，尤其是高阶累积量，使其相对于平衡值和Fickian预测在幅度、位置和形状上发生改变，这对解读QCD临界点信号至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The search for the QCD critical point relies on non-monotonic behavior in conserved-charge cumulants measured in heavy-ion collisions. However, the finite lifetime of the QGP fireball means these observables do not reflect instantaneous equilibrium values. Standard modeling uses Fickian diffusion, which assumes an instantaneous diffusive current response to density gradients. This neglects possible delayed-response (memory) effects when the current-relaxation time $\\tau_J$ is comparable to fluctuation-mode relaxation times, potentially distorting critical-point signals.
\n\n
寻找QCD临界点依赖于重离子碰撞中测量到的守恒荷累积量的非单调行为。然而，QGP火球的有限寿命意味着这些观测量并不反映瞬时平衡值。标准模型采用Fickian扩散，假设扩散流对密度梯度的响应是瞬时的。当电流弛豫时间$\\tau_J$与涨落模的弛豫时间相当时，这种处理忽略了可能的延迟响应（记忆）效应，从而可能扭曲临界点信号。

Method: The authors generalize the diffusion equation from Fick’s law to the Maxwell–Cattaneo form: $\\tau_J \\partial_t \\mathbf{J} + \\mathbf{J} = -D \\nabla n$, where $\\mathbf{J}$ is the diffusive current, $D$ the diffusion coefficient, and $\\tau_J$ the current-relaxation time. They derive closed evolution equations for multi-point Wigner functions describing charge fluctuations, incorporating this finite-memory dynamics. These are solved along representative trajectories in the QCD phase diagram, and the resulting freezeout spatial correlators are converted into experimentally relevant acceptance-dependent cumulants.
\n\n
作者将扩散方程从Fick定律推广到Maxwell–Cattaneo形式：$\\tau_J \\partial_t \\mathbf{J} + \\mathbf{J} = -D \\nabla n$，其中$\\mathbf{J}$为扩散流，$D$为扩散系数，$\\tau_J$为电流弛豫时间。他们推导了描述电荷涨落的多点Wigner函数的闭合演化方程，并纳入这种有限记忆动力学。沿QCD相图中的代表性轨迹求解这些方程，并将所得的冻结空间关联函数转换为实验相关的接受度依赖累积量。

Result: Compared to Fickian diffusion, Maxwell–Cattaneo diffusion introduces an additional memory-induced lag beyond the standard diffusive delay. This leads to significant modifications in the non-monotonic structures of cumulants (e.g., kurtosis $\\kappa\\sigma^2$) near the critical point: suppression of peak amplitude, shifting of peak location in collision energy or temperature, and reshaping of the signal profile. These effects are most pronounced in higher-order cumulants and their ratios, which are key experimental observables.
\n\n
与Fickian扩散相比，Maxwell–Cattaneo扩散引入了超出标准扩散延迟之外的额外记忆诱导滞后。这导致临界点附近累积量（如峰度$\\kappa\\sigma^2$）的非单调结构发生显著改变：峰值幅度被抑制、峰值位置在碰撞能量或温度上发生偏移、信号轮廓被重塑。这些效应在高阶累积量及其比值中最为显著，而这些正是关键的实验观测量。

Conclusion: Memory effects in charge diffusion, captured by Maxwell–Cattaneo theory, can substantially alter the interpretation of fluctuation observables as signatures of the QCD critical point. Ignoring current relaxation may lead to misidentification or underestimation of critical behavior. Therefore, a consistent dynamical framework including finite relaxation times is essential for reliable extraction of critical-point signals from heavy-ion collision data.
\n\n
由Maxwell–Cattaneo理论所刻画的电荷扩散中的记忆效应，会显著改变将涨落观测量作为QCD临界点信号的解读。忽略电流弛豫可能导致对临界行为的误判或低估。因此，包含有限弛豫时间的一致动力学框架对于从重离子碰撞数据中可靠提取临界点信号至关重要。

Abstract: Fluctuations of conserved charges are among the main proposed signatures of the quantum chromodynamics (QCD) critical point, but their interpretation requires a dynamical description of how fluctuation correlators evolve during the finite lifetime of the quark--gluon plasma (QGP) fireball. The standard baseline for this evolution is Fickian diffusion, in which the diffusive current follows the local density gradient instantaneously. This instantaneous-current limit can miss delayed-response effects when the current-relaxation time becomes comparable to the relaxation time of the relevant fluctuation modes. In this work we extend this baseline to Maxwell--Cattaneo diffusion, where the current relaxes on a finite time scale and therefore retains memory. We derive closed evolution equations for multi-point Wigner functions and convert the freezeout correlators into acceptance-dependent cumulants along representative trajectories in the QCD phase diagram. While Fickian diffusion already causes the correlators to lag behind their instantaneous equilibrium values, finite current relaxation introduces an additional memory effect beyond this diffusive lag. As a result, current memory can suppress, shift, and reshape the non-monotonic behavior of the cumulants relative to both instantaneous equilibrium and Fickian diffusion, with the most visible effects appearing in higher-order cumulants and their ratios.

Abstract: We study the interaction between spontaneous symmetry breaking and phase separation dynamics in holography. Using a double-quench protocol, the system first rapidly crosses the critical point and generates topological defects, while a second quench drives the system into a nonlinear unstable regime with spinodal decomposition. We investigate both $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and $U(1)$ symmetric systems, where different types of topological defects emerge during symmetry breaking. We show that topological defects dynamically determine the nucleation sites of phase separation. As the instability grows, the defect cores expand into macroscopic phase-separated domains. Despite the distinct symmetries and topological properties of these defects, both systems exhibit the same universal dynamical behavior, indicating that topological defects can universally serve as dynamical seeds for subsequent phase separation.

### [53] [21cmEMUv3: a hybrid diffusion-LSTM emulator of 21cmFAST summary observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00219)
*Daniela Breitman,Andrei Mesinger,Steven G. Murray,Ivan Nikolic,Roberto Trotta*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper introduces 21cmEMUv3, a machine learning emulator trained on 21cmFASTv3 simulations that models both atomically and molecularly cooling galaxies. It emulates seven key observables—including the cylindrical and spherically-averaged 21cm power spectra—with sub-percent accuracy using score-based diffusion (for the cylindrical PS) and LSTM networks (for other summaries). The emulator is applied to reinterpret HERA upper limits and forecast SKA detection prospects, yielding constraints on early X-ray sources consistent with low-metallicity high-mass X-ray binaries.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rapid growth of observational data from the cosmic dawn (CD) and epoch of reionisation (EoR) necessitates fast, accurate, and flexible theoretical frameworks for inference. Traditional semi-numerical simulations like 21cmFAST are computationally expensive, limiting their use in Bayesian parameter estimation. Machine learning emulators offer a solution by approximating simulation outputs at a fraction of the computational cost, enabling robust statistical analysis of current and future 21cm datasets.
\n\n
随着宇宙黎明（Cosmic Dawn, CD）和再电离时期（Epoch of Reionisation, EoR）观测数据的快速增长，迫切需要快速、精确且灵活的理论解释框架。传统的半数值模拟（如21cmFAST）计算成本高昂，限制了其在贝叶斯参数估计中的应用。机器学习emulator通过以极低计算代价近似模拟输出，为当前及未来21cm数据集的稳健统计分析提供了可行方案。

Method: The authors construct 21cmEMUv3, an emulator conditioned on cosmological parameter $σ_8$ and ten astrophysical parameters. It uses two distinct neural architectures: (i) a score-based diffusion model to emulate the high-resolution cylindrical 21cm power spectrum across redshifts $z \sim 6$–30; and (ii) long-short term memory (LSTM) networks for the remaining six summary statistics—spherically-averaged 21cm PS, mean neutral fraction, mean spin temperature, global 21cm signal, UV luminosity functions, and Thomson optical depth. The training data comes from 21cmFASTv3 simulations that include both atomically and molecularly cooling halos. The emulator achieves sub-percent median accuracy across all outputs.
\n\n
作者构建了21cmEMUv3 emulator，其输入条件为宇宙学参数$σ_8$和十个天体物理参数。该emulator采用两种不同的神经网络架构：(i) 使用score-based diffusion模型模拟红移范围$z \sim 6$–30内高分辨率的柱对称21cm功率谱；(ii) 使用长短期记忆（LSTM）网络模拟其余六个统计量——球对称平均21cm功率谱、IGM平均中性分数、平均自旋温度、全局21cm信号、紫外（UV）光度函数和Thomson散射光学深度。训练数据来自包含原子冷却和分子冷却晕的21cmFASTv3模拟，所有输出均达到亚百分比中位精度。

Result: Applying 21cmEMUv3 to HERA 21cm power spectrum upper limits, the authors derive constraints on the soft-band X-ray luminosity per unit star formation rate ($L_X$/SFR) in early galaxies. They exclude values below $10^{39.2}~\mathrm{erg~s^{-1}~M_\odot^{-1}~yr}$ at 95% confidence, consistent with extrapolations of high-mass X-ray binaries (HMXBs) to low-metallicity environments. Additionally, they produce detailed forecasts for 21cm power spectrum detection with the Square Kilometre Array (SKA) under various array configurations, demonstrating the emulator’s utility for experimental design.
\n\n
作者将21cmEMUv3应用于HERA的21cm功率谱上限数据，对早期星系中软能段X射线光度与恒星形成率之比（$L_X$/SFR）施加了限制：在95%置信水平下排除低于$10^{39.2}~\mathrm{erg~s^{-1}~M_\odot^{-1}~yr}$的值，该结果与将高光度X射线双星（HMXBs）外推至低金属丰度环境的预期一致。此外，他们还针对不同阵列构型下的平方公里阵列（Square Kilometre Array, SKA）给出了21cm功率谱探测的详细预测，展示了该emulator在实验设计中的实用性。

Conclusion: 21cmEMUv3 provides a fast, accurate, and publicly available emulation framework for interpreting 21cm cosmology observations during the CD and EoR. By combining state-of-the-art simulations with modern ML techniques—specifically score-based diffusion and LSTMs—it enables efficient Bayesian inference and forecasting. Its application demonstrates that early X-ray heating was likely driven by populations consistent with low-metallicity HMXBs, and it paves the way for optimal use of upcoming SKA data.
\n\n
21cmEMUv3提供了一个快速、精确且公开可用的emulation框架，用于解释宇宙黎明和再电离时期的21cm宇宙学观测。通过结合前沿模拟与现代机器学习技术（特别是score-based diffusion和LSTM），该框架实现了高效的贝叶斯推断与预测。其应用表明，早期X射线加热很可能由与低金属丰度HMXBs一致的天体驱动，并为未来SKA数据的最优利用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We are witnessing a surge in observations of the cosmic dawn (CD) and epoch of reionisation (EoR), driving an increasing demand for fast and robust theoretical interpretation frameworks. In response, machine learning (ML), and emulation in particular, has emerged as a powerful approach to accelerate and enhance inference pipelines. In this work, we present 21cmEMUv3, an emulator trained on 21cmFASTv3 simulations that model both atomically and molecularly cooling galaxies. 21cmEMUv3 is conditioned on $σ_8$ and ten astrophysical parameters to produce seven summary observables: (i) the cylindrical 21cm power spectrum (PS), emulated for the first time at such high resolution and accuracy across a wide redshift range of $z \sim$ 6--30; (ii) the spherically-averaged 21cm PS; (iii) the mean neutral fraction of the intergalactic medium (IGM); (iv) the mean 21cm spin temperature; (v) the global 21cm signal; (vi) the ultraviolet (UV) luminosity functions (LFs); and (vii) the Thomson scattering optical depth. Notably, the cylindrical 21cm PS is emulated via score-based diffusion, while the remaining six summaries are emulated via long-short term memory (LSTM) networks, all achieving sub-percent median accuracy. We use the emulator to reinterpret current 21cm PS upper limits from HERA, for the first time using state-of-the-art hydrodynamical simulations to inform priors on star formation inside molecularly cooling galaxies. We find that our inferred soft-band X-ray luminosity per unit star formation rate is consistent with extrapolations of high-mass X-ray binaries to the low-metallicity regimes expected in the first galaxies, excluding values below $10^{39.2}$ erg s$^{-1}M^{-1}_\odot \rm{yr}$ at $95\%$ confidence. Finally, we produce forecasts for the detection of the cosmic 21cm PS with the Square Kilometre Array for different array configurations. The 21cmEMU package is publicly available.

Method: The authors construct a joint parameterization of dark energy involving its equation of state $w(a)$ and effective sound speed $c_s^2(k,a)$, embedded within both GR and generic modified gravity frameworks. Abstract: Different candidate models are able to reproduce the dynamical dark energy signal preferred by combinations of recent distance measurements. Abstract: Large-scale structure measurements have revealed persistent tensions between early- and late-time cosmological probes, most notably the long-standing discrepancy in the structure-growth parameter $S_8$. Conclusion: At current levels of GRB systematics and with the two specific interaction forms tested (IDE-$ρ_{\rm de}$ and IDE-$ρ_{\rm c}$), there is no evidence from combined GRB and Pantheon+ data favoring interacting dark energy over $Λ$CDM. Abstract: The standard $Λ$CDM model faces long-standing theoretical and observational problems, such as the Hubble tension, which motivate extensions beyond $Λ$CDM, including interacting dark energy (IDE). Type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia) are precise probes of the late-time expansion history, while gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) can extend distance measurements to higher redshifts. However, GRB cosmology depends on the calibration of luminosity relations, the covariance treatment, and the intrinsic scatter. In this work, we use 15 years of Fermi/GBM long-GRB observations and Pantheon+ SNe Ia to test whether current distance data provide evidence in favor of IDE models over $Λ$CDM. The GRB covariance is constructed by propagating the Amati-relation calibration covariance, and the GRB intrinsic scatter is sampled as a nuisance parameter. A diagonal GRB covariance is also considered as a robustness test. With the full GRB covariance, both the GOLD and FULL samples give $H_0\simeq 72.8~{\rm km~s^{-1}~Mpc^{-1}}$ in $Λ$CDM. The IDE models do not improve the fit enough to compensate for their extra parameters, and the BIC favors the simpler $Λ$CDM model. The diagonal-covariance test gives the same model-selection conclusion, although it changes the fitted GRB intrinsic scatter. We conclude that, for the two interaction forms considered here and at the present level of GRB systematics, current GRB and Pantheon+ data do not provide evidence for interacting dark energy. Result: The measured peculiar velocity is $|v| = 443.8 \pm 204.1$ km/s toward $(l, b) = (107.4^\circ \pm 86.8^\circ, 28.4^\circ \pm 45.2^\circ)$. Conclusion: Using the largest high-redshift quasar sample to date, the study finds no statistically significant evidence against the cosmological principle.   <summary>Details</summary>

动机源于PDF-SYM方法的计算低效性——该方法是一种先进的剪切估计器，可在不引入系统误差的前提下最小化统计误差，但其迭代性质在处理未来星系巡天产生的海量数据时变得难以承受。因此亟需一种更快且统计性能相当的剪切估计方法，并能基于真实观测数据可靠地校准其偏差。

Method: The authors propose the Self-Weighted Shear Estimation (SWSE) method, which uses a self-weighting scheme to suppress shape noise and balance contributions from bright and faint galaxies. Because SWSE is intrinsically biased, they employ the field-distortion (FD) test—a data-driven calibration technique—to recover and correct its multiplicative ($m$) and additive ($c$) shear biases. Validation is performed using both simulated data and real survey data in the context of galaxy-galaxy lensing.

Result: The FD test successfully calibrates the intrinsic biases of SWSE, enabling it to achieve statistical precision comparable to the PDF-SYM method. This is demonstrated through both simulated and real survey data analyses in galaxy-galaxy lensing measurements, confirming that SWSE+FD yields unbiased and high-precision shear estimates suitable for large-scale applications.

Conclusion: The combination of the Self-Weighted Shear Estimation (SWSE) method and the field-distortion (FD) test provides a computationally efficient and statistically precise shear estimation pipeline that is robust against intrinsic biases. Abstract: Accurate cosmic shear measurement is the key to fully realize the scientific potential of large scale galaxy surveys. The Fourier\_Quad shear measurement method has been significantly developed to avoid biases caused by various factors, including the point spread function (PSF), photon noise, pixelation effect, selection effects, etc.. Shear statistics has also been optimized by using the PDF-SYM method (symmetrization of the Probability Distribution Function of the shear estimators) to achieve the minimal statistical error, without introducing systematic error. Nevertheless, the iterative nature of the PDF-SYM method makes it computationally expensive for massive datasets. To substantially improve efficiency, we introduce the Self-Weighted Shear Estimation (SWSE) method, which employs a specific self-weighting scheme to naturally suppress the shape noise as well as to balance the contributions from the bright and faint galaxies. Because SWSE is intrinsically biased, the primary focus of this work is to robustly evaluate whether the field-distortion test (FD test, a way to calibrate the shear bias on real data) can accurately recover and correct these inherent multiplicative and additive biases. Using both simulated and real survey data, we demonstrate that the FD test can successfully calibrate SWSE on galaxy-galaxy lensing measurements, enabling it to achieve statistical precision comparable to PDF-SYM. Our results establish the combination of FD test and SWSE as a robust shear estimation approach for forthcoming large-scale weak lensing surveys.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard 3D dark matter reconstruction from weak lensing is severely ill-posed due to single-line-of-sight observations, noisy galaxy shape measurements, and uncertain photometric redshifts. Traditional methods rely on handcrafted priors or approximate Bayesian techniques that fail to model the complex, non-Gaussian filamentary structure of the cosmic web. Recent high-resolution N-body simulations offer a more accurate representation of nonlinear structure formation, motivating a shift toward simulation-based, data-driven priors.

从弱引力透镜进行标准的三维暗物质重建因单视线观测、星系形状测量噪声以及光度红移不确定性而高度不适定。传统方法依赖手工设计的先验或近似贝叶斯技术，难以刻画宇宙网复杂且非高斯的纤维状结构。近期高分辨率 N-body 模拟能更准确地描述非线性结构形成，促使研究转向基于模拟的数据驱动先验。

Method: The authors construct a new simulation-based dataset, $\texttt{Conicus3D}$, derived from high-resolution cosmological simulations that pair 3D dark matter density fields with corresponding weak-lensing observables (convergence maps and photometric redshift distributions). They train a 3D diffusion model on this dataset to learn a prior over the full spatiotemporal distribution of dark matter. Using a plug-and-play framework, they combine this learned prior with a differentiable forward model of weak lensing (including noise and redshift uncertainty) within a diffusion-based posterior sampling scheme. This enables generation of full Bayesian posterior samples consistent with both observations and the simulation-derived prior.

作者构建了一个新的基于模拟的数据集 $\texttt{Conicus3D}$，该数据集源自高分辨率宇宙学模拟，将三维暗物质密度场与其对应的弱透镜观测量（收敛图和光度红移分布）配对。他们在该数据集上训练一个三维扩散模型，以学习暗物质在时空全域上的先验分布。通过即插即用（plug-and-play）框架，他们将这一学习到的先验与一个可微的弱透镜前向模型（包含噪声和红移不确定性）结合，嵌入到基于扩散的后验采样方案中，从而生成与观测数据和模拟先验一致的完整贝叶斯后验样本。

Conclusion: By integrating high-fidelity cosmological simulations into a diffusion-based Bayesian framework, this work provides a powerful new approach to 3D dark matter reconstruction that captures the non-Gaussian structure of the cosmic web while quantifying uncertainty. Abstract: Reconstructing the three-dimensional distribution of dark matter from weak-lensing observations is a central but highly ill-posed inverse problem in cosmology. Unlike standard 3D reconstruction with multiple viewpoints, we observe the universe from a single line of sight, through noisy shape distortions of galaxies with uncertain distances, so meaningful recovery of the 3D matter field requires strong prior assumptions. Existing methods either produce point estimates with handcrafted priors or use neural ensembles for approximate Bayesian uncertainty, and struggle to capture the non-Gaussian, filamentary structure of the cosmic web. With the advent of new high-resolution cosmological simulations, we now have an alternative source of prior knowledge that captures the nonlinear statistics of structure formation with far greater fidelity than analytic prescriptions. We leverage these simulations to build a new dataset $\texttt{Conicus3D}$, which enables us to learn a data-driven diffusion-model prior capturing the full 3D distribution of dark matter structure across cosmic time.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The stochastic bubble nucleation during cosmological first-order phase transitions leads to variations in the phase transition initiation times across different Hubble volumes, thereby generating non-Gaussian density perturbations in regions with delayed transitions. ### [63] [CSST large-scale structure analysis pipeline: IV. Cosmic Voids Identified from Galaxy Group Samples as Probes of the Large-scale Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01907)
*Yingxiao Song,Xiaohu Yang,Yan Gong,Yizhou Gu,Qingyang Li,Hong Guo,Yunkun Han,Yipeng Jing,Cheng Li,Feng Shi,Jipeng Sui,Run Wen,Hu Zhan,Pengjie Zhang,Youcai Zhang,Gong-Bo Zhao,Xian Zhong Zheng,Xingchen Zhou,Hu Zou*

TL;DR: This paper proposes using voids identified in galaxy group catalogs (group-voids) as a robust and theoretically simpler probe of large-scale structure (LSS). Using mock CSST surveys with varying spectroscopic completeness (100% and ~30%), the authors show that group-void statistics—specifically the void size function (VSF) and void density profile—closely match those of halo-voids when redshift completeness is ≥40%. Even at 30% completeness, agreement can be recovered by including a redshift error term, demonstrating group-voids' viability for LSS studies and emulator-based analyses.

本文提出利用星系群星表中识别的空洞（group-voids）作为大尺度结构（LSS）的一种稳健且理论更简化的探针。作者利用具有不同光谱红移完备度（100% 和约 30%）的 CSST 模拟巡天数据，表明当红移完备度 ≥40% 时，group-voids 的统计量——特别是空洞尺寸函数（void size function, VSF）和空洞密度轮廓——与暗晕空洞（halo-voids）高度一致。即使在 30% 完备度下，通过引入红移误差项仍可恢复一致性，证明 group-voids 在 LSS 研究及基于模拟器（emulator-based）的方法中具有重要应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard void-finding algorithms applied to individual galaxies suffer from shot noise, galaxy bias, and complex observational systematics, making theoretical modeling challenging. Since galaxy groups trace dark matter halos more directly and reliably than individual galaxies, voids identified in group catalogs (group-voids) offer a cleaner connection to the underlying dark matter distribution, simplifying both analytical modeling and comparison with N-body simulations. This motivates the use of group-voids as a complementary probe of cosmic large-scale structure.
\n\n
应用于单个星系的标准空洞搜寻算法受散粒噪声、星系偏置和复杂观测系统误差的影响，导致理论建模困难。由于星系群比单个星系更直接、可靠地示踪暗物质晕，因此在星系群星表中识别的空洞（group-voids）能更清晰地关联底层暗物质分布，从而简化解析建模并与 N-body 模拟进行比较。这促使作者将 group-voids 作为宇宙大尺度结构的一种补充探针。

Method: The authors construct two mock galaxy group catalogs from the CSST reference Mock Galaxy Redshift Survey (MGRS): one with 100% spectroscopic redshift completeness (ideal case) and another with ~30% completeness (conservative case). Groups are assumed to correspond to halos, with the Brightest Central Galaxy (BCG) used as the group center to improve void centering accuracy. Voids are identified in both group catalogs and the underlying halo catalog using standard void-finding algorithms. They measure two statistics—the void size function (VSF) and the void density profile—across five redshift bins from $z=0$ to $z=1.0$. Comparisons between group-voids and halo-voids are performed, and a redshift error term is introduced to model incompleteness effects in the low-completeness scenario.
\n\n
作者基于 CSST 参考模拟星系红移巡天（Mock Galaxy Redshift Survey, MGRS）构建了两个模拟星系群星表：一个具有 100% 光谱红移完备度（理想情形），另一个约为 30%（保守情形）。假设星系群对应于暗晕，并采用最亮中心星系（Brightest Central Galaxy, BCG）作为群中心以提高空洞中心定位精度。使用标准空洞搜寻算法在星系群星表和底层暗晕星表中识别空洞，并在 $z=0$ 到 $z=1.0$ 的五个红移区间内测量两种统计量：空洞尺寸函数（VSF）和空洞密度轮廓。通过引入红移误差项来建模低完备度情形下的不完整性效应，并对 group-voids 与 halo-voids 进行比较。

Result: Group-void statistics (VSF and density profile) closely match those of halo-voids when the spectroscopic redshift completeness is ≥40%, indicating that group-voids faithfully trace the underlying dark matter void population. Even at 30% completeness, the discrepancy can be mitigated by incorporating a redshift error term into the modeling, enabling reliable inference of halo-void properties from observed group-voids. The use of BCGs as group centers significantly improves void centering accuracy, enhancing the robustness of the statistics.

当光谱红移完备度 ≥40% 时，group-voids 的统计量（VSF 和密度轮廓）与 halo-voids 高度一致，表明 group-voids 能忠实反映底层暗物质空洞分布。即使在 30% 完备度下，通过在模型中引入红移误差项也可有效缓解偏差，从而从观测到的 group-voids 中可靠推断 halo-voids 的性质。使用 BCG 作为群中心显著提高了空洞中心定位精度，增强了统计量的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Group-voids provide a theoretically cleaner and observationally viable alternative to traditional galaxy-based voids for probing large-scale structure. Their strong correspondence with halo-voids—even under moderate spectroscopic incompleteness—makes them particularly suitable for precision cosmology and emulator-based inference frameworks (e.g., for EFTofLSS or Cosmological Bootstrap applications), where accurate mapping between observables and dark matter structure is essential.
\n\n
Group-voids 为探测大尺度结构提供了一种理论更简洁、观测上可行的传统基于星系的空洞替代方案。即使在中等光谱不完备情况下，其与 halo-voids 的强对应关系使其特别适用于精确宇宙学和基于模拟器的推断框架（例如用于 EFTofLSS 或 Cosmological Bootstrap 应用），这些场景中观测量与暗物质结构之间的精确映射至关重要。

Abstract: Because groups are directly associated with halos, they allow for considerably simpler theoretical modeling than approaches based on individual galaxies. We therefore propose to use voids identified in galaxy group catalogs, referred to as group-voids, to investigate the cosmic large-scale structure (LSS). Using the reference mock galaxy redshift survey (MGRS) designed for the Chinese Space-station Survey Telescope (CSST), we build two galaxy group catalogs representing ideal and conservative scenarios, derived from galaxy samples with 100\% and roughly 30\% spectroscopic redshift completeness, respectively. We then identify voids in these two mock group catalogs, as well as in the underlying halo catalog, and measure two void statistics, the void size function (VSF) and the void density profile, within five redshift intervals spanning $z=0$ to $1.0$. We compare the statistics obtained from two kinds of voids: those defined by galaxy groups (group-voids) and those defined by dark matter halos (halo-voids). In the void-finding process, we adopt the brightest central galaxy (BCG) as the group center to improve the accuracy of the inferred void centers. Our analysis shows that void statistics derived from group-voids with spectroscopic redshift completeness of at least 40\% can faithfully reproduce the corresponding statistics from halo-voids. Even when the redshift completeness of galaxies falls to as low as 30\%, we can still reliably describe group-voids via halo-voids by incorporating a redshift error term. This indicates that group-voids are a promising tool for probing LSS and offer a valuable complement to standard void studies, which is especially advantageous for emulator-based methods.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from tensions in cosmological parameters—particularly the Hubble tension—and anomalous observations of high-redshift galaxies that challenge the standard flat $Λ$CDM model. The authors seek to explore whether an operationally defined redshift duality, incorporating non-metric quantum effects along the photon path, can reconcile local and early-Universe measurements while preserving consistency with the Planck-anchored baseline.

动机源于宇宙学参数（尤其是哈勃张力）的不一致性以及对标准平坦$Λ$CDM模型构成挑战的高红移星系异常观测。作者试图探究一种操作性定义的红移对偶——即在光子传播路径上引入非度规的量子效应——是否能够在保持与Planck锚定基线一致的同时，调和局域与早期宇宙的测量结果。

Method: The authors construct a hybrid redshift model: $z_{\text{obs}} = z_{\Lambda} + z_{\text{quantum}}$, where $z_{\Lambda}$ is the standard FLRW redshift due to metric expansion and $z_{\text{quantum}}$ is a cumulative line-of-sight contribution from photon energy conversion into effective mass, dependent on path length and frequency. They fit this model to the Pantheon+SH0ES Type Ia supernova compilation using likelihood analysis, perform redshift-binned inference of the Hubble parameter $H_Λ(z)$, and re-evaluate derived cosmological quantities under the new redshift interpretation.
\n\n
作者构建了一个混合红移模型：$z_{\text{obs}} = z_{\Lambda} + z_{\text{quantum}}$，其中$z_{\Lambda}$是由于度规膨胀引起的标准FLRW红移，而$z_{\text{quantum}}$是光子能量转化为有效质量所导致的累积视线方向贡献，依赖于路径长度和频率。他们使用似然分析将该模型拟合到Pantheon+SH0ES Ia型超新星数据集，进行哈勃参数$H_Λ(z)$的红移分箱推断，并在此新红移解释下重新评估导出的宇宙学物理量。

Result: Fitting the hybrid model yields a metric-expansion Hubble constant $H_Λ = 67.4 \pm \Delta H$ consistent with Planck at $\lesssim 0.33σ$. Redshift-binned analyses reveal that while standard $Λ$CDM shows apparent evolution in $H(z)$, the hybrid model restores constancy of $H_Λ$ across bins. Re-inferred cosmological quantities (e.g., luminosity distances, ages) show trends that may resolve tensions with observations of unexpectedly mature high-redshift galaxies.
\n\n
拟合混合模型得到的度规膨胀哈勃常数$H_Λ = 67.4 \pm \Delta H$在$\lesssim 0.33σ$内与Planck一致。红移分箱分析表明，尽管标准$Λ$CDM模型显示出$H(z)$的明显演化，但混合模型在各红移区间恢复了$H_Λ$的恒定性。重新推断的宇宙学物理量（如光度距离、宇宙年龄）呈现出可能解决“意外成熟”高红移星系观测张力的趋势。

Conclusion: The redshift duality framework offers a phenomenologically viable extension to standard cosmology that reconciles late-time supernova data with early-Universe Planck constraints without modifying the underlying flat $Λ$CDM geometry. It suggests that part of the observed redshift may have a non-metric quantum origin, warranting inclusion in future observational pipelines and theoretical modeling.
\n\n
红移对偶框架为标准宇宙学提供了一个现象学上可行的扩展，在不修改底层平坦$Λ$CDM几何的前提下，协调了晚期超新星数据与早期宇宙Planck约束。它表明观测红移的一部分可能具有非度规的量子起源，值得纳入未来的观测处理流程和理论建模中。

Abstract: We test an operationally defined redshift duality in which the observed redshift comprises the standard metric-expansion component together with an additional line-of-sight quantum contribution arising from the cumulative conversion of photon energy into effective mass as a function of path length and frequency. Fitting this hybrid model to the Pantheon+SH0ES compilation, we find that the metric-expansion Hubble constant, $H_Λ$, is recovered to a value consistent with the Planck baseline of $67.4~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ within $\lesssim 0.33σ$. Redshift-binned analyses show that while the flat Lambda cold dark matter ($Λ$CDM) model produces an apparent drift in the inferred Hubble parameter across the Hubble flow, the hybrid model restores the constancy of $H_Λ$ across redshift bins. The correctional trends of cosmological physical quantities re-inferred under this framework further indicate the potential to alleviate anomalies associated with high-redshift galaxies. These results suggest that redshift duality warrants further consideration in observational processing and inference, while preserving consistency with a Planck-anchored flat $Λ$CDM baseline.

本文提出，由暗物质湮灭供能的超大质量暗星（Supermassive Dark Stars, SMDSs）为形成准星（quasi-stars）提供了一条自然路径，可解释JWST观测到的“小红点”（Little Red Dots, LRDs），且无需传统超大质量星（SMSs）模型所需的精细调谐条件。

  <summary>Details</summary>

JWST数据中观测到的“小红点”（LRDs）对现有天体物理模型构成挑战。传统的通过超大质量星（SMSs）形成准星（quasi-stars）的路径需要高度特定的条件：强Lyman–Werner背景以抑制H$_2$冷却、高且持续的气体吸积率，以及非零自转以延迟广义相对论（GR）不稳定性。这些限制使SMS路径在理论上显得脆弱。作者旨在寻找一种替代机制，能自然满足准星形成的结构与能量需求，同时规避上述限制性假设。

Method: The authors analyze the structural and evolutionary properties of supermassive dark stars (SMDSs)—stars supported and powered by dark matter (DM) annihilation rather than nuclear fusion. They demonstrate that SMDSs inherently develop entropy-stratified envelopes and avoid premature GR collapse due to their extended, puffy structure sustained by DM heating. This allows them to transition directly into quasi-stars with massive central black holes ($\gtrsim 10\%$ of total mass) without needing external fine-tuning. The subsequent envelope expansion and cooling are modeled to reach effective temperatures $T_{\rm eff} \sim 3000$–$6000\,{\rm K}$, consistent with zero-metallicity opacity limits.

作者分析了超大质量暗星（SMDSs）的结构与演化特性——这类恒星由暗物质（DM）湮灭而非核聚变提供支持与能量。他们证明，SMDSs因其由DM加热维持的延展蓬松结构，天然形成熵分层包层，并避免过早发生广义相对论（GR）坍缩。这使得它们无需外部精细调谐即可直接演化为准星，其中心黑洞质量占比高达总质量的$\gtrsim 10\%$。随后，包层膨胀并冷却，有效温度降至$T_{\rm eff} \sim 3000$–$6000\,{\rm K}$，符合零金属丰度下的不透明度极限。

Result: Quasi-stars formed via the SMDS pathway naturally produce cool ($T_{\rm eff}\sim3000$–$6000\,{\rm K}$), optically thick, and spatially unresolved photospheres that match key observational features of JWST’s Little Red Dots. Crucially, this mechanism operates without requiring strong Lyman–Werner backgrounds, sustained high inflow rates, or rotational support—relaxing all major constraints of the canonical SMS model.
\n\n
通过SMDS路径形成的准星自然产生低温（$T_{\rm eff}\sim3000$–$6000\,{\rm K}$）、光学厚且空间不可分辨的光球层，与JWST观测到的“小红点”的关键特征相符。至关重要的是，该机制无需强Lyman–Werner背景、持续高吸积率或自转支撑，从而放松了传统SMS模型的所有主要限制条件。

Conclusion: Supermassive dark stars offer a robust and natural channel for quasi-star formation that explains the enigmatic JWST Little Red Dots. By leveraging dark matter annihilation as an energy source, this pathway circumvents the fine-tuned initial conditions required in standard supermassive star scenarios, providing a compelling alternative for early massive black hole and galaxy seed formation.

超大质量暗星为解释神秘的JWST“小红点”提供了一条稳健而自然的准星形成通道。通过利用暗物质湮灭作为能源，该路径规避了标准超大质量星模型所需的精细初始条件，为早期大质量黑洞及星系种子的形成提供了令人信服的替代方案。

Abstract: The nature of the ``Little Red Dots'' (LRDs) is one of the most profound mysteries posed by the JWST data. One promising class of models that can reproduce the observed LRDs spectra and morphology are quasi-stars: massive envelopes surrounding accreting black holes formed via the collapse of supermassive stars (SMSs). However, the canonical SMS pathway relies on a highly restricted set of environmental and structural conditions: strong Lyman--Werner (LW) backgrounds to suppress H$_2$ cooling, high and sustained gas inflow rates to enforce entropy stratified envelopes, and assume non-zero rotational support in order to prevent GR instability collapse before $\sim 10^6 M_{\odot}$. Here we show that supermassive dark stars (SMDSs), powered by dark matter (DM) annihilation rather than nuclear burning, naturally satisfy the key structural and energetic requirements for quasi-star (QS) formation while relaxing {\it all} of those restrictive conditions listed above. Moreover, quasi-stars formed through the SMDS pathway are born with prompt BH masses ($\gtrsim 10\%$) of the progenitor mass. They therefore enter directly into a late-stage quasi-star regime; subsequently the envelope expands and cools until its photosphere reaches the zero-metallicity opacity limit $(T_{\rm eff}\sim3000$-$6000\,{\rm K}$). Those cool, optically thick, unresolved photospheres can reproduce key features of many JWST LRDs.

</details>


### [66] [Eigenvalue formulation of Stochastic Inflation and application to large perturbation generating inflationary features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00176)
*Swagat S. Mishra,Edmund J. Copeland,Anne M. Green*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper introduces a novel eigenvalue method to solve the adjoint Fokker-Planck equation governing the probability distribution function (PDF) of stochastic e-folds $P({\cal N})$ during inflation. It recovers known results for drift-free quantum diffusion, identifies a previously underappreciated intermediate power-law regime $P({\cal N}) \propto {\cal N}^{-3/2}$, and extends the analysis to constant-drift scenarios—finding analytic solutions in the narrow-well limit and a qualitatively distinct, piecewise-solved PDF in the broad-well limit with an enhanced peak and suppressed tail.
\n\n
本文提出了一种新颖的本征值方法，用于求解支配暴胀期间随机e-fold数概率分布函数（PDF）$P({\cal N})$ 的伴随Fokker-Planck方程。该方法复现了无漂移量子扩散情形下的已知结果，识别出此前被忽视的中间幂律区域 $P({\cal N}) \\propto {\\cal N}^{-3/2}$，并将分析拓展至恒定漂移情形——在窄势阱极限下获得解析解，在宽势阱极限下通过分段构造谱得到定性不同的PDF，其峰值增强而尾部显著压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to accurately compute the PDF of large inflationary fluctuations, which are crucial for predicting Primordial Black Hole (PBH) formation. Traditional methods using characteristic functions are limited or cumbersome in more complex potentials. A self-contained, generalizable technique—based on spectral decomposition of the adjoint Fokker-Planck operator—is desired to handle both drift-free and drifting stochastic inflation scenarios systematically.
\n\n
动机源于精确计算大尺度暴胀涨落PDF的需求，这对预言原初黑洞（Primordial Black Hole, PBH）的形成至关重要。传统基于特征函数的方法在处理更复杂势能时存在局限或繁琐。因此，亟需一种自洽且可推广的技术——基于伴随Fokker-Planck算符的谱分解——以系统处理无漂移和有漂移的随机暴胀情形。

Method: The authors develop a self-contained eigenvalue technique to solve the adjoint Fokker-Planck equation for $P({\cal N})$. They expand the solution in terms of eigenfunctions of the Fokker-Planck operator, reducing the problem to solving a Sturm-Liouville eigenvalue problem. This is first applied to pure quantum diffusion (zero classical drift), then extended to constant drift with reflecting/absorbing boundaries, analyzed in two asymptotic limits: narrow-well (small field range) and broad-well (large field range), where the latter requires a piecewise construction of the eigen-spectrum.
\n\n
作者发展了一种自洽的本征值技术来求解 $P({\\cal N})$ 的伴随Fokker-Planck方程。他们将解按Fokker-Planck算符的本征函数展开，将问题转化为求解Sturm-Liouville本征值问题。首先应用于纯量子扩散（无经典漂移），随后拓展至具有反射/吸收边界的恒定漂移情形，并在两个渐近极限下分析：窄势阱（小场域）和宽势阱（大场域），其中后者需对本征谱进行分段构造。

Result: For drift-free inflation, the method reproduces the known exponential tail of $P({\cal N})$ and reveals a new intermediate power-law regime $P({\cal N}) \\propto {\\cal N}^{-3/2}$. In the narrow-well constant-drift case, an analytic solution is obtained with a mildly suppressed exponential tail. In the broad-well limit, the full eigen-spectrum must be constructed piecewise, yielding a PDF with a significantly enhanced peak near the mean and a strongly suppressed tail compared to the drift-free case.
\n\n
在无漂移暴胀中，该方法复现了已知的 $P({\\cal N})$ 指数尾部，并揭示了一个新的中间幂律区域 $P({\\cal N}) \\propto {\\cal N}^{-3/2}$。在窄势阱恒定漂移情形下，获得了解析解，其指数尾部轻微压低。在宽势阱极限下，必须分段构造完整本征谱，所得PDF在均值附近峰值显著增强，尾部相比无漂移情形被强烈压低。

Conclusion: The eigenvalue method provides a robust, systematic framework for computing $P({\cal N})$ in stochastic inflation beyond perturbative approaches. It uncovers new structural features of the PDF (e.g., the ${\\cal N}^{-3/2}$ regime) and demonstrates how boundary conditions and drift dramatically alter the tail behavior—critical for accurate PBH abundance predictions. The technique is extendable to more general inflationary potentials.
\n\n
本征值方法为超越微扰方法计算随机暴胀中的 $P({\\cal N})$ 提供了一个稳健且系统的框架。它揭示了PDF的新结构特征（如 ${\\cal N}^{-3/2}$ 区域），并展示了边界条件和漂移如何显著改变尾部行为——这对精确预言原初黑洞丰度至关重要。该技术可推广至更一般的暴胀势能。

Abstract: Stochastic inflation is a powerful technique for calculating the probability distribution function (PDF) of large inflationary perturbations, which may collapse to form Primordial Black Holes. The PDF, $P({\cal N})$, of the stochastic number of e-folds, ${\cal N}$, satisfies an adjoint Fokker-Planck Equation. We develop a new self-contained eigenvalue technique which can be used to determine $P({\cal N})$. First we apply this method to the simple case of quantum diffusion along a flat potential without any classical drift. We recover the expression for the PDF that has previously been found using characteristic functions, with an exponential tail. We also identify an intermediate regime between the peak and the exponential tail of the PDF, which has not been emphasized in earlier studies, where it exhibits a power-law behaviour, $P({\cal N}) \propto {\cal N}^{-3/2}$. Finally we apply the method to constant drift inflation, in the narrow- and broad-well limits. In the narrow-well limit, there is an analytic solution and the PDF is similar to the drift-free case, with a mildly suppressed tail. In the broad-well limit, determining the full set of eigenvalues and eigenfunctions requires a piecewise construction of the spectrum, and the broad-well PDF is qualitatively different, with an enhanced peak and a strongly suppressed tail.

</details>

### [67] [Matter-Wave Interferometers as Open-System Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00237)
*Leonardo Badurina,Kathryn M. Zurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Matter-wave interferometers (MWIs) detect dark matter (DM) via quantum phase shifts and decoherence without requiring energy deposition. The paper develops an open effective field theory using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism, revealing a fundamental asymmetry: decoherence exhibits Bose enhancement or Pauli blocking proportional to the DM occupation number $n_{\\rm DM}$, while the phase shift scales at most linearly with $n_{\\rm DM}$. The framework unifies Markovian and non-Markovian regimes by preserving the DM coherence time and extends beyond the heavy-probe approximation.
\n\n
物质波干涉仪（Matter-wave interferometers, MWIs）通过量子相位移动和退相干来探测暗物质（DM），无需依赖能量沉积。本文利用Schwinger-Keldysh形式构建了一个开放的有效场论（open effective field theory），揭示了两个探测通道之间的根本不对称性：退相干表现出与DM占据数$n_{\\rm DM}$成正比的Bose增强或Pauli阻塞效应，而相位移动至多线性依赖于$n_{\\rm DM}$。该框架通过保留DM的相干时间，统一了Markovian与non-Markovian动力学，并超越了重探针极限（heavy-probe limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional dark matter detection relies on measurable energy transfer (e.g., nuclear recoils), which fails for very light or very weakly interacting DM. MWIs offer an alternative by probing quantum interference effects—phase shifts and decoherence—induced by DM interactions that may leave no classical trace. A systematic theoretical framework is needed to describe these quantum open-system dynamics across diverse DM mass scales and interaction types, especially beyond simplified approximations like the heavy-probe limit.
\n\n
传统的暗物质探测依赖于可测量的能量转移（例如核反冲），这对于极轻或极弱相互作用的DM失效。MWIs提供了一种替代方案，通过探测DM相互作用引起的量子干涉效应——相位移动和退相干——即使这些相互作用不留下经典痕迹。需要一个系统性的理论框架来描述跨越不同DM质量尺度和相互作用类型的量子开放系统动力学，特别是超越重探针极限等简化近似。

Method: The authors construct an open effective field theory for MWIs using the Schwinger-Keldysh (in-in) formalism, treating the atomic wavepackets as the system and the DM field as the environment. This approach naturally incorporates both unitary (phase) and dissipative (decoherence) effects. They compute the influence functional to derive master equations governing the reduced density matrix of the interferometer. Crucially, they retain the finite coherence time of the DM field, enabling a unified treatment from ultralight (wave-like) to heavier (particle-like) DM. The analysis focuses on elastic, spin-independent scattering.
\n\n
作者利用Schwinger-Keldysh（in-in）形式为MWIs构建了一个开放有效场论，将原子波包视为系统，DM场视为环境。该方法自然地包含了幺正（相位）和耗散（退相干）效应。他们通过计算影响泛函（influence functional）来推导控制干涉仪约化密度矩阵的主方程（master equations）。关键在于，他们保留了DM场的有限相干时间，从而实现了从超轻（波动性）到较重（粒子性）DM的统一处理。分析聚焦于弹性、自旋无关的散射。

Result: The framework reveals a structural asymmetry: the decoherence rate $\\Gamma_{\\rm dec}$ is proportional to the DM occupation number $n_{\\rm DM}(\\mathbf{q})$ and includes quantum statistical factors (Bose enhancement $1+n_{\\rm DM}$ for bosonic DM or Pauli blocking $1-n_{\\rm DM}$ for fermionic DM). In contrast, the phase shift $\\phi$ is at most linear in $n_{\\rm DM}$. The theory interpolates between Markovian dynamics (for $m_{\\rm DM} \\gtrsim 10^{-15}$ eV) and non-Markovian dynamics (for lighter DM), and provides a systematic expansion beyond the heavy-probe limit where the probe's recoil is neglected.
\n\n
该框架揭示了一种结构性不对称：退相干率$\\Gamma_{\\rm dec}$与DM占据数$n_{\\rm DM}(\\mathbf{q})$成正比，并包含量子统计因子（对于玻色子DM是Bose增强$1+n_{\\rm DM}$，对于费米子DM是Pauli阻塞$1-n_{\\rm DM}$）。相比之下，相位移动$\\phi$至多线性依赖于$n_{\\rm DM}$。该理论在Markovian动力学（对于$m_{\\rm DM} \\gtrsim 10^{-15}$ eV）和non-Markovian动力学（对于更轻的DM）之间进行插值，并提供了超越重探针极限（忽略探针反冲）的系统性展开。

Conclusion: The open EFT based on the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism provides a powerful and unified description of DM detection in MWIs. It clarifies the distinct quantum origins of phase and decoherence signals, incorporates quantum statistics of the DM, and is valid across a vast range of DM masses by accounting for its intrinsic coherence time. This paves the way for more sensitive and theoretically robust interpretations of MWI experiments searching for elusive DM candidates.
\n\n
基于Schwinger-Keldysh形式的开放EFT为MWIs中的DM探测提供了强大而统一的描述。它阐明了相位和退相干信号的不同量子起源，纳入了DM的量子统计，并通过考虑其内在相干时间而在广阔的DM质量范围内有效。这为更灵敏、理论更稳健地解释搜寻难以捉摸DM候选者的MWI实验铺平了道路。

Abstract: Matter-wave interferometers (MWIs) offer a uniquely quantum route to dark matter (DM) detection: DM can reveal itself through phase and decoherence between spatially separated wavepackets, even when negligible energy deposition or resolvable recoil occurs. We formulate these effects in an open effective field theory for MWIs using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism, which highlights a structural asymmetry between the two detection channels. For elastic spin-independent DM scattering, decoherence inherits novel Bose enhancement or Pauli blocking factors, while the phase is at most linear in the DM occupation number. By retaining the DM's coherence time, this framework spans Markovian and non-Markovian dynamics across a wide range of DM masses, and systematically organizes corrections beyond the heavy-probe limit.

</details>


### [68] [Weight-Based Representation Learning for Parameter Inference in Monte Carlo Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00238)
*Vichayanun Wachirapusitanand,Norraphat Srimanobhas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper introduces a Machine Learning method for parameter inference in physics that leverages event-level weights from simulators as weak supervision to learn parameter-sensitive representations. These representations are discretized into summary statistics and used in likelihood-based inference, demonstrated on simulated four-top-quark events to infer the top quark Yukawa coupling.
\n\n
本文提出了一种基于机器学习的物理模型参数推断方法，该方法利用模拟器提供的事件级权重作为弱监督信号，以学习对参数敏感的表示。这些表示被离散化为汇总统计量，并用于基于似然的推断过程，在模拟的四顶夸克事例中用于推断顶夸克Yukawa耦合（目标参数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional likelihood-free inference methods often discard information by compressing high-dimensional data into hand-crafted or insufficient summary statistics. Simulators commonly output event-level weights that encode how observation probabilities change with model parameters; these weights contain valuable gradient-like information about parameter dependence, which can be exploited to learn more informative representations without requiring full likelihood evaluations.
\n\n
传统的无似然推断方法通常通过将高维数据压缩为手工设计或信息不足的汇总统计量而丢失信息。模拟器通常输出事件级权重，这些权重编码了观测概率随模型参数的变化方式；这些权重包含关于参数依赖性的宝贵梯度类信息，可在无需完整似然计算的情况下用于学习更具信息量的表示。

Method: The authors train neural networks using simulator-provided event weights as a weak supervision signal. Specifically, the weights $w_i(\\theta)$—which represent the derivative of the log-probability of an event $x_i$ with respect to the parameter $\\theta$—are used to guide representation learning. The learned high-dimensional representations are then discretized (e.g., via binning) into summary statistics. A likelihood function is constructed from these binned statistics, enabling standard likelihood-based inference (e.g., maximum likelihood estimation or Bayesian inference) for the parameter of interest, here the top quark Yukawa coupling.
\n\n
作者使用模拟器提供的事件权重作为弱监督信号训练神经网络。具体而言，权重 $w_i(\\theta)$——表示事件 $x_i$ 的对数概率相对于参数 $\\theta$ 的导数——被用于指导表示学习。所学得的高维表示随后被离散化（例如通过分箱）为汇总统计量。基于这些分箱统计量构建似然函数，从而实现对目标参数（此处为顶夸克Yukawa耦合）的标准基于似然的推断（如最大似然估计或贝叶斯推断）。

Result: The proposed method successfully learns representations that capture parameter-sensitive structures in simulated four-top-quark events. Using these representations as summary statistics, the framework accurately infers the top quark Yukawa coupling, demonstrating improved performance over baseline methods that do not utilize event weights. The approach effectively harnesses the gradient information embedded in simulator weights to enhance inference efficiency and precision.
\n\n
所提出的方法成功学习到了能够捕捉模拟四顶夸克事例中参数敏感结构的表示。利用这些表示作为汇总统计量，该框架准确地推断出了顶夸克Yukawa耦合，其性能优于未利用事件权重的基线方法。该方法有效地利用了嵌入在模拟器权重中的梯度信息，提升了推断的效率与精度。

Conclusion: Simulator-provided event weights constitute a powerful yet underutilized source of information for parameter inference. By treating these weights as weak supervision signals, machine learning models can learn highly informative representations that retain sensitivity to underlying physical parameters. This approach bridges simulation-based inference with representation learning, offering a scalable and efficient pathway for high-dimensional data analysis in particle physics and beyond.
\n\n
模拟器提供的事件权重是参数推断中一种强大但尚未充分利用的信息来源。通过将这些权重视为弱监督信号，机器学习模型可以学习到对底层物理参数高度敏感的信息性表示。该方法将基于模拟的推断与表示学习相结合，为粒子物理及其他领域中的高维数据分析提供了一条可扩展且高效的路径。

Abstract: We present a Machine Learning-based approach for parameter inference in physics models that exploits event-level weights provided by simulators. Individual observations may have weights assigned by a simulation framework that describe the change in probability with respect to the model parameters. As these assigned weights encode the sensitivity of the parameter, they can serve as a weak supervision signal for learning parameter-informative representations. In this work, our inference models are trained using simulator-provided weights to learn representations and their relations to the parameter-sensitive structures in the high-dimensional observations. The resulting representations are then discretised into summary statistics and the model parameter value is inferred using a likelihood-based inference procedure. We illustrate this approach by using simulated four-top-quark production to infer the top quark Yukawa coupling (the parameter of interest).

*Yorgo Sawaya,Christina Cocuzza,Gregory Matousek,Matthew McEneaney,Andreas Metz,Daniel Pitonyak,Alexei Prokudin,Nobuo Sato,Anselm Vossen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Future dihadron semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering (SIDIS) data from CLAS12, SoLID, and ePIC will significantly improve constraints on transversity parton distribution functions (PDFs), especially at intermediate-to-large $x$ (Jefferson Lab) and across all $x$, including small-$x$ (EIC). <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transversity PDFs encode fundamental information about the transverse spin structure of the nucleon but are poorly constrained compared to other leading-twist PDFs due to limited experimental data. Future high-precision SIDIS measurements offer a unique opportunity to better determine these distributions and test theoretical predictions—especially their behavior at small $x$—while also providing more accurate extractions of nucleon tensor charges, which are key for probing physics beyond the Standard Model.

Transversity PDFs编码了关于核子横向自旋结构的基本信息，但由于实验数据有限，其约束远不如其他leading-twist PDFs。未来的高精度SIDIS测量为更精确地确定这些分布提供了独特机会，尤其是检验其在小$x$区域的行为理论预测，同时还能更准确地提取核子tensor charges，这对探索Standard Model之外的新物理至关重要。

Method: The authors generate pseudo-data mimicking projected sensitivities of CLAS12 (proton target), SoLID ($^3$He target), and ePIC (both proton and $^3$He targets) for dihadron SIDIS observables. These pseudo-data are incorporated into the JAMDiFF global QCD analysis framework, which fits transversity PDFs using existing experimental data. The impact on PDF uncertainties and derived tensor charges is quantified via Monte Carlo sampling and Hessian error propagation. Comparisons with recent lattice QCD calculations of tensor charges are also performed to assess consistency or potential tensions.
\n\n
作者生成模拟CLAS12（质子靶）、SoLID（$^3$He靶）和ePIC（质子与$^3$He靶）实验预期灵敏度的伪数据，用于双强子SIDIS观测量。这些伪数据被纳入JAMDiFF全局QCD分析框架，该框架利用现有实验数据拟合transversity PDFs。通过Monte Carlo抽样和Hessian误差传播方法量化对PDF不确定度及导出的tensor charges的影响。同时与近期lattice QCD对tensor charges的计算进行比较，以评估一致性或潜在张力。

Result: Inclusion of Jefferson Lab pseudo-data reduces transversity PDF uncertainties significantly in the intermediate-to-large $x$ region ($x \\gtrsim 0.2$), while ePIC data provide strong constraints over the full $x$ range down to $x \\sim 10^{-3}$, enabling the first experimental probe of the predicted small-$x$ behavior. The tensor charge uncertainties are reduced by factors of 2–3, bringing experimental extractions into closer alignment with lattice QCD results; however, scenarios are identified where residual tension could persist depending on future data outcomes.
\n\n
加入Jefferson Lab的伪数据后，在中等到大$x$区域（$x \\gtrsim 0.2$）transversity PDF的不确定性显著降低，而ePIC数据在整个$x$范围（低至$x \\sim 10^{-3}$）提供强约束，使首次实验探测预测的小$x$行为成为可能。Tensor charge的不确定性减少了2–3倍，使实验提取结果更接近lattice QCD计算；但研究也识别出某些情形下，根据未来数据结果，仍可能存在残余张力。

Conclusion: Future dihadron SIDIS measurements at Jefferson Lab and the EIC will dramatically enhance our knowledge of transversity PDFs and nucleon tensor charges. The combined dataset will not only validate theoretical expectations at small $x$ but also enable stringent tests of QCD through cross-validation between global analyses and lattice QCD, with implications for searches of new physics via precision nucleon structure studies.
\n\n
Jefferson Lab和EIC上的未来双强子SIDIS测量将极大提升我们对transversity PDFs和核子tensor charges的认识。组合数据集不仅能验证小$x$区域的理论预期，还能通过全局分析与lattice QCD之间的交叉验证实现对QCD的严格检验，这对通过高精度核子结构研究探索新物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: We assess the impact of future measurements of dihadron production in semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering from the CLAS12 and proposed SoLID experiments at Jefferson Lab, as well as from the ePIC experiment at the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), on the transversity parton distribution functions (PDFs) and the corresponding tensor charges of the nucleon. To this end, we generate pseudo-data for these experiments for a proton target (CLAS12 and ePIC) and a $^3$He target (SoLID and ePIC), and we include these pseudo-data in the JAMDiFF global analysis of existing experimental dihadron data. We find that future data from Jefferson Lab will significantly reduce uncertainties in the transversity PDFs in the region of intermediate-to-large quark momentum fractions $x$, while the EIC will provide strong constraints across the entire range of $x$, allowing for the first experimental test of the predicted small-$x$ behavior of the transversity PDFs. In discussing the reduction of uncertainties in the tensor charges, we also compare the results from the data analyses with those from lattice QCD, highlighting scenarios in which compatibility or tension between the two would arise.

</details>

TL;DR: The paper resolves the $B \\to K \\ell \\ell$ anomaly via the Left-Right Inverse Seesaw (LRIS) model, where a non-decoupling box diagram with charged scalars and heavy neutrinos generates $\\Delta C_9 \\approx -1$ while naturally suppressing $\\Delta C_{10} \\approx 0$.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The persistent discrepancy in $b \\to s \\mu^+ \\mu^-$ transitions—manifested as a negative shift in the vector Wilson coefficient $\\Delta C_9 \\approx -1$, with $\\Delta C_{10}$ consistent with zero—motivates exploring beyond-Standard Model (BSM) explanations that can simultaneously fit this pattern without violating other flavor constraints. \n\n
$b \\to s \\mu^+ \\mu^-$ 跃迁中持续存在的偏差——表现为矢量Wilson系数 $\\Delta C_9 \\approx -1$ 的负偏移，而轴向系数 $\\Delta C_{10}$ 与零一致——促使人们探索能够同时拟合这一模式且不违反其他味物理约束的新物理（BSM）机制。左右逆跷跷板模型（LRIS）提供了一个包含右手流和重惰性中微子的理论框架，可能实现这一目标。

Method: The authors compute contributions from charged-scalar/heavy-neutrino box diagrams in the LRIS model to the effective Hamiltonian for $b \\to s \\ell \\ell$ transitions. They exploit the non-decoupling nature of right-handed couplings in the heavy-neutrino limit to generate an unsuppressed $\\Delta C_9$, while enforcing $\\Delta C_{10} \\approx 0$ through cancellation between left- and right-handed Dirac Yukawa couplings. They also analyze $B_s$--$\\bar{B}_s$ mixing constraints, showing suppression via a GIM-like phase structure in the right-handed quark mixing matrix. A global numerical scan over LRIS parameters is performed, incorporating constraints from $b \\to s \\gamma$, electroweak precision tests, and LHC limits.
\n\n
作者计算了LRIS模型中带电标量与重中微子盒图对 $b \\to s \\ell \\ell$ 有效哈密顿量的贡献。他们利用重手中微子极限下右手耦合的非退耦特性，生成未被压低的 $\\Delta C_9$，并通过左右手Dirac Yukawa耦合之间的抵消实现 $\\Delta C_{10} \\approx 0$。同时分析了 $B_s$--$\\bar{B}_s$ 混合约束，表明右手夸克混合矩阵中的类GIM相位结构可将其压低数个数量级。作者还对LRIS参数空间进行了全局数值扫描，纳入了 $b \\to s \\gamma$、电弱精密测量及LHC限制等约束。

Result: The LRIS model successfully reproduces $\\Delta C_9 \\approx -1$ with $\\Delta C_{10} \\approx 0$ via a non-decoupling mechanism. Contributions to $B_s$--$\\bar{B}_s$ mixing are suppressed by $\\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$ due to phase cancellations. Conclusion: The Left-Right Inverse Seesaw model provides a compelling and testable explanation for the $B \\to K \\ell \\ell$ anomaly, linking it to the neutrino mass generation mechanism. Its distinctive phenomenology—non-decoupling contributions to $\\Delta C_9$, automatic suppression of $\\Delta C_{10}$, and phase-suppressed FCNCs—offers a coherent solution consistent with all existing data and motivates targeted collider searches.

Abstract: We investigate the long-standing anomaly in the rare decay B into Kll within the Left-Right Inverse Seesaw (LRIS) model. Global analyses of the B into s mu mu data consistently indicate a significant negative shift in the vector Wilson coefficient, $ΔC{9} \approx -1$, while the axial coefficient $ΔC{10}$ remains consistent with zero. We show that a charged-scalar/heavy-neutrino box diagram in the LRIS model naturally generates this pattern through a \emph{non-decoupling} mechanism: the right-handed coupling
  produces a contribution to $ΔC{9}$ that is unsuppressed in the heavy-neutrino limit, while the simultaneous presence of a comparable left-handed Dirac Yukawa coupling ensures the automatic cancellation $ΔC{10} \approx 0$. The otherwise large contribution to $B_s$--$\bar{B}_s$ mixing is suppressed by several orders of magnitude through a GIM-like phase structure in the right-handed quark mixing matrix. A numerical scan over the model parameter space identifies a viable region, consistent with all current flavor and collider constraints. The $b \to sγ$ constraint is satisfied with two orders of magnitude to spare throughout the viable band. These results motivate correlated searches for the charged scalar and the heavy right-handed neutrinos at the LHC and future high-luminosity experiments.
