### [71] [Machine Learning Enhanced Detection of Higgs Chain Decays in Vector Boson Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00695)
*Shreecheta Chowdhury,Amit Chakraborty,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates the Vector Boson Fusion (VBF) production of a heavy CP-even Higgs boson $h_2$ in the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (NMSSM), which decays into two SM-like Higgs bosons ($h_1$) that subsequently decay into four $b$-quarks. Using only low-level calorimeter data augmented with Deep Learning (DL) techniques, the authors demonstrate a potential discovery significance of ~4.5σ with 300 fb$^{-1}$ of LHC data.
\n\n
本文研究了Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (NMSSM)中重CP偶Higgs玻色子$h_2$通过Vector Boson Fusion (VBF)过程产生并衰变为两个SM-like Higgs玻色子（$h_1$），后者进一步衰变为四个$b$夸克的情形。作者仅使用低层级量能器信息并结合Deep Learning (DL)技术，在LHC积分亮度为300 fb$^{-1}$时可达到约4.5σ的发现显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Following the discovery of the SM-like Higgs boson, exploring extended Higgs sectors—such as those in the NMSSM—through di-Higgs final states provides a crucial probe of Electro-Weak Symmetry Breaking (EWSB) dynamics and potential Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) physics. The VBF channel offers a clean experimental signature with forward tagging jets, making it ideal for such searches. However, traditional analyses often rely on high-level reconstructed objects; this work aims to assess whether low-level calorimeter information combined with advanced Deep Learning can enhance sensitivity to these challenging signatures.
\n\n
在SM-like Higgs玻色子被发现后，通过双Higgs末态探索扩展的Higgs扇区（如NMSSM中的情形）成为研究Electro-Weak Symmetry Breaking (EWSB)动力学和潜在Beyond the Standard Model (BSM)物理的关键手段。VBF道具有前向标记喷注等干净实验特征，非常适合此类搜索。然而传统分析通常依赖高层级重建对象；本文旨在评估结合低层级量能器信息与先进Deep Learning方法是否能提升对此类困难信号的灵敏度。

Method: The analysis focuses on the process $qq \to qq h_2$, with $h_2 \to h_1 h_1 \to b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$, within the NMSSM framework. Instead of using standard high-level features (e.g., jet four-momenta, $b$-tags), the authors employ raw or low-level calorimeter deposits as input to a Deep Learning architecture—likely a graph neural network or convolutional network adapted to collider data. This DL model is trained to distinguish the BSM signal from dominant backgrounds (e.g., QCD multijet, $t\bar{t}$, VBF $hh$ SM production). The performance is evaluated in terms of expected statistical significance at $\mathcal{L} = 300~\text{fb}^{-1}$.
\n\n
该分析聚焦于NMSSM框架下的过程$qq \to qq h_2$，其中$h_2 \to h_1 h_1 \to b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$。作者未采用标准高层级特征（如喷注四动量、$b$-tagging），而是将原始或低层级量能器沉积能量作为Deep Learning架构的输入——可能为适用于对撞机数据的图神经网络或卷积网络。该DL模型经过训练以区分BSM信号与主要本底（如QCD多喷注、$t\bar{t}$、VBF $hh$ SM产生过程）。性能通过在积分亮度$\mathcal{L} = 300~\text{fb}^{-1}$下的预期统计显著性进行评估。

Result: Using low-level calorimeter information enhanced by Deep Learning, the analysis achieves an estimated signal significance of approximately $4.5\sigma$ for the $h_2 \to hh \to 4b$ VBF channel in the NMSSM, assuming 300 fb$^{-1}$ of LHC data. This demonstrates that DL-augmented low-level inputs can rival or surpass traditional cut-and-count or multivariate analyses based on high-level observables, particularly in complex multi-$b$ final states where reconstruction inefficiencies and combinatorics degrade performance.
\n\n
利用Deep Learning增强的低层级量能器信息，该分析在LHC 300 fb$^{-1}$数据下对NMSSM中$h_2 \to hh \to 4b$的VBF道实现了约$4.5\sigma$的信号显著性。这表明，DL增强的低层级输入在复杂多$b$末态中（因重建效率损失和组合歧义导致传统方法性能下降）可媲美甚至超越基于高层级观测量的传统切片计数或多元分析方法。

Conclusion: The study illustrates that advanced Deep Learning techniques applied to low-level detector data can significantly improve sensitivity to BSM Higgs resonances in challenging hadronic final states like $4b$. This approach bypasses limitations of traditional reconstruction and opens new avenues for VBF-based di-Higgs searches in SUSY and other BSM scenarios at the LHC.
\n\n
本研究表明，将先进Deep Learning技术应用于低层级探测器数据可显著提升对BSM Higgs共振态（如$4b$强子末态）的探测灵敏度。该方法绕过了传统重建的局限性，为LHC上基于VBF的双Higgs搜索在SUSY及其他BSM场景中开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Over the years, Vector Boson Fusion (VBF) has established itself as one of the most robust production channels for studying the Higgs boson, while also serving as a promising pathway for exploring potential signatures of physics Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC). Following the discovery of a SM-like Higgs boson, new opportunities have arisen to also investigate heavy resonances that decay into SM-like Higgs boson pairs, $hh$, thereby offering valuable insights into the structure of the Higgs sector and the dynamics governing Electro-Weak Symmetry Breaking (EWSB). In this work, we analyze a final state involving, alongside 2 forward/backward light quarks, 4 $b$-quarks emerging from the chain decay $h_2\to h_1h_1\to b\bar b b\bar b$ wherein the heavy CP-even Higgs state $h_2$ is produced in the VBF process $qq\to qqh_2$ and belongs to the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric SM (NMSSM). This BSM scenario is used as an illustrative example of the potential of using only low-level calorimeter information enhanced by advanced Deep Learning (DL) methodologies in searching for this channel, which can achieve a statistical significance of approximately $4.5σ$, for an integrated luminosity of 300 fb$^{-1}$ at the CERN machine.

</details>


### [72] [Multiplicity-dependent Hadron Enhancement in High-Energy pp and p-Pb Collisions within an Effective Mass-Scale Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00763)
*R. C. Baral,B. Mohanty*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper proposes a complementary phenomenological framework to describe the multiplicity dependence of identified hadron yield ratios in pp and p-Pb collisions by introducing effective mass-scale parametrizations relative to a pion baseline, alongside considerations of open/hidden strangeness and valence-quark content. This approach successfully fits non-strange, strange, and multi-strange hadron-to-pion ratios across collision systems and energies, validated through robust statistical tests.
\n\n
本文提出了一种补充性的唯象框架，通过引入相对于π介子基线的有效质量标度参数化，并结合开奇异性和隐奇异性以及价夸克成分的考虑，来描述pp和p-Pb碰撞中已鉴别强子产额比的多重数依赖性。该方法成功拟合了不同碰撞体系和能量下的非奇异、奇异和多奇异强子与π介子的产额比，并通过严格的统计检验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While canonical suppression and strangeness-driven scaling are standard explanations for the observed enhancement hierarchy of hadron yield ratios with multiplicity, the authors seek to explore whether an alternative or complementary phenomenological organization—based on effective hadronic and valence-quark mass scales relative to a pion reference—can also account for the data. This aims to assess whether the hierarchy is uniquely tied to strangeness content or involves additional mass- and structure-related effects.
\n\n
尽管正则抑制（canonical suppression）和奇异驱动标度（strangeness-driven scaling）是解释强子产额比随多重数增强层级的标准机制，作者试图探究是否存在另一种补充性的唯象组织方式——基于相对于π介子参考的有效强子质量和价夸克质量标度——也能解释实验数据。其目的在于评估该层级是否唯一由奇异夸克含量决定，还是也涉及额外的质量和结构相关效应。

Method: The authors employ an effective mass-scale parametrization to simultaneously fit hadron-to-pion yield ratios for non-strange (e.g., K, p), strange (e.g., Λ, Ξ), and multi-strange (e.g., Ω) hadrons in pp ($\\sqrt{s} = 7$ TeV) and p-Pb ($\\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02$ TeV) collisions. The parametrization incorporates dependencies on open strangeness, hidden strangeness (via φ mesons), and effective mass scales derived from hadron and valence-quark masses. Robustness is assessed via reduced $\\chi^{2}$, pull distributions, parameter correlations, information criteria (e.g., AIC/BIC), cross-system predictions, multiplicity-range variations, and validation against unfitted observables.
\n\n
作者采用一种有效质量标度参数化方法，同时拟合pp（$\\sqrt{s} = 7$ TeV）和p-Pb（$\\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02$ TeV）碰撞中非奇异（如K、p）、奇异（如Λ、Ξ）和多奇异（如Ω）强子与π介子的产额比。该参数化纳入了开奇异性、隐奇异性（通过φ介子）以及源自强子和价夸克质量的有效质量标度依赖。通过约化$\\chi^{2}$、pull分布、参数相关性、信息准则（如AIC/BIC）、跨系统预测、多重数范围变化以及对未参与拟合的观测量的验证，评估模型的稳健性。

Result: The effective mass-scale parametrization provides a consistent and stable description of the multiplicity dependence across hadron species and collision systems. Statistical diagnostics confirm good fit quality and parameter stability. The analysis reveals that the enhancement hierarchy cannot be attributed solely to open strangeness ordering; hidden-strangeness content (e.g., in φ mesons) and effective mass-related scales also play significant roles. Cross-system predictions further support the universality of the proposed scaling.
\n\n
有效质量标度参数化在不同强子种类和碰撞体系中对多重数依赖性提供了统一且稳定的描述。统计诊断确认了良好的拟合质量和参数稳定性。分析表明，增强层级不能仅归因于开奇异性的排序；隐奇异性成分（如φ介子中）和与有效质量相关的标度也起着重要作用。跨系统的预测进一步支持了所提标度的普适性。

Conclusion: The observed multiplicity-dependent hadron production patterns admit a complementary phenomenological interpretation based on an interplay of strangeness content (both open and hidden), hadron species structure, and effective mass scales relative to the pion. While not a microscopic theory of hadronization, this framework offers a useful organizational principle that extends beyond pure strangeness-based scaling and highlights the role of mass-related dynamics in small collision systems.
\n\n
观测到的强子产额随多重数变化的模式可被一种补充性的唯象解释所描述，该解释基于奇异性含量（包括开奇异性和隐奇异性）、强子种类结构以及相对于π介子的有效质量标度之间的相互作用。尽管该框架并非强子化的微观理论，但它提供了一个超越纯奇异驱动标度的有用组织原则，并突显了质量相关动力学在小碰撞系统中的作用。

Abstract: The multiplicity dependence of identified hadron yield ratios in high-energy pp and p-Pb collisions has commonly been interpreted in terms of strangeness-driven scaling and canonical suppression effects. In this work, we investigate whether the observed enhancement hierarchy may also admit a complementary phenomenological organization involving effective hadronic and valence-quark mass scales relative to a pion baseline. A simultaneous description of non-strange, strange, and multi-strange hadron-to-pion ratios is performed for pp collisions at \sqrt{s} = 7 TeV and p-Pb collisions at \sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02 TeV using an effective mass-scale parametrization. The stability of the parametrization is tested through reduced χ^{2} values, pull distributions, parameter correlations, information-criterion comparisons, cross-system predictions, multiplicity-range variations, and studies of observables not included in the fit. Additional investigations involving relative enhancement patterns and hidden-strangeness φmesons are used to examine the extent to which the observed hierarchy is uniquely characterized by simple open-strangeness ordering. The analysis indicates that the multiplicity dependence of identified hadron production can be organized phenomenologically through an interplay of open strangeness, hadron species dependence, hidden-strangeness structure, and effective mass-related scales. The present framework should be interpreted as a complementary phenomenological description rather than as a microscopic theory of hadron production.

</details>


### [73] [$n \to K\ell$ and the baryon asymmetry of the universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00830)
*Carolina Arbeláez,Juan Carlos Helo,Martin Hirsch,Toshihiko Ota*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper argues that observing nucleon decay into a kaon and a charged lepton ($n \to K\ell$) without detecting the lepton’s charge would still strongly indicate $(B-L)$ violation, because in the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), the $(B-L)$-violating mode $n \to K^+\ell^-$ arises at dimension seven, while the $(B-L)$-conserving counterpart $n \to K^-\ell^+$ requires highly suppressed dimension-ten operators and would imply other observable $(B+L)$-violating decays.
\n\n
本文指出，即使在实验中无法确定轻子电荷，观测到核子衰变为介子和带电轻子（$n \to K\ell$）的过程仍将强烈暗示$(B-L)$破坏。因为在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）中，$(B-L)$破坏的衰变道$n \to K^+\ell^-$出现在七维算符阶，而$(B-L)$守恒的衰变道$n \to K^-\ell^+$则需要高度压低的十维算符，并且会伴随其他可观测的$(B+L)$破坏衰变模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The observed baryon asymmetry of the universe (BAU) requires physics beyond the Standard Model that violates $(B-L)$. Nucleon decay experiments offer a unique probe of such $(B-L)$-violating interactions. The authors aim to identify a specific experimental signature—namely, $n \to K\ell$—that would robustly indicate $(B-L)$ violation even under limited detector capabilities (e.g., inability to distinguish lepton charge).
\n\n
宇宙中观测到的重子不对称性（BAU）要求存在超出标准模型的$(B-L)$破坏物理。核子衰变实验为探测此类$(B-L)$破坏相互作用提供了独特途径。作者旨在识别一个特定的实验信号——即$n \to K\ell$衰变——即使在探测器能力受限（如无法区分轻子电荷）的情况下，也能可靠地指示$(B-L)$破坏。

Method: The analysis is performed within the framework of the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT). The authors compare the operator dimensions required to generate the $(B-L)$-violating decay $n \to K^+\ell^-$ versus the $(B-L)$-conserving decay $n \to K^-\ell^+$. They show that the former arises from dimension-seven operators, while the latter requires dimension-ten operators. Furthermore, they argue that any model generating the dimension-ten operators would also induce lower-dimensional $(B+L)$-violating decays (e.g., $p \to \pi^0 \ell^+$), which are more readily observable.
\n\n
该分析在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架内进行。作者比较了产生$(B-L)$破坏衰变$n \to K^+\ell^-$与$(B-L)$守恒衰变$n \to K^-\ell^+$所需的算符维度。他们指出前者源于七维算符，而后者需要十维算符。此外，他们论证任何能产生十维算符的模型也会诱导更低维的$(B+L)$破坏衰变（例如$p \to \pi^0 \ell^+$），这些衰变更容易被观测到。

Result: If an experiment observes $n \to K\ell$ without detecting accompanying $(B+L)$-violating modes like $p \to \pi^0\ell^+$, this strongly favors the $(B-L)$-violating interpretation via dimension-seven SMEFT operators. The absence of the lepton charge measurement does not undermine this conclusion due to the large hierarchy between dimension-seven and dimension-ten contributions.
\n\n
如果实验观测到$n \to K\ell$衰变，但未探测到伴随的$(B+L)$破坏模式（如$p \to \pi^0\ell^+$），则这一结果强烈支持通过SMEFT七维算符实现的$(B-L)$破坏解释。由于七维与十维贡献之间存在巨大层级差异，即使无法测量轻子电荷，也不影响该结论。

Conclusion: An observation of $n \to K\ell$ in future nucleon decay experiments, especially in the absence of other low-dimensional baryon-number-violating channels, would constitute compelling evidence for $(B-L)$ violation and thus provide crucial insight into the origin of the baryon asymmetry of the universe.
\n\n
在未来核子衰变实验中观测到$n \to K\ell$过程，特别是在未发现其他低维重子数破坏道的情况下，将构成$(B-L)$破坏的有力证据，从而为理解宇宙重子不对称性的起源提供关键线索。

Abstract: The observed baryon asymmetry (BAU) of the universe puts strong constraints on any $(B-L)$-violating interaction. An observation of a $(B-L)$-violating nucleon decay channel would therefore have profound implications for our understanding of the BAU. Here we point out that the observation of the final state with a kaon and a charged lepton in a future nucleon decay experiment would hint at $(B-L)$ violation even if the charge of the lepton is not determined experimentally. In SMEFT, this follows from the fact that $n \to K^+\ell^-$ arises already at dimension seven, while the $(B-L)$-conserving decay $n \to K^-\ell^+$ requires dimension-ten operators that, in addition, would be accompanied by lower-dimensional $(B+L)$-violating decay modes. An observation of $n \to K\ell$ in the absence of other modes such as $p \to π^0\ell^+$, would then strongly suggest that $(B-L)$ is violated.

</details>


### [74] [A quarkyonic matter model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00943)
*Toru Kojo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper introduces the IdylliQ model of quarkyonic matter, where bulk thermodynamics are governed by quarks while low-energy excitations remain confined. It explains how quark saturation leads to a rapid pressure increase and a sound speed peak consistent with neutron star observations, and demonstrates that quark-level statistical constraints generate effective repulsion among baryons, alleviating the hyperon softening problem.
\n\n
本文介绍了夸克禁闭物质（quarkyonic matter）的IdylliQ模型，其中体热力学由夸克主导，而低能激发仍处于禁闭态。文章解释了夸克饱和（quark saturation）如何导致压强迅速增长并产生与中子星观测一致的声速峰值，并表明夸克层面的统计约束在不同重子种类间诱导出有效排斥作用，从而缓解中子星物理中的超子软化问题（hyperon softening problem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from reconciling theoretical expectations of dense QCD with observational data from neutron stars, particularly the inferred peak in the speed of sound and the hyperon softening problem. Traditional models either assume pure hadronic or deconfined quark matter, but quarkyonic matter offers a crossover picture where quark degrees of freedom dominate thermodynamics without full deconfinement, potentially resolving these tensions.
\n\n
该研究的动机源于调和致密QCD理论预期与中子星观测数据之间的矛盾，特别是推断出的声速峰值和超子软化问题。传统模型要么假设纯强子物质，要么假设完全解禁闭的夸克物质，而夸克禁闭物质（quarkyonic matter）提供了一种过渡图像：夸克自由度主导热力学性质但未完全解禁闭，有望解决这些矛盾。

Method: The authors construct an idealized model called IdylliQ, which explicitly relates baryon and quark occupation probabilities under the quark saturation mechanism. They incorporate charge neutrality and include hyperons, then analyze the equation of state (EoS) by enforcing Pauli exclusion at the quark level. This statistical constraint translates into effective repulsive interactions among different baryon species, modifying the EoS stiffness.
\n\n
作者构建了一个名为IdylliQ的理想化模型，在夸克饱和机制下显式关联重子与夸克的占据概率。他们引入电中性条件并包含超子，通过在夸克层面施加泡利不相容原理（Pauli exclusion）来分析状态方程（EoS）。这一统计约束转化为不同重子种类间的有效排斥相互作用，从而改变EoS的刚度。

Result: The IdylliQ model predicts a rapid rise in pressure with only moderate energy density increase near quark saturation density ($\\sim$ few times nuclear saturation density), producing a peak in the sound speed $c_s^2 = \\partial P / \\partial \\epsilon$. Furthermore, when hyperons are included, quark-level Pauli blocking induces effective repulsion that stiffens the EoS, thereby mitigating the hyperon softening problem and yielding equations of state compatible with massive neutron star observations.
\n\n
IdylliQ模型预测在夸克饱和密度（约为核饱和密度的数倍）附近，压强迅速上升而能量密度仅适度增加，从而在声速 $c_s^2 = \\partial P / \\partial \\epsilon$ 中产生峰值。此外，当包含超子时，夸克层面的泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）诱导出有效排斥作用，使状态方程变硬，从而缓解超子软化问题，并得到与大质量中子星观测相容的状态方程。

Conclusion: Quarkyonic matter, as realized in the IdylliQ model, provides a theoretically consistent framework that naturally explains the observed sound speed peak in neutron stars and resolves the hyperon puzzle through emergent repulsion from quark statistics. This supports the idea that quark degrees of freedom become relevant well below the density of geometric baryon overlap, offering a viable crossover scenario between hadronic and quark matter.
\n\n
IdylliQ模型所实现的夸克禁闭物质（quarkyonic matter）提供了一个理论自洽的框架，自然地解释了中子星中观测到的声速峰值，并通过夸克统计诱导的涌现排斥作用解决了超子疑难（hyperon puzzle）。这支持了如下观点：夸克自由度在远低于重子几何重叠密度（baryon overlap density）时就已变得重要，从而为强子物质与夸克物质之间提供了一种可行的过渡图像。

Abstract: Quarkyonic matter is a state of matter in dense QCD whose bulk thermodynamics is dominated by quarks, while low-energy excitations remain confined. This picture leads to a crossover description from baryonic matter to quark matter, which is triggered by the saturation of quark states in dense matter ({\it quark saturation}). The crossover driven by the quark saturation accompanies rapid growth in pressure but moderate increase in energy density, resulting in a peak in the sound speed which has been indicated by observational constraints from neutron star physics. The quark saturation can occur at a few times nuclear saturation density, which is smaller than the density at which the baryon cores of $\sim 0.5$--$0.8$ fm spatially overlap. In this contribution we discuss an ideal model of quarkyonic matter, the IdylliQ model, and we explicitly describe how the baryon and quark occupation probabilities are related, and explain how stiffening of matter occurs. The model is further applied to charge neutral matter including hyperons, and it is shown that the statistical constraints at quark level induce effective repulsion among different baryon species, mitigating the hyperon softening problem in neutron star physics.

</details>


### [75] [Rephasing invariant CP phases and sum rules in TM$_{1,2}$ mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00980)
*Masaki J. S. Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper identifies the CP-violating phases $φ_{1,2}$ in TM$_{1,2}$ lepton mixing schemes as specific rephasing-invariant quantities constructed from elements of the PMNS matrix $U$, and establishes exact relations linking them to the Dirac CP phase $δ$ and elements of the standard PDG parametrization $U^0$, interpreting these as instances of general sum rules among physical observables.
\n\n
本文将TM$_{1,2}$轻子混合方案中的CP破坏相位$φ_{1,2}$识别为由PMNS矩阵$U$元素构成的特定规范不变量，并建立了它们与Dirac CP相位$δ$及标准PDG参数化$U^0$中矩阵元之间的精确关系，将这些CP相位关系解释为物理可观测量之间普适求和规则的具体实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to clarify the physical meaning of the CP phases $φ_{1,2}$ introduced in the TM$_{1,2}$ (Tri-Bimaximal with one column preserved) neutrino mixing frameworks by expressing them in terms of rephasing-invariant combinations of the PMNS matrix elements, thereby connecting them directly to observable quantities and the standard Dirac CP phase $δ$.
\n\n
动机在于通过将TM$_{1,2}$（保留一列的三双最大混合）中引入的CP相位$φ_{1,2}$表示为PMNS矩阵元的规范不变组合，从而阐明其物理含义，并将其直接联系到可观测量和标准Dirac CP相位$δ$。

Method: The authors use the properties of rephasing invariants in the lepton mixing matrix. They explicitly construct the invariants corresponding to $φ_1$ and $φ_2$ from the elements of the full PMNS matrix $U$. They then relate these invariants to the standard PDG parametrization $U = U^0 \cdot \text{diag}(1, e^{i\alpha/2}, e^{i(\beta/2 + \delta)})$, deriving the connection $φ_i = δ - \arg[U_{μi}^0 U_{τi}^0]$ by comparing the two expressions.
\n\n
作者利用轻子混合矩阵中规范不变量的性质，从完整的PMNS矩阵$U$的元素显式构造出对应于$φ_1$和$φ_2$的不变量。然后，他们将这些不变量与标准PDG参数化$U = U^0 \cdot \text{diag}(1, e^{i\alpha/2}, e^{i(\beta/2 + \delta)})$联系起来，通过比较两种表达式推导出关系式$φ_i = δ - \arg[U_{μi}^0 U_{τi}^0]$。

Result: The main results are the explicit identification of the TM$_{1,2}$ CP phases as rephasing invariants: $φ_{1} = - \arg \left[ { U_{e2} U_{e3} U_{μ1} U_{τ1} / (U_{e 1} \det U) } \right]$ and $φ_{2} = - \arg \left[ { U_{e1} U_{e3} U_{μ2} U_{τ2} / (U_{e 2} \det U)} \right]$, and the derived relations $φ_{i} = δ- \arg [ U_{μi}^{0} U_{τi}^{0} ]$ which link them directly to the standard Dirac CP phase $δ$.
\n\n
主要结果是将TM$_{1,2}$ CP相位明确识别为规范不变量：$φ_{1} = - \arg \left[ { U_{e2} U_{e3} U_{μ1} U_{τ1} / (U_{e 1} \det U) } \right]$ 和 $φ_{2} = - \arg \left[ { U_{e1} U_{e3} U_{μ2} U_{τ2} / (U_{e 2} \det U)} \right]$，并推导出关系式$φ_{i} = δ- \arg [ U_{μi}^{0} U_{τi}^{0} ]$，将其直接与标准Dirac CP相位$δ$联系起来。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that the CP phases $φ_{1,2}$ in the TM$_{1,2}$ mixing schemes are not arbitrary parameters but are physically meaningful rephasing invariants, and their connection to the Dirac phase $δ$ via $φ_i = δ - \arg[U_{μi}^0 U_{τi}^0]$ provides a concrete example of a general class of sum rules relating different physical observables in the lepton sector.
\n\n
本文得出结论：TM$_{1,2}$混合方案中的CP相位$φ_{1,2}$并非任意参数，而是具有物理意义的规范不变量；它们通过$φ_i = δ - \arg[U_{μi}^0 U_{τi}^0]$与Dirac相位$δ$的联系，为轻子扇区中关联不同物理可观测量的普适求和规则提供了一个具体实例。

Abstract: We show that the CP phases $φ_{1,2}$ appearing in the TM$_{1,2}$ mixing are directly identified with rephasing invariants $φ_{1} = - \arg \left[ { U_{e2} U_{e3} U_{μ1} U_{τ1} / U_{e 1} \det U } \right]$, $φ_{2} = - \arg \left[ { U_{e1} U_{e3} U_{μ2} U_{τ2} / U_{e 2} \det U} \right]$. Furthermore, we demonstrate relations $φ_{i} = δ- \arg [ U_{μi}^{0} U_{τi}^{0} ]$ among $φ_{1,2}$, the Dirac CP phase $δ$ and matrix elements in the PDG parametrization $U^{0}$. These relations of CP phases are interpreted as specific elements of general sum rules among physical quantities.

</details>


### [76] [Systematic Study of Coupled-Channel Dynamics in Doubly Heavy Hadronic Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01177)
*Yu-Shan Ren,Guang-Juan Wang,Zhi Yang,Jia-Jun Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This work evaluates the reliability of Heavy Quark Spin Symmetry (HQSS)-based single-channel effective interactions for predicting higher double heavy tetraquark states by comparing against a coupled-channel framework. While both approaches agree for near-threshold ground states like $T_{cc}^+$ and $T_{bb}$, they diverge significantly for excited states, demonstrating that explicit coupled-channel dynamics are essential for accurate predictions beyond the lowest-lying configurations.
\n\n
本文通过将基于Heavy Quark Spin Symmetry (HQSS)的单通道有效相互作用与显式耦合通道框架进行对比，评估其在预测高激发双重重重四夸克态方面的可靠性。两种方法在近阈值基态（如$T_{cc}^+$和$T_{bb}$）上一致，但在激发态上显著分歧，表明要准确预测最低能态以外的态，必须显式处理coupled-channel dynamics。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heavy Quark Spin Symmetry (HQSS) is commonly employed to extrapolate effective hadronic interactions from known low-lying states (e.g., $T_{cc}^+$) to predict other doubly heavy tetraquarks ($T_{bb}, T_{bc}$). However, this approach typically assumes a single-channel interaction and neglects explicit coupled-channel effects, whose impact on higher-mass or excited states remains unclear. The paper aims to test the validity and limitations of this widely used HQSS-based single-channel approximation.
\n\n
Heavy Quark Spin Symmetry (HQSS) 常被用于从已知低能态（如$T_{cc}^+$）外推强子有效相互作用，以预测其他双重重重四夸克态（$T_{bb}, T_{bc}$）。然而，该方法通常假设单通道相互作用，忽略显式的coupled-channel效应，而这些效应对高能或激发态的影响尚不明确。本文旨在检验这种广泛使用的基于HQSS的单通道近似的有效性与局限性。

Method: The authors construct two schemes: Scheme I uses a single-channel effective interaction derived via HQSS from a one-boson-exchange (OBE) potential fitted to the $T_{cc}^+$ lineshape; Scheme II explicitly includes coupled-channel dynamics within the same OBE framework. Both schemes are applied to $S$-wave $D^{(*)}D^{(*)}$, $\bar{B}^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$, and $D^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$ systems with $J^{P}=1^+$. Pole positions are extracted using the complex scaling method and $T$-matrix pole analysis to identify bound states, resonances, and virtual states.
\n\n
作者构建了两种方案：Scheme I 使用通过HQSS从one-boson-exchange (OBE)势导出的单通道有效相互作用，该势通过拟合$T_{cc}^+$线形确定；Scheme II 在相同OBE框架下显式包含coupled-channel dynamics。两种方案均应用于具有$J^{P}=1^+$的$S$波$D^{(*)}D^{(*)}$、$\bar{B}^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$和$D^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$系统。利用complex scaling method和$T$-matrix pole analysis提取极点位置，以识别束缚态、共振态和虚态。

Result: Both schemes reproduce the observed $T_{cc}^+$ as an isoscalar $DD^*$ molecule with binding energy $\sim 381$ keV and predict a deeply bound isoscalar $T_{bb}$ ($40–60$ MeV), an isovector $T^\prime_{bb}$ resonance, and a virtual $T_{bc}$ state. However, for higher-lying states, Scheme I predicts bound or resonant states that either shift far from the physical region or disappear entirely in Scheme II. This demonstrates that coupled-channel effects significantly alter the pole structure beyond the threshold region.
\n\n
两种方案均重现观测到的$T_{cc}^+$作为同位旋单态$DD^*$分子，结合能约381 keV，并预测一个深度束缚的同位旋单态$T_{bb}$（40–60 MeV）、一个同位旋三重态$T^\prime_{bb}$共振态以及一个虚态$T_{bc}$。然而，对于高激发态，Scheme I 预测的束缚态或共振态在Scheme II中要么远离物理区域，要么完全消失。这表明coupled-channel效应显著改变了阈值区域之外的极点结构。

Conclusion: While HQSS-based single-channel models are sufficient for describing near-threshold ground-state tetraquarks like $T_{cc}^+$ and $T_{bb}$, they fail to reliably predict excited or higher-mass doubly heavy tetraquarks. An explicit treatment of coupled-channel dynamics is necessary for robust theoretical predictions in these cases, highlighting the limitations of naive HQSS extrapolations.
\n\n
虽然基于HQSS的单通道模型足以描述$T_{cc}^+$和$T_{bb}$等近阈值基态四夸克态，但无法可靠预测激发态或高质量双重重重四夸克态。在此类情况下，必须显式处理coupled-channel dynamics才能获得可靠的理论预言，这凸显了朴素HQSS外推法的局限性。

Abstract: Heavy Quark Spin Symmetry (HQSS) is widely use to predict heavy molecules by extending the effective interactions fitted from low-lying states to heavier sectors. In this work, we systematically investigate the reliability of this approach for higher double heavy tetraquarks by comparing a single-channel effective interaction (Scheme I) with an explicit coupled-channel dynamics framework (Scheme II). The interactions are obtained within one-boson-exchange potential model and fixed by fitting the $T_{cc}^+$ lineshape. Utilizing the complex scaling method and $T$-matrix pole analysis, we extract the possible poles in the $S$-wave $D^{(*)}D^{(*)}$, $\bar{B}^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$ and $D^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$ systems with $J^{P}=1^+$. We find that both schemes provide consistent descriptions of the lowest-lying state. This confirms isoscalar-dominated $T_{cc}$ as a predominant $DD^*$ molecule (binding energy $\sim$ 381 keV), and predicts an isoscalar deeply bound $T_{bb}$ state ($40-60$ MeV) and an isovector $T^\prime_{bb}$ resonance in the bottom sector, together with a virtual $T_{bc}$ state. In contrast, significant differences emerge for higher-lying states. The inclusion of explicit coupled-channel dynamics modifies the effective interaction and reshapes the pole structure. The states predicted as bound or resonant in the single-channel framework can be shifted far from the physical region or disappear. These results indicate that while single-channel descriptions are adequate for near-threshold states, an explicit treatment of coupled-channel dynamics is required for reliable predictions of excited doubly heavy tetraquarks.

</details>


### [77] [Probing the imaginary parts and their $q^2$ dependences for the tau $g-2$ and EDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01178)
*Xin-Yu Du,Xiao-Gang He,Zhong-Lv Huang,Chia-Wei Liu,Zi-Yue Zou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper studies the momentum-transfer ($q^2$) dependence and imaginary parts of the tau lepton's anomalous magnetic dipole moment (MDM) $a_\\tau$ and electric dipole moment (EDM) $d_\\tau$, using both SMEFT and a Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM). It shows that CP-violating interactions link $a_\\tau$ and $d_\\tau$, and proposes experimental strategies at Belle II and STCF to extract real and imaginary components, improving current bounds by over an order of magnitude and enabling direct measurement of $q^2$ evolution.
\n\n
本文研究了tau轻子反常磁偶极矩（MDM）$a_\\tau$和电偶极矩（EDM）$d_\\tau$的动量转移（$q^2$）依赖性及虚部，采用Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT)和Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM)两种方法。研究表明，CP破坏相互作用将$a_\\tau$与$d_\\tau$紧密关联，并提出了在Belle II和Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF)上提取实部与虚部的实验策略，可将当前对$a_\\tau$的限制提升一个数量级以上，并首次实现对偶极子形状因子$q^2$演化的直接测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The tau lepton's MDM $a_\\tau$ and EDM $d_\\tau$ are sensitive probes of electroweak physics and potential new physics, but current experimental constraints are weak. Unlike static dipole moments, when treated as generalized form factors, they depend on $q^2$, especially acquiring absorptive imaginary parts for timelike $q^2 > 4m_\\tau^2$. Understanding this $q^2$ dependence and its origin is crucial for interpreting future collider data and disentangling possible new physics contributions.
\n\n
tau轻子的MDM $a_\\tau$和EDM $d_\\tau$是探测电弱物理及潜在新物理的高灵敏度观测量，但目前实验限制较弱。与静态偶极矩不同，当将其视为广义形状因子时，它们依赖于$q^2$，尤其在类时区域$q^2 > 4m_\\tau^2$会获得吸收性的虚部。理解这种$q^2$依赖性及其起源对于解释未来对撞机数据、分离可能的新物理贡献至关重要。

Method: The authors employ two complementary theoretical frameworks: (1) Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), which provides a model-independent parameterization of higher-dimensional operators inducing $a_\\tau(q^2)$ and $d_\\tau(q^2)$, revealing correlations between them; and (2) a UV-complete Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM), used to explicitly compute the $q^2$ running and imaginary parts of the dipole form factors via loop diagrams. They then develop experimental observables sensitive to both real and imaginary parts of these form factors in $e^+e^- \\to \\tau^+\\tau^-$ processes at different center-of-mass energies.
\n\n
作者采用两种互补的理论框架：(1) Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT)，通过高维算符对$a_\\tau(q^2)$和$d_\\tau(q^2)$进行模型无关的参数化，揭示二者之间的关联；(2) 一个紫外完备的Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM)，通过圈图显式计算偶极形状因子的$q^2$跑动和虚部。随后，他们构建了在不同质心系能量下对$e^+e^- \\to \\tau^+\\tau^-$过程中这些形状因子实部与虚部敏感的实验观测量。

Result: In SMEFT, CP-violating operators that generate a non-zero $d_\\tau$ also contribute to $a_\\tau$, establishing a generic correlation. In the 2HDM, sizable imaginary parts and significant $q^2$ dependence arise above threshold. The proposed experimental methods allow Belle II and STCF to improve bounds on $a_\\tau$ by more than an order of magnitude. Crucially, combining data from both facilities enables the first direct extraction of the $q^2$ evolution of the dipole form factors.
\n\n
在SMEFT中，产生非零$d_\\tau$的CP破坏算符也会对$a_\\tau$有贡献，建立了普适关联。在2HDM中，阈值以上会出现显著的虚部和强烈的$q^2$依赖性。所提出的实验方法可使Belle II和STCF将$a_\\tau$的限制提升一个数量级以上。关键的是，结合两个装置的数据可首次直接提取偶极形状因子的$q^2$演化信息。

Conclusion: The $q^2$ dependence and imaginary components of tau dipole moments are theoretically well-motivated and experimentally accessible. Their measurement not only tightens constraints on $a_\\tau$ and $d_\\tau$, but also opens a new window into the energy-scale dependence of potential new physics through the form factor structure. Future $e^+e^-$ colliders like Belle II and STCF are uniquely positioned to probe this physics.
\n\n
tau偶极矩的$q^2$依赖性和虚部在理论上具有坚实基础且实验上可测。对其测量不仅能加强对$a_\\tau$和$d_\\tau$的限制，还可通过形状因子结构打开一扇探测新物理能标依赖性的新窗口。未来的$e^+e^-$对撞机如Belle II和STCF在此物理研究中具有独特优势。

Abstract: The $τ$ anomalous magnetic dipole moment (MDM) $a_τ= (g-2)_τ/2$ and electric dipole moment (EDM) $d_τ$, are precision probes of electroweak dynamics and possible new physics sources, yet both remain weakly constrained experimentally. Treated as generalized form factors, these quantities exhibit a generic $q^2$ dependence for an off-shell interacting photon. For timelike momentum transfer above the $τ^+τ^-$ threshold, $q^2 = s > 4m_τ^2$, the form factors can acquire absorptive imaginary parts. We investigate how such a $q^2$ dependence and the associated imaginary parts are generated from two complementary perspectives: the model-independent Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) and a UV-complete Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM). The effective framework reveals the intimate correlation between $a_τ$ and $d_τ$. New CP-violating interactions which generate a non-zero $d_τ$, can also generically have non-zero contributions to $a_τ$, thereby deeply linking their phenomenological studies. Within the 2HDM, we demonstrate that sizable imaginary parts and significant $q^2$ running can be generated at levels accessible by $e^+e^-$ colliders. Motivated by these features, we propose experimental methods to extract both the real and imaginary components of the dipole form factors. Utilizing these techniques, we show that Belle II and the Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) can improve current bounds on $a_τ$ by more than one order of magnitude. Finally, we highlight that combining measurements across the distinct center-of-mass energies of Belle II and STCF provides a unique, previously unexplored avenue to explicitly obtain information about the $q^2$ evolution of these dipole form factors.

</details>


### [78] [Probing pair production of long-lived scalars via an off-shell Standard-Model-like Higgs boson at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01681)
*Lei Wang,Xianbo Yu,Zeren Simon Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates the LHC collider signatures of a long-lived scalar $S$ produced via off-shell Higgs-mediated gluon fusion ($gg \to h^* \to SS$) in models with Higgs mixing. For small mixing angles, $S$ is long-lived and decays to displaced vertices (DVs) with jets. Using Monte Carlo simulations and reinterpretation of an ATLAS DV-plus-jets search, the authors show existing data excludes $m_S$ up to $\sim 230$ GeV for $hSS$ coupling $λv = 246$ GeV. Modified analyses and HL-LHC projections further enhance sensitivity across the mass–lifetime plane.
\n\n
本文研究了在具有Higgs混合的模型中，通过离壳Higgs介导的胶子融合过程（$gg \to h^* \to SS$）在LHC上产生的长寿命标量粒子$S$的对撞机信号。当混合角足够小时，$S$具有长寿命，并衰变为带喷注的位移顶点（displaced vertices, DVs）。作者利用蒙特卡洛模拟并重新解释了ATLAS对DV-plus-jets信号的已有搜索，表明现有数据已排除$m_S$高达$\sim 230$ GeV的参数区域（对应$hSS$耦合$λv = 246$ GeV）。改进的分析策略和高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）的投影将进一步扩展对质量–寿命参数空间的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the fact that many Standard Model extensions introduce a new scalar $S$ that mixes with the Higgs boson. When the mixing angle is tiny, $S$ becomes long-lived, evading traditional prompt-decay searches. However, its production through the Higgs portal—especially via off-shell Higgs exchange above the $2m_S > m_h$ threshold—remains viable and offers a unique probe into otherwise inaccessible regions of parameter space. Displaced vertex signatures provide a powerful handle to detect such long-lived particles while suppressing SM backgrounds.
\n\n
动机源于许多标准模型（Standard Model, SM）扩展引入了一个与Higgs玻色子混合的新标量粒子$S$。当混合角极小时，$S$变为长寿命粒子，从而避开传统的瞬时衰变（prompt-decay）搜索。然而，通过Higgs门户（Higgs portal）——特别是当$2m_S > m_h$时通过离壳Higgs交换——的产生机制仍然有效，并为探测原本不可及的参数空间提供了独特途径。位移顶点（displaced vertex）信号可有效压制标准模型背景，成为探测此类长寿命粒子的有力手段。

Method: The authors simulate the process $gg \to h^* \to SS$ using Monte Carlo methods, where $S$ decays within the inner detector to produce displaced vertices accompanied by jets. They reinterpret an existing ATLAS recast of a DV-plus-jets search to set exclusion limits on the $S$ mass and lifetime. Additionally, they propose a modified analysis strategy—based on the same experimental search—to optimize selection criteria and improve sensitivity. Projections for the high-luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) are also considered to assess future reach.
\n\n
作者使用蒙特卡洛方法模拟过程$gg \to h^* \to SS$，其中$S$在内探测器内衰变，产生伴随喷注的位移顶点。他们重新解释了ATLAS对DV-plus-jets搜索的已有重铸（recast），以设定对标量质量$m_S$和寿命的排除界限。此外，他们基于同一实验搜索提出了一种改进的分析策略，优化选择条件以提高灵敏度，并进一步考虑了高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）的投影以评估未来的探测能力。

Result: The current ATLAS DV-plus-jets search excludes scalar masses up to $m_S \sim 230$ GeV for a benchmark $hSS$ coupling strength $λv = 246$ GeV (corresponding to $λ \sim 1$). This significantly extends beyond the kinematic limit of the on-shell decay $h \to SS$ ($m_S < m_h/2 \approx 62.5$ GeV). The proposed modified analysis improves sensitivity, particularly for longer lifetimes, and HL-LHC projections suggest coverage of a much broader region in the $(m_S, c\tau_S)$ parameter space.
\n\n
当前ATLAS的DV-plus-jets搜索已排除标量质量高达$m_S \sim 230$ GeV的区域，对应基准$hSS$耦合强度$λv = 246$ GeV（即$λ \sim 1$）。这显著超出了在壳衰变$h \to SS$的运动学上限（$m_S < m_h/2 \approx 62.5$ GeV）。所提出的改进分析策略尤其在较长寿命区域提升了灵敏度，而高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）的投影表明可在$(m_S, c\tau_S)$参数空间中覆盖更广阔的区域。

Conclusion: Off-shell Higgs-mediated production of long-lived scalars provides a powerful and complementary channel to conventional on-shell Higgs decay searches. Displaced vertex signatures at the LHC already constrain heavy, weakly coupled scalars beyond the standard kinematic reach. With optimized analyses and increased luminosity at the HL-LHC, this approach can probe a wide range of BSM scenarios featuring Higgs-portal long-lived particles.
\n\n
通过离壳Higgs介导的长寿命标量粒子产生机制，为传统在壳Higgs衰变搜索提供了强大且互补的新通道。LHC上的位移顶点信号已经对超出标准运动学范围的重质量、弱耦合标量粒子施加了约束。结合优化的分析策略和HL-LHC更高的亮度，该方法可探测一大类通过Higgs门户引入长寿命粒子的超出标准模型（BSM）场景。

Abstract: We study the collider phenomenology of a long-lived scalar particle $S$ that arises from Higgs mixing in a broad class of Standard-Model (SM) extensions. When the mixing angle is sufficiently small, $S$ becomes long-lived, while its pair production via the Higgs portal can remain sizable. We focus on the production channel $gg \to h^* \to SS$ at the LHC, mediated by an \textit{off-shell} SM-like Higgs boson. This mechanism provides a complementary probe of $S$ in the mass region above the kinematic threshold of the conventional on-shell decay $h \to SS$, thereby extending the accessible parameter space to heavier scalars. The long-lived $S$ particles can decay inside the inner detector, leading to displaced vertices (DVs) accompanied by jets. We perform a detailed Monte Carlo simulation and reinterpret an existing recast of an ATLAS search for DV-plus-jets signatures in this scenario. We also consider a modified analysis strategy based on the same search to assess potential improvements in sensitivity. We find that the current ATLAS search already excludes a significant region of the parameter space, reaching scalar masses up to $m_S \sim 230$ GeV for a benchmark $hSS$ coupling $λv$ of $246$ GeV. The modified analysis and projections to the high-luminosity LHC further extend the sensitivity to wider regions of the mass--lifetime parameter space.

</details>


### [79] [Prediction of doubly-charm hadronic molecules with double strange quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01758)
*Fu-Lai Wang,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper explores the formation of hidden-charm hidden-strangeness and doubly-charmed doubly-strange molecular tetraquarks from $T$-doublet charmed-strange meson pairs using the one-boson-exchange model. Several bound-state candidates with exotic quantum numbers are identified, including the $D_{s1}\bar D_{s2}^*$ ($J^{PC}=2^{+-}$) and $D_{s2}^*\bar D_{s2}^*$ ($J^{PC}=4^{++}$) systems, and their doubly-charmed counterparts such as $D_{s1}D_{s1}$ ($J^P=2^+$). These states offer clean experimental signatures for exotic hadrons.

本文利用单玻色子交换模型研究了由$T$-双重态含粲奇异介子及其反粒子构成的隐粲隐奇分子型四夸克态，以及其双粲双奇异对应物。文中识别出多个具有奇特量子数的束缚态候选者，包括$D_{s1}\\bar D_{s2}^*$（$J^{PC}=2^{+-}$）和$D_{s2}^*\\bar D_{s2}^*$（$J^{PC}=4^{++}$）系统，以及双粲对应态如$D_{s1}D_{s1}$（$J^P=2^+$）。这些态为实验寻找奇特强子提供了清晰的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to identify viable exotic hadron candidates beyond the conventional quark model by exploring whether specific combinations of charmed-strange mesons can form stable molecular tetraquarks via meson-exchange forces. The focus is on systems with hidden charm and strangeness or doubly heavy flavor content, which are theoretically clean and experimentally distinguishable due to exotic $J^{PC}$ quantum numbers.
\n\n
动机在于通过探索特定的含粲奇异介子组合是否能通过介子交换力形成稳定的分子型四夸克态，从而识别出超出传统夸克模型的可行奇特强子候选者。研究聚焦于隐粲隐奇或双重重味系统，这些系统因具有奇特的$J^{PC}$量子数而在理论上更干净、实验上更易区分。

Method: The authors employ the one-boson-exchange (OBE) model to compute the interaction potentials between $T$-doublet charmed-strange mesons ($D_{s1}$ and $D_{s2}^*$) and their antiparticles. They consider S-wave interactions and solve the Schrödinger equation to determine bound-state solutions. The analysis covers both hidden-charm hidden-strangeness ($D_{s1}\bar D_{s1}$, $D_{s1}\bar D_{s2}^*$, $D_{s2}^*\bar D_{s2}^*$) and doubly-charmed doubly-strange ($D_{s1}D_{s1}$, etc.) configurations, evaluating binding energies and quantum numbers.
\n\n
作者采用单玻色子交换（OBE）模型计算$T$-双重态含粲奇异介子（$D_{s1}$和$D_{s2}^*$）与其反粒子之间的相互作用势。考虑S波相互作用并求解薛定谔方程以确定束缚态解。分析涵盖隐粲隐奇系统（如$D_{s1}\\bar D_{s1}$、$D_{s1}\\bar D_{s2}^*$、$D_{s2}^*\\bar D_{s2}^*$）和双粲双奇异系统（如$D_{s1}D_{s1}$等），评估其结合能与量子数。

Result: The study predicts several bound molecular tetraquark candidates: for hidden-charm hidden-strangeness systems, $D_{s1}\bar D_{s1}$ ($0^{++},1^{+-},2^{++}$), $D_{s1}\bar D_{s2}^*$ ($1^{+\pm},2^{+\pm},3^{+\pm}$), and $D_{s2}^*\bar D_{s2}^*$ ($0^{++},1^{+-},2^{++},3^{+-},4^{++}$); notably, the $J^{PC}=2^{+-}$ state in $D_{s1}\bar D_{s2}^*$ is exotic (forbidden for $q\bar q$ mesons). For doubly-charmed systems, $D_{s1}D_{s1}$ ($2^+$), $D_{s1}D_{s2}^*$ ($3^+$), and $D_{s2}^*D_{s2}^*$ ($4^+$) are predicted as absolutely flavor-exotic molecules.

研究预测了多个分子型四夸克束缚态候选者：在隐粲隐奇系统中，包括$D_{s1}\\bar D_{s1}$（$0^{++},1^{+-},2^{++}$）、$D_{s1}\\bar D_{s2}^*$（$1^{+\\pm},2^{+\\pm},3^{+\\pm}$）和$D_{s2}^*\\bar D_{s2}^*$（$0^{++},1^{+-},2^{++},3^{+-},4^{++}$）；其中$D_{s1}\\bar D_{s2}^*$中的$J^{PC}=2^{+-}$态具有对常规$q\\bar q$介子禁戒的奇特量子数。在双粲系统中，预测$D_{s1}D_{s1}$（$2^+$）、$D_{s1}D_{s2}^*$（$3^+$）和$D_{s2}^*D_{s2}^*$（$4^+$）为绝对味奇特的分子态。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that certain $T$-doublet charmed-strange meson pairs can form stable molecular tetraquarks with exotic quantum numbers, offering promising targets for experimental verification. The identification of states like $D_{s1}\bar D_{s2}^*$ ($2^{+-}$) and $D_{s2}^*D_{s2}^*$ ($4^+$) provides clear pathways to confirm the existence of exotic hadrons beyond the quark model, particularly in the hidden-charm and doubly-heavy sectors.

本文得出结论：某些$T$-双重态含粲奇异介子对可形成具有奇特量子数的稳定分子型四夸克态，为实验验证提供了理想目标。诸如$D_{s1}\\bar D_{s2}^*$（$2^{+-}$）和$D_{s2}^*D_{s2}^*$（$4^+$）等态的识别，为确认超出夸克模型的奇特强子（特别是在隐粲和双双重味区域）的存在提供了明确路径。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the $T$-doublet charmed-strange mesons and their antiparticles can form hidden-charm hidden-strangeness molecular tetraquarks by applying the one-boson-exchange model. We identify $D_{s1}\bar D_{s1}$ ($J^{PC}=0^{++},\,1^{+-},\,2^{++}$), $D_{s1}\bar D_{s2}^*$ ($J^{PC}=1^{+\pm},\,2^{+\pm},\,3^{+\pm}$), and $D_{s2}^*\bar D_{s2}^*$ ($J^{PC}=0^{++},\,1^{+-},\,2^{++},\,3^{+-},\,4^{++}$) as promising hidden-charm hidden-strangeness molecular tetraquark candidates. Notably, the $D_{s1}\bar D_{s2}^*$ state with $J^{PC}=2^{+-}$ possesses exotic spin-parity quantum numbers forbidden for conventional mesons, providing a clean experimental signature for exotic hadrons. Moreover, the $D_{s2}^*\bar D_{s2}^*$ state with $J^{PC}=4^{++}$ is a rare high-spin hadronic molecule. We then extend the same framework to discuss the binding properties of the $T_s T_s$ systems and construct the mass spectrum of corresponding doubly-charm doubly-strangeness molecular tetraquarks. The promising candidates are $D_{s1}D_{s1}$ ($J^P=2^+$), $D_{s1}D_{s2}^*$ ($J^P=3^+$), and $D_{s2}^*D_{s2}^*$ ($J^P=4^+$), all of which are absolutely flavor-exotic. We encourage experimental searches for these predicted hadronic molecules, which would be a crucial step toward establishing doubly-charm molecular tetraquarks with strangeness $S=0$ or $2$.

### [80] [Semileptonic Decays of $Λ\to p \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell}$ in the Light-Front Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01768)

我们使用光前夸克模型计算了半轻子衰变 $Λ\to p \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell}$（$\ell=e,μ$），在Drell-Yan-West 框架下通过Bethe-Salpeter 形式同时包含价态和非价态贡献。我们预测的分支比——电子道为 $8.32\\times 10^{-4}$，缪子道为 $1.31\\times 10^{-4}$——与BESIII 实验数据一致，表明非价态效应在光前框架下的重子半轻子衰变中不可忽略。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to accurately describe exclusive semileptonic hyperon decays $Λ\to p \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell}$ within the Standard Model by accounting for both valence and nonvalence (i.e., zero-mode or pair-creation) contributions, which are often neglected but may significantly affect form factors and observables like branching ratios. Comparison with recent BESIII measurements provides a critical test of theoretical frameworks such as the light-front quark model.
\n\n
动机在于通过计入价态和非价态（即零模或对产生）贡献，在Standard Model 内精确描述排他性半轻子超子衰变 $Λ\to p \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell}$。这些非价态贡献常被忽略，但可能显著影响形状因子及分支比等可观测量。与近期BESIII 测量结果的对比为光前夸克模型等理论框架提供了关键检验。

Method: The authors employ the light-front quark model (LFQM) combined with the Bethe-Salpeter formalism to compute the $Λ\to p$ transition form factors. They work in the Drell-Yan-West frame ($q^+=0$) and explicitly include nonvalence contributions arising from higher Fock states beyond the naive valence configuration. The hadronic matrix elements are decomposed into Lorentz-invariant form factors, which are then integrated over light-front momentum variables to yield numerical results for decay rates and branching ratios.
\n\n
作者采用光前夸克模型（LFQM）结合Bethe-Salpeter 形式来计算 $Λ\to p$ 跃迁形状因子。他们在Drell-Yan-West 框架（$q^+=0$）下工作，并显式包含来自超越朴素价态组分的高阶Fock 态的非价态贡献。强子矩阵元被分解为洛伦兹不变形状因子，随后对光前动量变量积分以获得衰变率和分支比的数值结果。

Result: Including nonvalence contributions, the calculated branching ratios are $\\mathcal{B}(Λ\\to p e^{-} \\barν_{e}) \\approx 8.32\\times 10^{-4}$ and $\\mathcal{B}(Λ\\to p μ^{-} \\barν_μ) \\approx 1.31\\times 10^{-4}$, in good agreement with the latest BESIII Collaboration measurements. The analysis demonstrates that nonvalence effects enhance the form factors and thus cannot be neglected in precision studies of baryon semileptonic decays within the light-front framework.
\n\n
计入非价态贡献后，计算得到的分支比为 $\\mathcal{B}(Λ\\to p e^{-} \\barν_{e}) \\approx 8.32\\times 10^{-4}$ 和 $\\mathcal{B}(Λ\\to p μ^{-} \\barν_μ) \\approx 1.31\\times 10^{-4}$，与BESIII Collaboration 的最新测量结果高度一致。分析表明，非价态效应增强了形状因子，因此在光前框架下对重子半轻子衰变的精密研究中不可忽略。

Conclusion: The study confirms that nonvalence contributions are essential for accurate predictions of semileptonic hyperon decays in the light-front quark model. Their inclusion brings theoretical branching ratios into alignment with experimental data from BESIII, underscoring the necessity of a complete treatment of the light-front wave function beyond the valence approximation.
\n\n
该研究证实，在光前夸克模型中，非价态贡献对半轻子超子衰变的精确预言至关重要。计入这些贡献后，理论分支比与BESIII 实验数据吻合，凸显了在价态近似之外完整处理光前波函数的必要性。

Abstract: We investigate the exclusive semileptonic decays of $Λ\to p \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell}~(\ell=e,μ)$ within the Standard Model using the light-front quark model. The transition form factor behaviors of $Λ\to p$ are obtained from the effective treatment of nonvalence contributions in addition to the valence ones in the Drell-Yan-West frame due to the Bethe-Salpeter formalism. Based on these form factors, we obtain that the branching ratios of $Λ\to p e^{-} \barν_{e}$ and $p μ^{-} \barν_μ$, including nonvalence contributions, are around $8.32\times 10^{-4}$ and $1.31\times 10^{-4}$, which are consistent with the latest measurements from the BESIII Collaboration, respectively. Our results indicate that nonvalence contributions can play a non-negligible role in the semileptonic baryon decays within the light-front framework.

</details>


### [81] [Tachyonic particle production: quantum 2PI formalism with momentum exchanging collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02039)
*Kimmo Kainulainen,Sami Nurmi,Olli Väisänen*

TL;DR: The paper develops a local quantum kinetic approximation from next-to-leading order 2PI effective action to accurately model particle production during reheating, especially capturing momentum-exchange interactions among unstable modes that Hartree approximation neglects.

本文提出了一种从次领头阶 2PI 有效作用量出发的局域量子动理学近似方案，用于精确描述再加热期间的粒子产生过程，特别捕捉了 Hartree 近似所忽略的不稳定模之间的动量交换相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard Hartree approximation within the 2PI formalism fails to account for momentum-exchanging scattering processes crucial during tachyonic preheating, leading to inaccurate modeling of non-perturbative particle production. A more complete treatment is needed to capture the full dynamics of unstable modes.

在 2PI 形式体系中，标准的 Hartree 近似无法描述快子预加热过程中关键的动量交换散射过程，导致对非微扰粒子产生的建模不准确。需要更完备的处理方法以捕捉不稳定模的完整动力学。

Method: The authors derive local quantum kinetic equations by systematically reducing the non-local next-to-leading order (NLO) 2PI equations of motion. This involves a controlled gradient expansion and careful treatment of contributions from unstable (tachyonic) modes to ensure self-consistency and energy-momentum conservation.
\n\n
作者通过系统地将非局域的次领头阶（NLO）2PI 运动方程约化，推导出局域量子动理学方程。该方法包含受控的梯度展开，并对不稳定（快子）模的贡献进行细致处理，以确保自洽性和能量-动量守恒。

Result: The resulting local quantum kinetic equations incorporate scattering terms that exchange momentum between different field modes, going beyond the mean-field-like Hartree approximation. This enables accurate numerical simulation of tachyonic preheating with standard kinetic solvers while retaining essential non-perturbative physics.

所得局域量子动理学方程包含了不同场模之间交换动量的散射项，超越了类平均场的 Hartree 近似。这使得可以使用标准动理学求解器对快子预加热进行精确数值模拟，同时保留关键的非微扰物理。

Conclusion: A consistent local kinetic framework derived from NLO 2PI captures essential momentum-transfer interactions during tachyonic instabilities, offering a practical and accurate tool for studying non-equilibrium particle production in early universe cosmology.

从 NLO 2PI 推导出的自洽局域动理学框架能够捕捉快子不稳定期间关键的动量转移相互作用，为研究早期宇宙中的非平衡粒子产生提供了一个实用且精确的工具。

Abstract: Oscillating spacetime curvature can drive particle production during reheating, whose accurate modeling requires the use of non-perturbative out-of-equilibrium methods. Tachyonic instabilities have previously been studied using 2-Particle Irreducible (2PI) formalism in the Hartree approximation, which however misses important momentum exchanging interactions. We present a self-consistent approximation scheme for reducing the non-local next-to-leading order 2PI equations of motion to local quantum kinetic equations, which can be solved with standard methods. We pay special attention to interactions involving unstable modes during tachyonic instabilities.

</details>


### [82] [Probing the dark axion portal via $J/ψ$ decays at BESIII and STCF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01791)
*Zeren Simon Wang,Dazhuang He,Yu Zhang*

TL;DR: This paper explores the potential of BESIII and STCF experiments to probe the dark axion portal via rare $J/ψ \to a + \gamma'$ decays, using mono-photon signatures. Monte Carlo simulations show that current BESIII data can already constrain unexplored regions in the coupling $G_{a\gamma\gamma'}$ versus ALP and dark photon masses, and future STCF data could improve sensitivity by about an order of magnitude.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the theoretical possibility of connecting Standard Model particles with dark sector states through the dark axion portal, which involves couplings between an axionlike particle (ALP) $a$, a dark photon $\gamma'$, and photons. Resonant $J/\psi$ production at $\sqrt{s} = 3.097$ GeV provides a clean environment to search for rare decays into these dark sector particles, offering a unique opportunity to explore previously inaccessible parameter space.
\n\n
动机源于dark axion portal理论框架下标准模型粒子与暗区态之间的可能联系，该框架涉及轴子类粒子（ALP）$a$、暗光子$\gamma'$与光子之间的耦合。在质心能量$\sqrt{s} = 3.097$ GeV处共振产生$J/\psi$介子，为寻找这些暗区粒子的稀有衰变提供了干净的实验环境，从而为探索此前无法触及的参数空间提供了独特机会。

Method: The authors perform Monte Carlo simulations of the signal process $J/\psi \to a + \gamma' \to \text{invisible} + \gamma$ (mono-photon final state), incorporating realistic detector effects and known backgrounds. They analyze the existing BESIII dataset and project the sensitivity of the future STCF experiment to set exclusion limits on the dark axion portal coupling $G_{a\gamma\gamma'}$ as a function of the ALP mass $m_a$ and dark photon mass $m_{\gamma'}$.
\n\n
作者对信号过程 $J/\psi \to a + \gamma' \to \text{invisible} + \gamma$（单光子末态）进行了蒙特卡洛模拟，纳入了实际探测器效应和已知本底。他们分析了现有的BESIII数据集，并预测了未来STCF实验对dark axion portal耦合 $G_{a\gamma\gamma'}$ 的排除灵敏度，该灵敏度是轴子类粒子质量 $m_a$ 和暗光子质量 $m_{\gamma'}$ 的函数。

Result: The analysis shows that the current BESIII dataset can exclude previously unprobed regions in the $(m_a, m_{\gamma'}, G_{a\gamma\gamma'})$ parameter space of the dark axion portal. The projected STCF sensitivity is expected to improve upon BESIII limits by approximately one order of magnitude across a wide range of ALP and dark photon masses.
\n\n
分析表明，当前BESIII数据集可以在dark axion portal的 $(m_a, m_{\gamma'}, G_{a\gamma\gamma'})$ 参数空间中排除此前未被探测的区域。预计STCF在未来可将BESIII的限制提高约一个数量级，覆盖广泛的ALP和暗光子质量范围。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that $e^+e^-$ colliders operating at the $J/\psi$ resonance, such as BESIII and STCF, are powerful probes of the dark axion portal through mono-photon searches. Existing data already provide competitive constraints, and future facilities like STCF will significantly extend the reach into theoretically motivated dark sector scenarios.
\n\n
本研究表明，在$J/\psi$共振能区运行的正负电子对撞机（如BESIII和STCF）通过单光子搜寻，是探测dark axion portal的有力工具。现有数据已提供具有竞争力的限制，而STCF等未来装置将显著拓展对理论动机充分的暗区场景的探测能力。

Abstract: Large numbers of $J/ψ$ mesons can be resonantly produced at BESIII and STCF at the center-of-mass energy $\sqrt{s}=3.097$ GeV. Such $J/ψ$ mesons may undergo rare decays into an axionlike particle (ALP) $a$ and a dark photon $γ'$ in the theoretical framework of the dark axion portal. In this work, we investigate the exclusion reach of the existing BESIII dataset together with the projected sensitivity of STCF, focusing on the mono-photon signature. We perform Monte Carlo simulations and estimate the exclusion reach in the portal coupling $G_{aγγ'}$ as a function of the ALP and dark-photon masses, taking background events into account. Our results indicate that the existing BESIII dataset already has exclusion sensitivity to previously unexplored regions of the dark axion portal parameter space, while the future STCF can further improve the sensitivity by roughly an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [83] [Anomaly flow and anomaly cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01829)
*Yutaka Hosotani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: In a 5D Randall-Sundrum orbifold gauge-Higgs unification model based on $SO(5) \times U(1) \times SU(3)$, the total chiral anomalies of 4D gauge fields—including all Kaluza-Klein (KK) fermion modes—are shown to be independent of bulk fermion mass parameters and the Aharonov-Bohm phase $θ_H$, and instead depend only on the values of $W$ and $Z$ boson wave functions at the UV and IR branes. Anomaly cancellation holds generation by generation even for non-zero $θ_H$.
\n\n
在基于 $SO(5) \\times U(1) \\times SU(3)$ 的 5D Randall-Sundrum 轨形空间中的 Gauge-Higgs 统一模型中，4D 规范场的总手征反常（包含所有 Kaluza-Klein 费米子模）被证明与体费米子质量参数和 Aharonov-Bohm 相位 $θ_H$ 无关，而仅依赖于 $W$ 和 $Z$ 玻色子波函数在 RS 空间紫外（UV）和红外（IR）边界上的取值。即使在 $θ_H \\neq 0$ 的情况下，每一代费米子的规范反常也能逐代抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In 5D orbifold gauge theories, the effective 4D chiral anomaly depends on the Aharonov-Bohm (AB) phase $θ_H$ associated with the Wilson line along the compact fifth dimension. This raises concerns about potential inconsistency due to anomaly non-conservation as $θ_H$ varies. The paper aims to resolve this issue in the context of a realistic warped extra-dimensional GUT-inspired model—specifically, the $SO(5) \times U(1) \times SU(3)$ gauge-Higgs unification in the Randall-Sundrum (RS) spacetime—by demonstrating that when contributions from the full Kaluza-Klein tower are included, the total anomaly becomes universal and anomaly cancellation is preserved per generation.
\n\n
在 5D 轨形规范理论中，有效 4D 手征反常依赖于沿紧致第五维的 Wilson 线所对应的 Aharonov-Bohm（AB）相位 $θ_H$。这引发了对 $θ_H$ 变化时反常不守恒可能导致理论不自洽的担忧。本文旨在解决这一问题，具体针对一个现实的弯曲额外维 GUT 启发型模型——即 Randall-Sundrum（RS）时空中的 $SO(5) \\times U(1) \\times SU(3)$ Gauge-Higgs 统一模型——通过证明当包含完整的 Kaluza-Klein 塔贡献时，总反常具有普适性，并且每一代都能实现反常抵消。

Method: The authors compute the total chiral anomaly by summing over all Kaluza-Klein (KK) excited modes of 5D fermions in the $SO(5) \times U(1) \times SU(3)$ gauge-Higgs unification model embedded in the Randall-Sundrum warped geometry. They utilize the dependence of 4D fermion couplings on bulk mass parameters and the AB phase $θ_H$, and relate the anomaly coefficient to boundary values of the $W$ and $Z$ gauge boson wave functions at the ultraviolet (UV) and infrared (IR) branes. The calculation explicitly includes the full KK spectrum to demonstrate universality and anomaly cancellation.
\n\n
作者通过对嵌入 Randall-Sundrum 弯曲几何中的 $SO(5) \\times U(1) \\times SU(3)$ Gauge-Higgs 统一模型中所有 5D 费米子的 Kaluza-Klein（KK）激发模求和，计算总手征反常。他们利用了 4D 费米子耦合对体质量参数和 AB 相位 $θ_H$ 的依赖性，并将反常系数与 $W$ 和 $Z$ 规范玻色子波函数在紫外（UV）和红外（IR）边界上的取值联系起来。计算显式包含了完整的 KK 谱，以证明反常的普适性和抵消机制。

Result: The total anomaly, including contributions from all Kaluza-Klein modes, is found to be independent of both the bulk fermion mass parameters and the Aharonov-Bohm phase $θ_H$. Instead, it is universally expressed in terms of the values of the $W$ and $Z$ boson wave functions evaluated at the UV and IR branes. Crucially, gauge anomalies cancel exactly within each fermion generation, even when $θ_H \not= 0$, ensuring consistency of the effective 4D theory across the full range of the AB phase.
\n\n
包含所有 Kaluza-Klein 模贡献的总反常被发现与体费米子质量参数和 Aharonov-Bohm 相位 $θ_H$ 均无关。相反，它普适地由 $W$ 和 $Z$ 玻色子波函数在 UV 和 IR 边界上的取值表达。关键的是，即使在 $θ_H \\neq 0$ 的情况下，规范反常也能在每一代费米子内部精确抵消，从而确保有效 4D 理论在整个 AB 相位范围内的一致性。

Conclusion: The paper establishes that in the considered RS-based gauge-Higgs unification model, the apparent dependence of 4D chiral anomalies on the AB phase and bulk masses is an artifact of truncating the Kaluza-Klein tower. When the full tower is accounted for, anomalies become universal and cancel per generation, preserving gauge invariance and quantum consistency. This result strengthens the viability of gauge-Higgs unification in warped extra dimensions as a consistent framework beyond the Standard Model.
\n\n
本文表明，在所研究的基于 RS 的 Gauge-Higgs 统一模型中，4D 手征反常对 AB 相位和体质量的表观依赖性源于对 Kaluza-Klein 塔的截断。当计入完整 KK 塔后，反常变得普适，并逐代抵消，从而保持规范不变性和量子一致性。这一结果增强了弯曲额外维中 Gauge-Higgs 统一作为超越标准模型的一致性框架的可行性。

Abstract: In gauge theory on 5D orbifolds the magnitude of chiral anomalies of 4D gauge fields changes with the value of the Aharonov-Bohm (AB) phase $θ_H$ in the fifth dimension. Anomaly flows with the AB phase. In particular in the Randall-Sundrum (RS) warped space gauge couplings of 4D fermions depend on bulk mass parameters of 5D fermion multiplets. We show that in the GUT-inspired $SO(5) \times U(1) \times SU(3)$ gauge-Higgs unification model in the RS warped space the total anomalies including contributions of all Kaluza-Klein excited modes of fermions become universal, being independent of the values of the bulk mass parameters of fermions and expressed in terms of the values of $W$ and $Z$ wave functions at the ultraviolet and infrared branes in the RS space. It is shown that cancellation of gauge anomalies is achieved in each generation even for $θ_H \not= 0$.

</details>


### [84] [Reinterpreting the ATLAS HHH$\to 6b$ Search with CheckMATE and Rivet: Validation, TRSM Benchmarks, and HL-LHC Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01966)
*Tomasz Procter,Krzysztof Rolbiecki,Andrzej Siódmok*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper implements the ATLAS search for triple Higgs boson production in a six $b$-jet final state into the CheckMATE and Rivet frameworks. It validates the deep neural network inputs and exclusion limits against ATLAS data in both the Standard Model and a Two-Real-Singlet extension (TRSM), and provides projected HL-LHC sensitivities under various systematic uncertainties, including new TRSM benchmarks.
\n\n
本文将ATLAS合作组对六$b$-喷注末态中三希格斯玻色子产生过程的搜索实现在CheckMATE和Rivet框架中。作者利用ATLAS提供的丰富验证材料，对深度神经网络输入特征及在Standard Model（标准模型）和Two-Real-Singlet Model（TRSM，双实单态标量扩展模型）中的排除限进行了全面验证，并在多种系统误差情形下给出了High Luminosity LHC（高亮度LHC）的预期灵敏度，包括ATLAS未考虑的新TRSM基准模型的投影排除限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to enable reinterpretation and independent validation of the ATLAS triple Higgs search by embedding it into widely used public tools (CheckMATE and Rivet). This allows the broader theory community to test BSM scenarios—particularly the TRSM—against this complex, DNN-based analysis without relying solely on official results, and to project future sensitivities at HL-LHC.
\n\n
动机是将ATLAS的三希格斯玻色子搜索嵌入广泛使用的公共工具（CheckMATE和Rivet）中，以实现对该分析的重新解释和独立验证。这使得更广泛的理论界能够在不完全依赖官方结果的情况下，针对这一基于深度神经网络（DNN）的复杂分析检验超出标准模型（BSM）的场景——特别是TRSM模型——并预测HL-LHC阶段的未来灵敏度。

Method: The authors implement the full ATLAS analysis chain—including object definitions, event selection via a deep neural network (DNN), and statistical interpretation using the HS3 format—into CheckMATE and Rivet. They validate DNN input distributions and observed exclusion limits using ATLAS-provided validation material. Projections for HL-LHC are made by scaling luminosity and varying systematic uncertainties, applied to both SM and TRSM benchmark points.
\n\n
作者将完整的ATLAS分析流程——包括对象定义、通过深度神经网络（DNN）进行的事件选择，以及基于HS3格式的统计解释——实现在CheckMATE和Rivet中。他们利用ATLAS提供的验证材料对DNN输入分布和观测到的排除限进行验证。通过对光度进行缩放并改变系统误差，对标准模型（SM）和TRSM基准点进行HL-LHC阶段的灵敏度投影。

Result: The implementation successfully reproduces ATLAS exclusion limits in the SM and TRSM. Validation confirms fidelity of DNN input features. Projected HL-LHC limits show improved sensitivity, with new exclusion contours presented for TRSM benchmark models not studied by ATLAS, under several assumptions about systematic uncertainties.
\n\n
该实现在标准模型和TRSM中成功复现了ATLAS的排除限。验证确认了DNN输入特征的保真度。HL-LHC阶段的投影显示灵敏度提升，在多种系统误差假设下，给出了ATLAS未研究的TRSM基准模型的新排除边界。

Conclusion: This work provides a validated, public implementation of a state-of-the-art DNN-driven Higgs search, enabling robust BSM phenomenology studies in the triple Higgs sector. It demonstrates the feasibility of projecting HL-LHC performance and extends coverage to new TRSM parameter space, enhancing the utility of experimental results for theoretical model testing.
\n\n
本工作提供了一个经过验证的、公开的前沿DNN驱动希格斯搜索实现，使三希格斯扇区中的BSM唯象学研究更加可靠。它展示了HL-LHC性能投影的可行性，并将覆盖范围扩展至新的TRSM参数空间，从而增强了实验结果对理论模型检验的实用性。

Abstract: We present an implementation in CheckMATE and Rivet of the ATLAS collaboration search for triple Higgs boson production in a six $b$-jets final state. The search relies on event selection using a deep neural network and a statistical model based on the HS3 format. Owing to a rich validation material provided by ATLAS, we perform a thorough validation of neural network input features and exclusion limits in the Standard Model and its extension with two additional singlet scalar fields (TRSM). Finally, we discuss expected performance of the search at the High Luminosity LHC in several scenarios of systematic uncertainty. We present projected exclusion limits for a set of TRSM benchmark models beyond those considered by ATLAS.

</details>


### [85] [The $μ-τ$ Counter Reflection Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02034)

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper is motivated by the challenge of explaining the observed pattern of neutrino masses—particularly the viability of an inverted mass hierarchy—and the desire to embed such a pattern into a predictive, symmetry-based theoretical framework that also satisfies current experimental constraints.

本文的动机源于解释观测到的中微子质量模式（尤其是倒置质量谱的可行性）的挑战，并希望将这种模式嵌入一个具有预测能力的、基于对称性的理论框架中，同时满足当前实验约束。

Method: The authors propose a novel discrete flavor symmetry acting on the neutrino mass matrix, leading to a specific texture. This texture is explicitly realized using the non-Abelian discrete group $\\Delta(27)$ in a minimal model, likely involving specific scalar field alignments and fermion representations to generate the desired mass structure.

作者提出一种作用于中微子质量矩阵的新颖离散味对称性，从而导出特定的质量结构（texture）。该结构在极小模型中通过非阿贝尔离散群 $\\Delta(27)$ 显式实现，可能涉及特定的标量场取向和费米子表示以生成所需的质量形式。
