所提出的对称性导出了与倒置质量谱兼容的中微子质量矩阵，预言了与当前振荡数据一致的特定混合角和CP破坏相位，并可能相比一般参数化减少了自由参数的数量。

Conclusion: The $\\Delta(27)$-based symmetry provides a theoretically compelling and phenomenologically viable mechanism for generating an inverted neutrino mass hierarchy, demonstrating that discrete flavor symmetries remain a powerful tool for understanding lepton flavor structure.
\n\n
基于 $\\Delta(27)$ 的对称性为生成倒置中微子质量谱提供了一个理论上合理且唯象可行的机制，表明离散味对称性仍是理解轻子味结构的有力工具。

Abstract: A novel symmetry for the neutrino mass matrix is proposed that naturally accommodates an inverted mass hierarchy while offering additional phenomenological advantages. The corresponding texture can be realized within a minimal framework based on $Δ(27)$ symmetry.

</details>


### [86] [Interpreting Light Scalar Excesses and Heavy Scalar Cascades in the $μ$-Term Extended NMSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02069)
*Jingwei Lian*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates the persistent $95~{\rm GeV}$ scalar excesses seen at LEP ($b\bar b$) and LHC (diphoton) within the $μ$-term extended Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model ($μ$NMSSM). It identifies viable parameter regions consistent with all current constraints that explain these anomalies via a singlet-like CP-even scalar. Two distinct scenarios are found: one enhancing the diphoton rate via suppressed $h_s b\bar b$ coupling, and another boosting the LEP signal through enhanced doublet mixing. The model also predicts heavy Higgs cascades ($H \to h h_s$, $H \to h_s h_s$) and a CP-odd decay channel ($A_2 \to h A_1 \to 4b$) that may relate to a CMS $4b$ excess near $(600,400)~{\rm GeV}$. In the positive-$μ$ regime, it naturally accommodates a gravitino LSP with a long-lived neutralino NLSP.
\n\n
本文在引入$μ$项的Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model（$μ$NMSSM）框架下研究了LEP（$b\\bar b$道）和LHC（双光子道）中持续存在的$95~{\\rm GeV}$标量共振迹象。在满足所有现有实验约束的前提下，作者找到了两类可行参数区域：一类通过压低$h_s b\\bar b$耦合增强双光子信号率，另一类通过增强双态混合提升LEP信号率。模型还预言了重CP偶标量的级联衰变（$H \\to h h_s$、$H \\to h_s h_s$）以及CP奇标量衰变道（$A_2 \\to h A_1 \\to 4b$），后者可能与CMS在$(600,400)~{\\rm GeV}$附近观测到的$4b$末态最大局域偏差相关。在正$μ$情形下，该框架自然允许gravitino作为最轻超对称粒子（LSP），而次轻中性ino（neutralino NLSP）在对撞机尺度上通常具有长寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from two persistent experimental anomalies: (1) a $\sim 95~{\rm GeV}$ excess in LEP’s $b\bar b$ channel and LHC diphoton searches, and (2) recent CMS constraints on heavy resonances decaying to $b\bar b b\bar b$, which disfavor certain interpretations of extended Higgs sectors. The $μ$NMSSM is motivated as a well-motivated BSM framework where a light singlet-like CP-even scalar can naturally sit near $95~{\rm GeV}$ while heavier doublet-like states can be probed by current LHC searches. The goal is to determine whether this model can simultaneously accommodate the $95~{\rm GeV}$ signals and evade all other experimental bounds.
\n\n
动机源于两个持续存在的实验异常：(1) LEP的$b\\bar b$道和LHC双光子道中约$95~{\\rm GeV}$的超出；(2) CMS近期对$b\\bar b b\\bar b$末态中重共振态的限制，这排除了某些扩展Higgs sector的解释。$μ$NMSSM作为一个理论上有良好动机的BSM框架，其轻的单态类CP偶标量可自然位于$95~{\\rm GeV}$附近，而较重的双态类标量则处于当前LHC探测范围内。本文旨在检验该模型是否能在满足所有其他实验限制的同时，一致地解释$95~{\\rm GeV}$信号。

Method: The authors perform a comprehensive scan of the $μ$NMSSM parameter space, imposing a wide array of theoretical and experimental constraints: Higgs signal strength measurements (including the $95~{\rm GeV}$ excesses), direct searches for additional Higgs bosons, flavor physics observables (e.g., $B$-meson decays), electroweak precision data (S, T parameters), and limits from direct SUSY particle searches. They identify benchmark points that fit the $95~{\rm GeV}$ diphoton and $b\bar b$ rates within $2σ$. They then analyze the properties of the heavier CP-even ($H$) and CP-odd ($A_2$) scalars, computing branching ratios for cascade decays like $H \to h h_s$, $H \to h_s h_s$, and $A_2 \to h A_1 \to 4b$, comparing them with current CMS limits and excesses. The collider phenomenology of the SUSY spectrum, particularly the nature of the LSP/NLSP in the positive-$μ$ scenario, is also examined.
\n\n
作者对$μ$NMSSM参数空间进行了全面扫描，并施加了广泛的理论与实验约束：Higgs信号强度测量（包括$95~{\\rm GeV}$超出）、额外Higgs玻色子的直接搜索、味物理观测量（如$B$介子衰变）、电弱精密观测量（S、T参数）以及直接超对称粒子搜索的限制。他们筛选出能在$2σ$范围内拟合$95~{\\rm GeV}$双光子和$b\\bar b$信号率的基准点，进而分析重CP偶标量（$H$）和CP奇标量（$A_2$）的性质，计算级联衰变分支比（如$H \\to h h_s$、$H \\to h_s h_s$和$A_2 \\to h A_1 \\to 4b$），并与当前CMS限制及超出进行比较。此外，还考察了超对称谱的对撞机唯象学，特别是正$μ$情形下LSP/NLSP的性质。

Result: Two distinct viable scenarios are identified that explain the $95~{\rm GeV}$ excesses: (i) a suppressed $h_s b\bar b$ coupling enhances the diphoton rate relative to $b\bar b$, and (ii) enhanced mixing between Higgs doublets boosts the LEP $b\bar b$ production cross-section. The model predicts observable cascade decays of the heavy CP-even scalar $H$ into lighter scalars ($h h_s$ or $h_s h_s$), with rates just below current CMS $γγb\bar b$ limits but within reach of future runs. Additionally, the CP-odd scalar $A_2$ can decay via $A_2 \to h A_1 \to 4b$, populating the $(m_{A_2}, m_{A_1}) \approx (600, 400)~{\rm GeV}$ region where CMS observes its largest local deviation in the $4b$ channel, while staying under existing bounds. In the positive-$μ$ regime, the lightest supersymmetric particle is a gravitino, and the next-to-lightest is a neutralino with a macroscopic decay length at colliders.
\n\n
研究识别出两类能解释$95~{\\rm GeV}$超出的可行机制：(i) 压低$h_s b\\bar b$耦合，使双光子信号率相对于$b\\bar b$增强；(ii) 增强Higgs双态间的混合，提升LEP的$b\\bar b$产生截面。模型预言重CP偶标量$H$可通过级联衰变（$h h_s$或$h_s h_s$）产生可观测信号，其事例率略低于当前CMS在$γγb\\bar b$道的上限，但有望在未来运行中被探测到。此外，CP奇标量$A_2$可通过$A_2 \\to h A_1 \\to 4b$衰变，在$(m_{A_2}, m_{A_1}) \\approx (600, 400)~{\\rm GeV}$区域产生信号，该区域正是CMS在$4b$道观测到最大局域偏差之处，同时仍满足现有实验限制。在正$μ$情形下，最轻超对称粒子为gravitino，次轻粒子为中性ino，在对撞机上具有宏观衰变长度。

Conclusion: The $μ$NMSSM provides a theoretically consistent and phenomenologically viable framework that simultaneously explains the $95~{\rm GeV}$ LEP and LHC excesses while satisfying all current experimental constraints. It predicts correlated signals in multiple Higgs decay channels—including heavy Higgs cascades and exotic $4b$ final states—that are testable in ongoing and future LHC runs. Moreover, in the positive-$μ$ case, it links these Higgs sector anomalies to a compelling dark matter and collider scenario featuring a gravitino LSP and a long-lived neutralino NLSP, offering a rich target for displaced vertex and missing energy searches.
\n\n
$μ$NMSSM提供了一个理论自洽且现象学可行的框架，能够同时解释$95~{\\rm GeV}$处的LEP与LHC超出，并满足所有当前实验约束。该模型预言了多个Higgs衰变道中的关联信号——包括重Higgs级联衰变和奇异的$4b$末态——这些信号可在当前及未来的LHC运行中被检验。此外，在正$μ$情形下，该模型将Higgs sector异常与一个引人注目的暗物质及对撞机唯象图景联系起来：gravitino作为LSP，中性ino作为长寿命NLSP，为位移顶点和缺失能量搜索提供了丰富目标。

Abstract: The hints for a scalar resonance near $95~{\rm GeV}$ in the LEP $b\bar b$ channel and in the LHC diphoton searches remain among the most persistent small deviations in the Higgs sector. At the same time, searches for a heavier resonance decaying into the observed Higgs boson and an additional scalar have become more restrictive, especially after the recent CMS analysis of the $b\bar b b\bar b$ final state. We study these observations in the $μ$-term extended Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model ($μ$NMSSM), where a singlet-like CP-even scalar can lie near $95~{\rm GeV}$ and the heavier doublet states can appear around the mass range probed by the CMS searches. After imposing Higgs data, extra Higgs limits, flavor constraints, electroweak precision observables and direct SUSY search bounds, we find viable regions that can accommodate the $95~{\rm GeV}$ diphoton and $b\bar b$ rates within the $2σ$ ranges. The viable points fall into two characteristic patterns. One gives a larger diphoton signal through a suppressed $h_s b\bar b$ coupling, while the other gives a larger LEP rate through stronger doublet mixing. The heavy CP-even scalar can generate $H\to h h_s$ and $H\to h_s h_s$ cascades with rates below the original CMS best-fit value in $γγb\bar b$, but close to the reach of present and future searches. The CP-odd sector provides an additional channel, $A_2\to h A_1\to 4b$, which can populate the mass region around $(600,400)~{\rm GeV}$ where the latest CMS $4b$ analysis finds its largest local deviation, while remaining below its current limits. For the positive-$μ$ subset, the same framework admits a gravitino as the lightest supersymmetric particle (LSP), with a neutralino next-to-LSP that is typically long-lived at collider scales.

</details>


### [87] [Semi-analytical two-loop QCD corrections to $e^+e^-\to J/ψ+χ_{cJ}$ at B factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02182)
*Hao-Yang Liu,Cong Li,Wen-Long Sang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper computes the NNLO QCD corrections to $e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$ ($J=0,1,2$) at B factories using NRQCD factorization. Asymptotic expansions in the parameter $r = 16m_c^2/s$ around $r=0$ and $r=1$ yield highly accurate helicity amplitudes (relative error $<10^{-5}$). The $\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$ corrections significantly reduce scale uncertainties, with notable cancellations for $\\chi_{c2}$. Predictions for unpolarized cross sections agree with Belle and BaBar data for $\\chi_{c0}$, while a significant tension is found in the angular parameter $\\alpha_\\theta^0$.
\n\n
本文在NRQCD因子化框架下计算了B工厂中过程$e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$（$J=0,1,2$）的NNLO QCD修正。通过对参数$r = 16m_c^2/s$在$r=0$和$r=1$附近的渐近展开，获得了高精度的螺旋度振幅（相对误差$<10^{-5}$）。$\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$修正显著降低了能标不确定性，其中$\\chi_{c2}$通道存在显著抵消效应。对无极化截面的预言与Belle和BaBar对$\\chi_{c0}$的测量结果一致，但在角分布参数$\\alpha_\\theta^0$上与Belle数据存在显著张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Precise theoretical predictions for $e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$ are essential for testing Non-Relativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization and understanding heavy quarkonium production mechanisms. Previous calculations were limited to leading or next-to-leading order, leaving large theoretical uncertainties from missing higher-order QCD corrections and uncontrolled scale dependence. The observed discrepancies between earlier theoretical predictions and experimental data—especially in angular distributions—motivate a complete NNLO analysis to clarify the situation and provide robust benchmarks for future experiments.
\n\n
对过程$e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$进行精确的理论预言对于检验非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架和理解重夸克偶素产生机制至关重要。此前的计算仅限于领头阶（LO）或次领头阶（NLO），导致因缺失高阶QCD修正和不可控的能标依赖性而存在较大的理论不确定性。早期理论预言与实验数据（尤其是在角分布方面）之间的明显差异，促使作者开展完整的NNLO分析，以澄清现状并为未来实验提供可靠的基准。

Method: The authors employ the NRQCD factorization formalism to separate short-distance coefficients (computable perturbatively) from long-distance matrix elements. They compute helicity amplitudes for $e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$ up to $\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$ using asymptotic expansion techniques in the small parameters $r = 16m_c^2/s \\to 0$ (high-energy limit) and $r \\to 1$ (threshold limit). These two asymptotic series are then combined to approximate the full amplitude over the entire physical range $0 \\le r \\le 1$. Large logarithmic terms are resummed analytically. Unpolarized cross sections and angular distribution parameters $\\alpha_\\theta^J$—which depend only on perturbative coefficients—are derived from the squared amplitudes.
\n\n
作者采用NRQCD因子化形式，将可微扰计算的短程系数与长程矩阵元分离。他们利用渐近展开技术，在$r = 16m_c^2/s \\to 0$（高能极限）和$r \\to 1$（阈值极限）两个极限下，计算了$e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$过程直至$\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$阶的螺旋度振幅。随后将这两个渐近级数组合，以在整个物理区间$0 \\le r \\le 1$内高精度重构完整振幅。大的对数项被解析地求和。无极化截面和仅依赖于微扰系数的角分布参数$\\alpha_\\theta^J$由振幅模方导出。

Result: The NNLO corrections are substantial: for $\\chi_{c0}$, the $\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$ correction is $+33\\%$ of the LO cross section, greatly reducing scale uncertainty. For $\\chi_{c1}$, $\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s)$ and $\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$ corrections are $+35\\%$ and $-15\\%$, respectively. For $\\chi_{c2}$, they are $+25\\%$ and $-38\\%$, with strong cancellation bringing the NNLO result close to LO. The predicted $\\chi_{c0}$ cross section agrees with Belle data and is within $2\\sigma$ of BaBar. However, a sharp discrepancy is found in the predicted angular parameter $\\alpha_\\theta^0$ versus Belle measurement. The parameters $\\alpha_\\theta^{1,2}$ are predicted precisely as they are free of nonperturbative inputs.
\n\n
NNLO修正效应显著：对于$\\chi_{c0}$，$\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$修正为LO截面的$+33\\%$，大幅降低了能标不确定性。对于$\\chi_{c1}$，$\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s)$和$\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha_s^2)$修正分别为$+35\\%$和$-15\\%$。对于$\\chi_{c2}$，相应修正为$+25\\%$和$-38\\%$，强抵消效应使NNLO结果接近LO预言。对$\\chi_{c0}$截面的预言与Belle数据一致，且在$2\\sigma$内符合BaBar结果。然而，理论预言的角分布参数$\\alpha_\\theta^0$与Belle测量存在显著偏差。由于$\\alpha_\\theta^{1,2}$不依赖非微扰输入，其理论预言具有高精度。

Conclusion: The inclusion of NNLO QCD corrections stabilizes theoretical predictions for $e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$, significantly reducing scale dependence and improving agreement with cross section data for $\\chi_{c0}$. The striking tension in $\\alpha_\\theta^0$ suggests either underestimated experimental uncertainties, missing higher-order effects beyond current NNLO, or potential limitations of the NRQCD factorization framework itself. Future precise measurements of $\\alpha_\\theta^{1,2}$ will serve as clean tests of QCD dynamics in quarkonium production, as these observables are purely perturbative within NRQCD.
\n\n
包含NNLO QCD修正后，$e^+e^- \\to J/\\psi + \\chi_{cJ}$过程的理论预言趋于稳定，能标依赖性显著降低，且与$\\chi_{c0}$截面数据的吻合度提高。$\\alpha_\\theta^0$上的显著张力可能源于实验误差低估、当前NNLO之外的高阶效应缺失，或NRQCD因子化框架本身的局限性。未来对$\\alpha_\\theta^{1,2}$的精确测量将构成对夸克偶素产生过程中QCD动力学的干净检验，因为这些观测量在NRQCD框架下完全是微扰的。

Abstract: In this work, we compute the next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to the process $e^+e^-\to J/ψ+χ_{cJ}$ at B factories within the NRQCD factorization framework. The helicity amplitudes are obtained via asymptotic expansions around $r=0$ and $r=1$, with $r=16m_c^2/s$. Our asymptotic expressions reproduce the exact numerical results with high accuracy across the entire range $0\le r \le 1$, achieving a relative error below $10^{-5}$, which is sufficient for phenomenological applications. Notably, the large logarithmic terms are obtained analytically. We compute the unpolarized cross sections. The $\mathcal{O}(α_s)$ correction is found to be large, while the $\mathcal{O}(α_s^2)$ correction for $χ_{c0}$ production amounts to $33\%$ of the leading-order (LO) cross section, significantly reducing the scale uncertainties. For $χ_{c1}$, the $\mathcal{O}(α_s)$ and $\mathcal{O}(α_s^2)$ corrections correspond to $35\%$ and $-15\%$, respectively. For $χ_{c2}$, the corresponding corrections are $25\%$ and $-38\%$. The large cancellation between the corrections for $χ_{c2}$ brings the NNLO cross section close to the LO prediction. Our prediction for $χ_{c0}$ is consistent with the {\tt Belle} measurement and agrees with the {\tt BaBar} data within $2σ$. We also predict the angular distribution parameters $α^J_θ$, which are independent of nonperturbative inputs. A sharp discrepancy between the theory and the {\tt Belle} measurement is observed for $α^0_θ$, calling for further experimental and theoretical investigations. Moreover, future measurements of the angular distribution parameters for $χ_{c1}$ and $χ_{c2}$ will provide important tests of the theoretical framework.

</details>


### [88] [Azimuthal decorrelation in diffractive dijet production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02230)
*Ding Yu Shao,Yu Shi,Cheng Zhang,Jian Zhou,Ya-jin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper computes azimuthal angular decorrelation (acoplanarity) of diffractive dijets in ultra-peripheral heavy-ion, $ep$, and $eA$ collisions to access non-perturbative diffractive transverse momentum-dependent distributions (TMDs). It resums soft gluon emissions to all orders for the transverse energy-energy correlator, includes initial and final state radiation, studies heavy-quark production, examines jet axis dependence, and provides numerical predictions for LHC UPCs, HERA, and EIC.
\n\n
本文计算了超外围重离子、$ep$ 和 $eA$ 碰撞中衍射双喷注的方位角去关联（非共面性），以探测非微扰的衍射横向动量依赖分布（TMDs）。文章对横向能量-能量关联观测量进行了软胶子辐射的全阶重求和，同时考虑初态和末态辐射，研究了重夸克对产生过程，分析了喷注轴定义的影响，并给出了LHC超外围碰撞（UPCs）、HERA和未来电子离子对撞机（EIC）相关运动学下的数值预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to probe non-perturbative diffractive transverse momentum-dependent distributions (TMDs), which encode 3D partonic structure information in diffractive processes. Azimuthal decorrelation (acoplanarity) of diffractive dijets is sensitive to intrinsic transverse momentum and soft radiation dynamics, offering a clean channel due to the presence of a rapidity gap and color-singlet exchange.
\n\n
动机在于探测非微扰的衍射横向动量依赖分布（TMDs），其编码了衍射过程中部分子的三维结构信息。衍射双喷注的方位角去关联（非共面性）对本征横向动量和软辐射动力学敏感，由于存在快度间隙和色单态交换，该过程提供了干净的探测通道。

Method: The authors perform an all-order resummation of soft gluon emissions for the transverse energy-energy correlator observable, incorporating both initial- and final-state radiation. They focus on the semi-inclusive diffractive channel with an unobserved semi-hard gluon. Heavy-quark pair production is analyzed separately, and sensitivity to jet axis definitions (e.g., standard vs. winner-take-all) is examined. Predictions are made using perturbative QCD matched with TMD factorization.
\n\n
作者对横向能量-能量关联观测量进行了软胶子辐射的全阶重求和，同时包含初态和末态辐射。他们聚焦于带有未观测半硬胶子的半包含衍射道。重夸克对产生被单独分析，并考察了喷注轴定义（如标准轴与winner-take-all轴）的敏感性。预言基于微扰QCD与TMD因子化方案的匹配。

Result: The calculation shows that acoplanarity in diffractive dijet production is highly sensitive to diffractive TMDs and soft radiation patterns. The decorrelation exhibits strong dependence on the jet axis choice, especially for heavy quarks. Numerical results are provided for kinematics relevant to LHC ultra-peripheral collisions (UPCs), HERA, and the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), demonstrating measurable effects.
\n\n
计算表明，衍射双喷注产生的非共面性对衍射TMDs和软辐射模式高度敏感。去关联效应强烈依赖于喷注轴的选择，尤其在重夸克情形下更为显著。文章给出了LHC超外围碰撞（UPCs）、HERA和未来电子离子对撞机（EIC）相关运动学下的数值结果，显示出可观测的效应。

Conclusion: Azimuthal angular decorrelation (acoplanarity) of diffractive dijets is a promising observable to extract diffractive transverse momentum-dependent distributions. The interplay between soft gluon resummation, jet algorithms, and TMD physics makes this channel a valuable tool for studying non-perturbative QCD dynamics in diffraction at current and future colliders.
\n\n
衍射双喷注的方位角去关联（非共面性）是提取衍射横向动量依赖分布（TMDs）的一个有前景的观测量。软胶子重求和、喷注算法与TMD物理之间的相互作用，使得该通道成为在当前及未来对撞机上研究衍射过程中非微扰QCD动力学的有力工具。

Abstract: We calculate the azimuthal angular decorrelation of diffractive dijets in ultra-peripheral heavy-ion, $ep$, and $eA$ collisions to probe non-perturbative diffractive transverse momentum-dependent distributions. Focusing on the dominant semi-inclusive channel with an unobserved semi-hard gluon, we perform an all-order resummation of soft gluon emissions for the transverse energy-energy correlator observable, accounting for both initial and final state radiation. We also analyze heavy-quark pair production and demonstrate the sensitivity of the decorrelation to the jet axis definition. Finally, we provide numerical predictions for relevant kinematics at LHC UPCs, HERA, and the future EIC. Our results demonstrate that the acoplanarity of diffractive dijet production could serve as a promising probe of diffractive transverse momentum-dependent distributions.

</details>


### [89] [Probing Pair Correlations in QCD Matter with Photon Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02414)
*Xingjian Lu,Shuzhe Shi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates how two-particle phase-space correlations in non-equilibrium QCD matter influence photon production in relativistic heavy-ion collisions. By decomposing the two-particle distribution into a factorized part and a correlation term $g_{ab}$, and using a spectral basis expansion, the authors compute leading-logarithmic photon spectra from $2\\to2$ quark--antiquark annihilation and Compton scattering. They show that angular modes of $g_{ab}$ induce sign-changing corrections comparable in size to the uncorrelated contribution, demonstrating that photon spectra—despite being single-particle observables—can probe early-time momentum correlations and hydrodynamization.

\n\n
本文研究了非平衡QCD物质中两粒子相空间关联如何影响相对论重离子碰撞中的光子产生。作者将双粒子分布分解为因子化部分与关联项 $g_{ab}$，并通过谱基展开方法计算了来自 $2\\to2$ 夸克-反夸克湮灭和康普顿散射道的领头对数光子谱。研究表明，$g_{ab}$ 的相对角度模会导致符号变化的修正，其幅度可与无关联贡献相当，表明光子谱（尽管是单粒子观测量）能够探测发射介质的动量关联，从而揭示早期流体化动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In relativistic heavy-ion collisions, the initial state is a dense, far-from-equilibrium QCD medium where standard kinetic theory assumptions (e.g., molecular chaos) may break down. Two-particle correlations in phase space are expected to be significant during this pre-hydrodynamic stage. Since photons—being weakly interacting—escape the medium unscathed, their spectrum encodes information about the early-time dynamics. The motivation is to quantify how these non-equilibrium pair correlations modify photon production beyond the usual factorized approximation, thereby establishing photons as probes of early-time quantum correlations and hydrodynamization.

\n\n
在相对论重离子碰撞中，初态是一个稠密且远离平衡的QCD介质，此时标准动力学理论假设（如分子混沌假设）可能失效。在此流体化前阶段，相空间中的两粒子关联预期显著。由于光子几乎不与介质相互作用，其能谱携带了早期动力学的信息。本文旨在量化这些非平衡对关联如何修正光子产额（超越通常的因子化近似），从而确立光子作为早期量子关联与流体化过程探针的地位。

Method: The authors decompose the two-particle phase-space distribution as $\\mathcal{F}_{ab} = f_a f_b + g_{ab}$, where $f_a$ is the single-particle distribution and $g_{ab}$ encodes genuine two-particle correlations. They focus on leading-order $2\\to2$ processes: quark--antiquark annihilation ($q\\bar{q}\\to g\\gamma$) and Compton scattering ($qg\\to q\\gamma$). To handle arbitrary anisotropic distributions, they expand both $f_a$ and $g_{ab}$ in a spectral basis (e.g., spherical harmonics for rotationally invariant systems). The photon production rate is computed at leading logarithmic accuracy, integrating over phase space with the full correlated distribution. The formalism accommodates general forms of $g_{ab}$, particularly its dependence on the relative angle between particle momenta.

\n\n
作者将双粒子相空间分布分解为 $\\mathcal{F}_{ab} = f_a f_b + g_{ab}$，其中 $f_a$ 为单粒子分布，$g_{ab}$ 编码真实的两粒子关联。他们聚焦于领头阶 $2\\to2$ 过程：夸克-反夸克湮灭（$q\\bar{q}\\to g\\gamma$）和康普顿散射（$qg\\to q\\gamma$）。为处理任意各向异性分布，他们将 $f_a$ 和 $g_{ab}$ 在谱基（例如旋转不变系统中的球谐函数）中展开。在领头对数精度下计算光子产生率，对包含完整关联分布的相空间进行积分。该形式体系可容纳 $g_{ab}$ 的一般形式，特别是其对粒子动量间相对角度的依赖。

Result: For a rotationally invariant medium, the pair correlation $g_{ab}$ is expanded in relative-angle modes. The resulting correction to the photon spectrum exhibits sign changes across different photon energies and can reach magnitudes comparable to the uncorrelated ($f_a f_b$) contribution. This demonstrates that even though photons are single-particle observables, their spectrum is sensitive to two-particle correlations in the emitting medium. The effect is most pronounced when $g_{ab}$ has non-trivial angular structure, indicating that photon measurements can constrain early-time momentum correlations and inform models of pre-hydrodynamic evolution.

\n\n
对于旋转不变介质，关联项 $g_{ab}$ 按相对角度模展开。由此产生的光子谱修正随光子能量改变符号，且幅度可与无关联（$f_a f_b$）贡献相当。这表明，尽管光子是单粒子观测量，其能谱仍对发射介质中的两粒子关联敏感。当 $g_{ab}$ 具有非平凡角度结构时，效应最为显著，说明光子测量可约束早期动量关联，并为流体化前演化模型提供信息。

Conclusion: Photon production in the early stages of heavy-ion collisions is not solely determined by single-particle phase-space distributions but is significantly modified by two-particle correlations. The leading-logarithmic calculation shows that angular modes of the pair correlation $g_{ab}$ induce observable, sign-changing distortions in the photon spectrum. Consequently, electromagnetic probes like photons serve as direct diagnostics of non-equilibrium momentum correlations, offering a window into the hydrodynamization process that precedes the applicability of viscous hydrodynamics.

\n\n
重离子碰撞早期阶段的光子产生不仅由单粒子相空间分布决定，还受到两粒子关联的显著修正。领头对数计算表明，关联项 $g_{ab}$ 的角度模会在光子谱中引入可观测的、符号变化的畸变。因此，光子等电磁探针可直接诊断非平衡动量关联，为粘滞流体力学适用之前的流体化过程提供观测窗口。

Abstract: Correlations in the phase-space distribution of partons play an important role in the initial stage of relativistic heavy-ion collisions, where the matter is dense and far from equilibrium. Photons produced in the hot medium, which predominantly originate from two-parton initial states, are sensitive to two-particle correlations in the phase-space distribution. In this work, we study how pair correlations in non-equilibrium QCD matter affect in-medium photon production. We decompose the two-particle distribution as $\mathcal F_{ab}=f_a f_b+g_{ab}$, where $g_{ab}$ is the pair correlation. Focusing on the $2\to2$ quark--antiquark annihilation and Compton channels, we compute the leading-logarithmic photon spectrum by expanding the single-particle distribution and pair correlation in a spectral basis, thereby accommodating a broad class of two-particle distributions. For a rotationally invariant medium, we find that relative-angle modes of the pair correlation generate sign-changing modifications to the photon spectrum, with magnitudes that can be comparable to the factorized contribution. Thus, photon spectra, although single-particle observables, can measure the momentum correlations of the emitting medium and therefore probe the early-time hydrodynamization.

</details>


### [90] [On the spanning cuts consistency problem in the IBP reductions of Feynman integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02485)
*Zihao Wu,Yingxuan Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper identifies the origin of inconsistencies in the spanning cuts method for IBP reduction of Feynman integrals: hidden terms proportional to vanishing Feynman prescription parameters ($i0^+$) become finite due to pinch singularities introduced by cuts, and omitting them leads to violations of IBP relations. The authors propose an algorithm to detect linear hidden relations among propagators responsible for these effects.
\n\n
本文揭示了在使用 spanning cuts 方法进行 Feynman 积分的 IBP 约化时出现不一致性的根源：与“消失”的 Feynman prescription 参数（$i0^+$）成比例的隐藏项，在切割引入 pinch singularities 后变为有限量，若忽略这些项会导致 IBP 关系被破坏。作者提出了一个算法来探测导致此类效应的传播子之间的线性 hidden relations。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The spanning cuts method accelerates Integration-By-Parts (IBP) reduction by working on unitarity cuts, but suffers from a consistency problem—different cuts yield incompatible reduction coefficients. The underlying cause was unknown. This paper aims to explain why IBP identities fail under cuts and to resolve the inconsistency by identifying overlooked contributions.
\n\n
spanning cuts 方法通过在 unitarity cuts 上进行操作来加速 Integration-By-Parts (IBP) 约化，但存在一致性问题——不同切割给出的约化系数相互矛盾。其根本原因此前未知。本文旨在解释为何 IBP 恒等式在切割下失效，并通过识别被忽略的贡献来解决这一不一致性。

Method: The authors analyze IBP relations under unitarity cuts and show that terms proportional to the Feynman prescription $i0^+$—usually discarded as vanishing—can become finite when the cut induces a pinch singularity that cancels the $i0^+$ regulator. They identify such "hidden terms" as the source of inconsistency. To systematically address this, they develop an algorithm to find linear hidden relations among inverse propagators that signal the presence of such singular configurations.
\n\n
作者分析了 unitarity cuts 下的 IBP 关系，指出通常被视为消失而被丢弃的、与 Feynman prescription $i0^+$ 成比例的项，在切割引入 pinch singularity 从而抵消 $i0^+$ 调节因子时会变为有限值。他们将此类“隐藏项”确定为不一致性的来源。为系统处理此问题，他们开发了一个算法，用于寻找反传播子之间的线性 hidden relations，这些关系预示着此类奇异构型的存在。

Result: The paper demonstrates that neglecting finite hidden terms arising from pinch singularities under cuts violates IBP identities. It provides a concrete mechanism for the consistency problem and implements an algorithm that detects linear dependencies among propagators—termed "hidden relations"—which are responsible for generating these singularities and finite hidden contributions.
\n\n
本文证明了在切割下因忽略由 pinch singularities 产生的有限隐藏项会导致 IBP 恒等式被破坏。文章给出了 consistency problem 的具体机制，并实现了一个算法，用于探测传播子之间的线性依赖关系（称为“hidden relations”），这些关系正是产生此类奇点和有限隐藏贡献的原因。

Conclusion: The inconsistency in the spanning cuts method stems from improperly discarding terms that become finite due to pinch singularities induced by the cuts. By recognizing and computing these hidden contributions via detection of linear hidden relations among propagators, the method can be made consistent. The proposed algorithm enables systematic identification of such problematic configurations.
\n\n
spanning cuts 方法中的不一致性源于错误地丢弃了因切割诱导的 pinch singularities 而变为有限的项。通过识别传播子间的线性 hidden relations 来发现并计算这些隐藏贡献，可使该方法恢复一致性。所提出的算法能够系统地识别此类问题构型。

Abstract: The spanning cuts method is a powerful approach to reduce the cost of IBP reduction while computing Feynman integrals. However, its usage is limited due to the so-called consistency problem. It was unclear why the IBP reduction coefficients can be inconsistent with each other between different cuts. In this paper, we report a mechanism behind this inconsistency. We found that the IBP relations can be violated under the cuts, if we blindly erase the hidden terms that are proportional to the ``vanishing'' Feynman prescription parameters in the relations. In some cases, the cut introduces pinch singularities, which cancel the vanishing Feynman prescription parameters, making the hidden terms finite. In various cases, the error comes from omitting such finite hidden terms. We also claimed that the pinch singularity under the cuts are related to some hidden relations between the propagators. In this paper, we provide an algorithm and its implementation to find the linear hidden relations.

</details>


### [91] [Comment on "QCD-factorization amplitudes from flavour symmetries: beyond the $SU(3)$ symmetric case''](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02538)
*Bhubanjyoti Bhattacharya,David London*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This Comment addresses a disagreement between two analyses of $B \to PP$ decays: one claims Electroweak Penguin–Tree Relations (ETRs) are invalid, while the authors argue that ETRs are exact group-theoretical consequences of unbroken SU(3) flavor symmetry (neglecting small Wilson coefficients $c_{7,8}$), and thus mathematically rigorous without reliance on hadronic dynamics. They also critique weaknesses in the opposing formalism.
\n\n
本评论针对两项关于 $B \\to PP$ 衰变分析之间的分歧：一方声称电弱企鹅-树图关系（ETRs）无效，而作者则论证ETRs是在忽略小Wilson系数 $c_{7,8}$ 且SU(3)味对称性未破缺的前提下，由群论严格导出的精确关系，因此不依赖强子动力学计算，具有数学严谨性。作者还指出了对方形式体系中的若干缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from a recent conflicting result in the literature: a fit to $B \to PP$ decays using topological amplitudes combined with QCD factorization claimed that Electroweak Penguin–Tree Relations (ETRs)—long-standing group-theoretic constraints derived under SU(3) flavor symmetry—are invalid. This challenges decades of theoretical work. The authors aim to clarify that ETRs are exact consequences of SU(3) symmetry when small electroweak penguin contributions ($c_7, c_8$) are neglected, and thus their use is theoretically sound.
\n\n
动机源于近期文献中的一项矛盾结果：一项结合拓扑振幅与QCD因子化的 $B \\to PP$ 衰变拟合声称，长期以来基于SU(3)味对称性导出的电弱企鹅-树图关系（ETRs）——这些是群论约束——是无效的。这挑战了数十年来的理论工作。作者旨在澄清：当忽略小的电弱企鹅项贡献（$c_7, c_8$）时，ETRs 是SU(3)对称性的精确推论，因此其使用在理论上是可靠的。

Method: The authors invoke group-theoretical arguments within the framework of exact SU(3) flavor symmetry. They emphasize that Electroweak Penguin–Tree Relations (ETRs) arise purely from the structure of the weak effective Hamiltonian under SU(3), specifically when the small Wilson coefficients $c_7$ and $c_8$ (associated with electroweak penguin operators) are neglected. Under these conditions, the reduced matrix elements of different topological diagrams obey exact linear relations—ETRs—without any input from nonperturbative QCD or hadronic modeling. The method is thus analytical and symmetry-based, not phenomenological.
\n\n
作者在精确SU(3)味对称性框架下运用群论论证。他们强调，电弱企鹅-树图关系（ETRs）纯粹源于弱有效哈密顿量在SU(3)下的结构，特别是当忽略小的Wilson系数 $c_7$ 和 $c_8$（对应电弱企鹅算符）时。在此条件下，不同拓扑图的约化矩阵元满足精确的线性关系——即ETRs——无需任何非微扰QCD或强子模型输入。因此，该方法是解析的、基于对称性的，而非唯象的。

Result: The key result is the reaffirmation that Electroweak Penguin–Tree Relations (ETRs) are exact and mathematically rigorous under unbroken SU(3) flavor symmetry when $c_7, c_8 \\approx 0$. This contradicts the claim in arXiv:2604.19612 that ETRs are invalid. The authors demonstrate that ETRs follow automatically from group theory, independent of hadronic uncertainties. Additionally, they identify conceptual and technical weaknesses in the alternative formalism that discards ETRs.
\n\n
核心结果是重申：在SU(3)味对称性未破缺且 $c_7, c_8 \\approx 0$ 的条件下，电弱企鹅-树图关系（ETRs）是精确且数学上严格的。这与arXiv:2604.19612中“ETRs无效”的主张相矛盾。作者证明ETRs自动由群论导出，与强子不确定性无关。此外，他们还指出了抛弃ETRs的替代表述中存在的概念性和技术性缺陷。

Conclusion: The Comment concludes that Electroweak Penguin–Tree Relations (ETRs) are valid as exact group-theoretical identities under the stated assumptions (exact SU(3) and negligible $c_{7,8}$), and therefore analyses employing them are theoretically well-founded. Dismissing ETRs as “invalid” is incorrect and overlooks their algebraic origin in flavor symmetry. Future analyses of $B \\to PP$ decays should respect these symmetry constraints unless explicit SU(3)-breaking effects or $c_{7,8}$ contributions are included.
\n\n
本评论得出结论：在所述假设（精确SU(3)对称性及可忽略的 $c_{7,8}$）下，电弱企鹅-树图关系（ETRs）作为群论恒等式是有效的，因此采用ETRs的分析在理论上是合理的。“ETRs无效”的说法是错误的，忽视了其在味对称性中的代数起源。未来对 $B \\to PP$ 衰变的分析应尊重这些对称性约束，除非明确包含SU(3)破缺效应或 $c_{7,8}$ 贡献。

Abstract: Recently, a fit to $B \to PP$ decays ($P \in \{π, K, η, η'\}$) was performed (arXiv:2604.19612, "QCD-factorization amplitudes from flavour symmetries: beyond the $SU(3)$ symmetric case''}) using a formalism that combines topological diagrams with QCD factorization, and a good fit was found. We also recently performed such a fit, under the assumption that the $B \to PP$ amplitudes are related by flavour SU(3) symmetry, but we found a very poor fit. The two results therefore disagree with one another. The source of this disagreement is that we applied EWP-tree relations (ETRs). These were derived $\sim 30$ years ago, and relate different topological diagrams or reduced matrix elements, thus reducing the number of unknown parameters in the fit. In their paper, it is asserted that ETRs are invalid, so that analyses that use them are unreliable. We are writing this Comment to explain why this assertion is incorrect. The key point is that ETRs are mathematically rigorous, group theoretically. If SU(3) is unbroken, and the small Wilson coefficients $c_{7,8}$ in the weak effective Hamiltonian are neglected, ETRs follow automatically and are exact. That is, this is a group theory result -- no hadronic calculations are involved. In this Comment, we also point out several weaknesses of their formalism.

</details>


### [92] [New Windows on Heavy Dark Matter: Mineral Melt Modelling and X-Ray Readout for Muscovite Mica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02579)
*Yilda Boukhtouchen,Joseph Bramante,Andrew Buchanan,Alexander Hayes,Matthew Leybourne,Jennika McIntosh,Anupam Ray,Aaron Shugar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper proposes muscovite mica as a paleodetector for heavy composite dark matter, modeling track formation via a Sedov-Taylor thermal spike framework validated with SRIM/TRIM simulations, introduces a novel X-ray fluorescence readout technique with copper backing for micron-scale damage detection, and re-evaluates prior exclusion limits from etched mica experiments.
\n\n
本文提出将白云母（muscovite mica）作为一种用于探测重复合暗物质（heavy composite dark matter）的古探测器（paleodetector），利用Sedov-Taylor热峰模型描述径迹形成过程，并通过SRIM/TRIM模拟验证亚微米尺度；同时引入一种基于铜衬底对比的快速X射线荧光成像新读出方法，用于在宏观扫描区域内识别微米级损伤特征，并重新评估了以往蚀刻云母实验所得的暗物质排除限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muscovite mica possesses unique properties—such as natural translucency, perfect basal cleavage, extremely low intrinsic radioactivity, billion-year exposure times, and proven retention of radiation damage tracks over geological epochs—that make it ideal for detecting rare, high-energy depositions from heavy composite dark matter candidates. Existing paleodetector proposals lack a consistent microphysical model for track formation and robust large-area readout strategies; this work addresses both gaps.
\n\n
白云母具有独特的物理特性——包括天然半透明性、完美的基底解理、极低的本征放射性、长达十亿年的暴露时间，以及在地质时间尺度上保留辐射损伤径迹的能力——使其成为探测重复合暗物质候选者所引发的稀有高能沉积事件的理想材料。现有的古探测器方案缺乏对径迹形成的微观物理模型以及稳健的大面积读出策略；本文旨在填补这两项空白。

Method: The authors model energy deposition from transiting heavy composite dark matter using a Sedov-Taylor blast wave formalism to describe the thermal spike that leads to melt track formation in mica. This is cross-checked against SRIM/TRIM simulations of nuclear recoil cascades to calibrate phonon efficiency and validate the sub-micron damage regime. For readout, they develop a rapid X-ray fluorescence (XRF) mapping technique enhanced by a copper backing layer to provide contrast for micron-scale defect identification. Laser-ablated regions serve as calibration standards for minimum detectable feature size. Sensitivity projections are computed for both opaque and diffuse composite dark matter models, including a sub-melt “hole-channel” detection channel for strongly attenuated large composites.
\n\n
作者采用Sedov-Taylor爆轰波形式描述重复合暗物质穿越云母时产生的热峰，进而建模熔融径迹的形成；并通过SRIM/TRIM模拟核反冲级联过程来校准声子效率（phonon efficiency），验证亚微米损伤区域的模型。在读出方面，他们开发了一种快速X射线荧光（XRF）成像技术，并引入铜衬底层以增强微米级缺陷的对比度。激光烧蚀区域被用作最小可探测特征尺寸的校准标准。文中还针对不透明型与弥散型复合暗物质模型计算了灵敏度投影，包括针对被覆盖层显著衰减的大质量复合体的亚熔融“孔道”（hole-channel）探测模式。

Result: The framework successfully links dark matter mass and cross section to observable track morphologies in mica. The XRF readout achieves micron-scale spatial resolution over cm$^2$-scale areas, enabling efficient scanning. Projected sensitivities surpass existing limits for composite dark matter with masses $\\gtrsim 10^{10}$ GeV and nuclear cross sections above $\\sim 10^{-28}$ cm$^2$. The analysis also reveals that previous exclusions based on etched mica samples relied on unverified assumptions about track etchability and background discrimination, undermining their reliability.
\n\n
该框架成功将暗物质质量与截面关联到云母中可观测的径迹形貌。XRF读出技术在平方厘米量级的扫描面积上实现了微米级空间分辨率，支持高效扫描。针对质量$\\gtrsim 10^{10}$ GeV、核截面高于$\\sim 10^{-28}$ cm$^2$的复合暗物质，其投影灵敏度超越现有限制。分析还指出，以往基于蚀刻云母样本的排除限依赖于未经验证的径迹可蚀刻性与本底甄别假设，削弱了其可靠性。

Conclusion: Muscovite mica, when combined with modern readout techniques and a physically grounded track formation model, offers a powerful and scalable paleodetector platform for probing heavy composite dark matter beyond current accelerator and direct detection reach. Reinterpretation of historical mica data requires caution due to methodological limitations, but future dedicated experiments using this framework could explore uncharted parameter space.
\n\n
结合现代读出技术与基于物理的径迹形成模型，白云母为探测超出当前加速器与直接探测能力范围的重复合暗物质提供了一个强大且可扩展的古探测器平台。由于方法学局限，对历史云母数据的重新解释需谨慎，但未来基于此框架的专用实验有望探索尚未触及的参数空间。

Abstract: Muscovite mica is a translucent, layered silicate mineral whose basal cleavage, low radiogenic background, gigayear exposures, and demonstrated track retention over geological timescales make it a compelling target for rare particle searches. In this work, we develop a new framework for detecting heavy composite dark matter using muscovite mica as a paleodetector. We model melt track formation by heavy composite dark matter transiting through mica using a Sedov-Taylor thermal spike formalism, and validate the sub-micron regime with SRIM/TRIM simulations of nuclear recoil cascades, which also calibrate the phonon efficiency governing local energy deposition. We demonstrate a novel readout method using rapid X-ray fluorescence mapping with a copper backing contrast technique, capable of identifying micron-scale damage features in cleaved mica sheets over macroscopic scan areas, and calibrate the minimum detectable track size using laser-ablated defect regions. We present projected sensitivities for opaque and diffuse composite dark matter, including a sub-melt hole-channel detection mode for large composites substantially attenuated by overburden. We also revisit prior dark matter exclusions from etched mica searches, identifying shortcomings that compromise the robustness of these constraints.

</details>
